### Backend notes
- Pipeline order feeding this page, earliest first (America/New_York): `fetch-fred-rates` 06:30 daily (`daily_rates`) → `fetch-av-options-realtime` every 5 min from 09:00 to 15:55 Mon-Fri (`etf_options_snapshots`, REALTIME, SPY IWM QQQ) → `fetch-earnings-history` 19:15 Mon-Fri and Sun (`market_data_daily`) → `fetch-alphavantage-intraday` 21:00 Mon-Sat and `fetch-av-options-backfill` 21:00 Mon-Fri (`market_data_intraday`, `etf_options_snapshots` EOD) → `fetch-market-data` 23:00 Mon-Fri (`market_data_intraday`, `market_data_daily`) → `historical-signals-watchlist` 01:00 Tue-Sat (`historical_signals`, for the previous session) → `etf-options-retention` 02:00 daily. On the 1st, `fetch-av-options-backfill` also runs at 05:00 and `fetch-alphavantage-intraday` at 21:00. Nothing scheduled produces the backtester's CSVs (Gaps).
- Freshness the page assumes: `useMarketData` and `useReferenceLevels` never go stale (`staleTime: Infinity`) · the dates list five minutes · journal trades 10 s · indicators and signal-series 10 s · similar setups 60 s · the backtester 30 s · gamma levels one hour with `retry: false` · market hours 24 h ([useConfig.ts:68-78](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useConfig.ts#L68)) · any other failed query is retried once ([App.tsx:30-36](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/App.tsx#L30)). None of these hooks sets a `refetchInterval`, so the page asks again only on mount, window focus or reconnect once stale, or when a key changes (a replay step changes the indicators key). Server-side, the dates list is cached one hour behind a `MAX(ts)` probe (`platform/api/main.py:578-582`), backtest results and equity one hour and the run list ten minutes (`platform/api/routers/backtest.py:116-118`), and the chain behind `/levels` twelve hours (`platform/api/routers/options.py:90`).
- Three voters feed three elements, and they are different computations ([#912](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/912)): the Sig overlay and the similar card's trigger use the mean-reversion voter in `lib/signals.py` (`generate_signals`, called without a ticker, so no override read), the Strategy conditions card uses `lib/chart_voter.py`, and the `historical_signals` rows the similar card counts come from the momentum voter in `lib/trading_analysis.py`, the `--strategy` default at `scripts/run_historical_signals.py:105`.
- The scorecard handler reads more than 04 lists. Bars come through `_load_date_data` (`platform/api/routers/backtest.py:482-483`, the same Cloud SQL then GCS path as market data), overrides through `lib/backtest.py:848-850` into `lib/strategies/exit_config_overrides.py`, whose exit-target getters fall back to Tier-B defaults (lines 155-167), and configuration through `load_config` (`lib/config.py:704`), which reads `alert_config.json` from the working directory and returns dataclass defaults when it is absent. `platform/Dockerfile` copies only `lib/`, `gcp/`, `scripts/` and `platform/api/` (lines 40-45), so the service should be running the benchmark on those defaults, while the jobs image does copy the file (`gcp/Dockerfile:23`). Not checked against a running container.
- `PATCH /api/journal/trades/{trade_id}` is wired on this page (`useCloseChartTrade` behind `onTradeExited`), but nothing here triggers it: `startExitMode` is called only from [JournalPage.tsx:627](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L627), as the comment at [ChartsPage.tsx:218-221](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L218) says. No row owns it.
- Writers left out, with the lines that exclude them: `scripts/backfill_journal_embeddings.py` updates only `journal_entries.embedding` (lines 79-80), which neither the GET (`platform/api/routers/journal.py:892-901`) nor the replay query selects · `scripts/backfill_timeframe_tags.py` updates only `timeframe_tag` and `expected_hold_min` on `historical_signals` (lines 172-174) · `compute-spx-greeks-backfill` updates only the `*_computed` columns of `etf_options_snapshots` (`scripts/maintenance/compute_spx_greeks.py:149-155`), which the chain query behind `/levels` does not select (`platform/api/routers/options.py:562-579`), so the sidecar that exists for chains where the vendor returns no Greeks (`gcp/schema.sql:223-232`), as SPX's newest chain is, never reaches the `Gamma` overlay (Gaps, SPX). The `market_data_daily` exclusions are the ones in Live's Backend notes.
- Every route this page calls is gated. Staging answers 401 to each of the fourteen requests without a token, `GET /api/config/market-hours` included ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5919900514)), and none of them is among the backend's open paths (`_OPEN_API_EXACT` and `_OPEN_API_PREFIXES`, [auth.py:69-70](../../platform/api/auth.py#L69), matched by `_path_requires_auth`, [auth.py:180-185](../../platform/api/auth.py#L180)). The answer is `sign in to continue` for no token and `invalid or expired sign-in` for a bad one ([auth.py:205-208](../../platform/api/auth.py#L205)), and the page turns neither into a sign-in state on the chart card (Gaps).
- Producer tests beyond Live's: `historical-signals-watchlist` [test_historical_signals.py](../../tests/gcp/test_historical_signals.py), [test_run_historical_signals_args.py](../../tests/scripts/test_run_historical_signals_args.py), [test_run_historical_signals_provenance.py](../../tests/scripts/test_run_historical_signals_provenance.py), [test_run_historical_signals_strategy.py](../../tests/scripts/test_run_historical_signals_strategy.py) · options fetchers [test_fetch_av_historical_options.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_av_historical_options.py), [test_fetch_av_realtime_options.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_av_realtime_options.py) · `etf-options-retention` [test_options_retention_job.py](../../tests/gcp/test_options_retention_job.py) · `param-sweep` [test_param_sweep_persistence.py](../../tests/integration/test_param_sweep_persistence.py) (integration, outside `make test`) · `fetch-fred-rates`: no test found.

### Gaps
- [#912](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/912) (open) is this page's blocking issue in UI-SCREENS.md: duplicate indicator implementations behind one name. The similar card is where it shows. It asks for rows matching a mean-reversion fire's direction and score, and the query has no `strategy` predicate ([signals.py:354-356](../../platform/api/routers/signals.py#L354)). A read-only query on 2026-09-28 (`scripts/db_query_cr.sh`, execution `db-query-l4hpx`) found every `historical_signals` row labelled `momentum`, with `signal_strength` 3 to 7, and strengths 3 and 4 ending on 2026-05-04. So the card reports the other voter's outcomes, and a score-3 or score-4 fire is matched only against rows from 2026-05-04 or earlier. On 2026-09-30 the handler's own predicate matched 242 IWM rows for a CALL of score 4 at RSI 35 and 14,725 for a PUT of score 3 at RSI 60, none newer than 2026-05-01, while score 5 reaches 2026-09-29 ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5919900514), statements 9 and 10).
- **Nothing writes the backtester's objects.** The only code naming the prefix reads it (`platform/api/routers/backtest.py:102`). `scripts/run_backtest.py` writes CSVs to the local `data/backtest_results` directory (`lib/config.py:166`), and `upload_raw_parquets` (`gcp/migrate_to_gcp.py:79-91`) uploads `*.parquet` only. A read-only listing on 2026-09-28 found 111 objects (53.41 MiB). The 90 `backtest_*` and `equity_*` CSVs (IWM 17, QQQ 11, SPY 12, SPX 5 runs) were all uploaded between 2026-02-23T01:36:50Z and 01:36:54Z, and the newest object is from 2026-02-23T10:40:04Z (a listing read again on 2026-09-30 is identical, [V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5919900514)). 04 gives `via lib/` for all three routes, which read GCS through `platform/api/gcs_reader.py` and call no `lib/` module.
- **A GCS outage reads as no results.** `list_matching_blobs` returns `[]` on any storage error (`platform/api/gcs_reader.py:75-95`), and a hermetic probe with the client forced to fail got a 404 `No backtest results found in GCS for ticker 'IWM'`. The page then shows its not-found advice for every error ([BacktesterSection.tsx:411-417](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/backtest/BacktesterSection.tsx#L411)). The response's `trades` rows are free-form (`list[dict[str, Any]]`, `platform/api/schemas.py:1140`), so the contract check validates them trivially.
- **The replay scorecard's failure mode contradicts its docstring.** The docstring promises a 500 on any Cloud SQL error ([backtest.py:516-518](../../platform/api/routers/backtest.py#L516)), but `_replay_journal_query` forwards to the swallowing `query_to_dataframe` ([backtest.py:486-490](../../platform/api/routers/backtest.py#L486)), and a hermetic probe with the connection forced to fail got 404 `no matching trades found`. `platform/api/routers/journal.py` moved its own `_journal_query` to the strict helper for this reason (`platform/api/routers/journal.py:116-122`). 04 lists only `journal_entries` for this route, which also reads `market_data_intraday` and `exit_config_overrides` (Backend notes).
- **The scorecard needs a trade that Charts cannot close.** Mark Entry posts no exit ([useJournalChartTrades.ts:390-415](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useJournalChartTrades.ts#L390)), which creates an ACTIVE trade (`test_create_active_trade_without_exit_returns_null_return_pct` in [test_journal_phase2.py](../../tests/api/test_journal_phase2.py) passed on 2026-09-28), and nothing on this page closes one (Backend notes). Stop scores only closed session trades ([ChartsPage.tsx:238-252](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L238)), so from this page alone it ends with `Session ended: no closed trades to score`. The design seed's `Backtest my trades` button and journey no longer exist ([ChartsPage.tsx:226-229](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L226)).
- **The chart's own loading and error branches cannot render.** `WidgetState` shows its skeleton, `SignInEmptyState` or `WidgetError` before its children ([WidgetState.tsx](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/shared/WidgetState.tsx)), and a hermetic render on 2026-09-28 confirmed an erroring query never reaches the children. So `No market data available for this date` and `Markets may be closed` show only for a zero-bar day ([ChartsPage.tsx:765-770](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L765)), not for the error branch at [ChartsPage.tsx:722-733](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L722). The real handler never answers a 200 with no bars either (an empty day is a 404, [main.py:927-928](../../platform/api/main.py#L927)), so in production the two-line empty text is unreachable and a day with no rows reads `Couldn't load this data` with `No data found in Cloud SQL or GCS for iwm date=20260425` (executed 2026-09-30 through the real handler).
- The Strategy conditions card substitutes `EMPTY_CHART_VOTER` for a missing response, error included ([ChartsPage.tsx:393](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L393), `isError` is never read), while the comment at [ChartsPage.tsx:56-59](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L56) says the empty voter shows only before the query resolves; executed 2026-09-30, a held, a failing and a 401 indicators request all left `No setup` with `0/5` in both columns, the same as a real answer of no setup.
- Silent fallbacks on this page's path: the `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker at [ChartsPage.tsx:364](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L364) (candle volume `?? 0` sent to indicators and signal-series) · `useReferenceLevels` returns `null` on any non-OK response ([useMarketData.ts:87](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useMarketData.ts#L87)), so a failed reference call just hides the Prev lines · `data: trades = []` at [ChartsPage.tsx:222](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L222), so a failed journal read draws no trade and says nothing (executed 2026-09-30 with a 500) · `/levels` reads the chain with the swallowing `query_to_dataframe` ([options.py:580](../../platform/api/routers/options.py#L580) and [:591](../../platform/api/routers/options.py#L591)), which returns an empty frame on any error ([database.py:218-241](../../gcp/database.py#L218)), so a Cloud SQL outage answers 404 `No AlphaVantage options data for IWM on 2026-09-04. No earlier data ingested for this ticker.`, which reads as a ticker that was never ingested, and the page, which reads `data` only, draws nothing and says nothing ([test_route_coverage.py:249](../../tests/api/test_route_coverage.py#L249) pins that 404 against a dead backend; executed 2026-09-30 through the real handler with the connection refused, where the dates route answers 503 `Could not read trading dates for IWM: the database is unavailable.`) · market data's unmarked GCS fallback and `0` volume, recorded in Live's Gaps.
- The comment at [ChartsPage.tsx:801-803](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L801) says the similar card posts to `/api/live/similar-setups`. No such route exists in the snapshot: the card sends a GET to `/api/signals/{ticker}/similar` ([useSimilarSetups.ts:75](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useSimilarSetups.ts#L75)).
- `Gamma toggle is hidden for non-ETF tickers` asserts only that the toggle is visible for IWM, and says it passes vacuously ([gamma-levels.spec.ts:132-141](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/shared/gamma-levels.spec.ts#L132)). The hidden case is untested on main; `the overlay toggles flip their state, Gamma asks for the levels only once it is on, and it is absent for a non-ETF ticker` asserts it (solyra commit cd2c08b, added on this branch).
- **The `RTH` toggle filters only what the canvas draws**, so the Strategy conditions card and the similar card's trigger describe the last bar of the extended day while the chart, with `RTH` on, draws only bars opening before 16:00 ([ChartsPage.tsx:356-393](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L356); executed 2026-09-30: 192 five minute bars from 04:00 to 19:55 reached the indicators request as 192 bars ending 19:55, and switching `RTH` off sent no new request).
- **On the `1h` timeframe the `RTH` filter drops the bar that opens at 09:00 and holds the session's first half hour**, because it keeps bars by open time in `[09:30, 16:00)` ([CandlestickChart.tsx:279-282](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/charts/CandlestickChart.tsx#L279)), so the chart starts at 10:00 (executed 2026-09-30 with hourly bars from 04:00 to 20:00).
- **In review mode the last bar runs past the cutoff on every timeframe above 1m**, because `end_time` is applied after the resample and keeps bars by open time ([main.py:981-987](../../platform/api/main.py#L981)): through the real handler with a stubbed query, a cutoff of 09:45 on `5m` returned a bar closing at 09:49 (103.492 against 103.452 for the 1 minute bar at the cutoff) and a cutoff of 10:30 on `1h` returned the 10:00 bar through 10:59 (104.192 against 103.902) (executed 2026-09-30).
- **Every replay step posts all the bars revealed so far to `POST /api/live/indicators` with no debounce**: a 20x playback of a 120 bar day sent 105 requests in 5.5 s (executed 2026-09-30 on one hermetic page, the answer mocked), and the real handler took a median of 13 to 31 ms for 30 to 961 bars in process ([useLiveIndicators.ts:26-50](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useLiveIndicators.ts#L26), `TestClient`).
- **A replay session is not tied to its day or timeframe**: a session at 40 of 78 five minute bars switched to `15m` read `40/26` with all 26 bars drawn, and a date change kept the count for the new day's bars ([useReplaySession.ts:135-195](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useReplaySession.ts#L135), executed 2026-09-30).
- **A failed Mark Entry post is silent**: `onTradeCreated` calls `mutate` with no error handler ([ChartsPage.tsx:750](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L750)) and nothing reads the mutation's state, so a 503 drops the trade and the toolbar returns to `Mark Entry` as after a success (executed 2026-09-30: no alert, no message).
- **A review date older than every listed date shows the oldest listed day with no `Snapped to` note**, because `snappedFromReview` stays false when no earlier listed date exists ([ChartsPage.tsx:136-150](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L136), executed 2026-09-30: a review date of 2026-04-20 showed 2026-04-22).
- **The `Sig` button is titled `Production alert signals (lib/signals mean-reversion voter)`, but the overlay is evaluated without the per-ticker overrides**: `POST /api/live/signal-series` calls `generate_signals(df)` with no ticker ([live.py:610-669](../../platform/api/routers/live.py#L610)) while `gcp/signal_monitor.py` passes one ([signal_monitor.py:1107-1113](../../gcp/signal_monitor.py#L1107)), and production holds overrides for IWM and QQQ dated 2026-05-08 that disable conditions and, for QQQ, the PUT side ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5919900514), dispatch C statement 1), so the overlay can draw a fire that the signal monitor would not fire (read directly; no overlay was compared with a production alert).
- **A 404 on the `Gamma` request draws nothing and says nothing**: the page reads `data` only ([ChartsPage.tsx:203-207](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L203)), so a date with no stored chain leaves the toggle lit over an unchanged chart (executed 2026-09-30).
- **SPX is offered on this page where it cannot draw a day correctly, and nothing says so.** `GAMMA_LEVELS_TICKERS` includes SPX, so the `Gamma` button renders for it ([ChartsPage.tsx:54,586](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L54)), and SPX reaches the ticker store from the combobox, which passes AlphaVantage `SYMBOL_SEARCH` through with no filter ([TickerCombobox.tsx:259-272](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/shared/TickerCombobox.tsx#L259), [insights.py:544-556](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L544), [ticker_info.py:403-446](../../lib/ticker_info.py#L403); a search for `SPX` returns `SPX`, `S&P 500 INDEX`, first, read through the AlphaVantage connector on 2026-09-30). Cloud SQL holds no SPX bars: a read-only dispatch on 2026-09-30 found no row for `SPX`, `^SPX` or `SPXW` in `market_data_intraday` or `market_data_daily` (`scripts/db_query_cr.sh`, execution `db-query-gv8xf`; execution `db-query-8wl4r`, read from `query-results/` on 2026-09-30, gave the same answer), so the dates list, which reads Cloud SQL only, is a 200 with empty lists ([main.py:820-825](../../platform/api/main.py#L820), executed 2026-09-30 through the real handler), no date is selected, `gammaLevelsEnabled` stays false ([ChartsPage.tsx:203-204](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L203)) and the chart card reads `Select a date to load chart data`, while a lit `Gamma` toggle sends no request and says nothing (executed 2026-09-30 on a hermetic page with SPX chosen in the combobox). A date typed into the input, which has no `min` or `max` for an empty list, is requested all the same, and the loader then falls to the legacy GCS files ([main.py:1670-1696](../../platform/api/main.py#L1670)), which hold SPX minute files for 76 sessions from 2025-09-02 to 2025-12-17 and a year file, `spx_2025.parquet`, of 81 daily rows from 2025-08-25 to 2025-12-17 (the prefix `raw/data/spx/`, read 2026-09-30). A minute-file date such as 2025-12-15 answers 200 with 78 five minute bars and no label. The five dates 2025-08-25 to 2025-08-29, which only the year file holds, answer 200 with one bar, the day's daily open, high, low and close at 00:00, again with no label, because the loader reads the year file when the minute and month files are absent ([main.py:1691-1694](../../platform/api/main.py#L1691)) and the handler cuts it to the requested date ([main.py:968-978](../../platform/api/main.py#L968)). Any other date answers 404: `No data found in Cloud SQL or GCS for spx date=20260929` when no file of its year exists, and `No data for SPX on 20250901` (Labor Day) or `20251218` when the 2025 year file holds no row for it, and the chart card shows the 404 as `Couldn't load this data` with that text and no canvas (executed 2026-10-01 through the real handler with downloaded copies of all 77 files, for each of the 153 dates from 2025-08-01 to 2025-12-31 and for 2026-09-29, and for the card on a hermetic page with each of the three 404 texts). Neither kind of 200 draws the session on the page by default. The one daily bar sits at 00:00, outside the `RTH` window that is on by default, so the card is an empty chart with no message until `RTH` is turned off, and then it shows the bar (`O 6457.67 H 6466.89 L 6438.06 C 6439.45` for 2025-08-25). All 76 minute files carry UTC stamps (`datetime64[ns, UTC]`, read from each file on 2026-10-01: the session opens at 13:30 in the 44 files to 2025-10-31 and at 14:30 in the 32 from 2025-11-03, which is 09:30 in New York), and the handler drops the zone and sends them as the Eastern wall clock the chart expects ([main.py:962-964,993-998](../../platform/api/main.py#L962)), so the 78 bars of 2025-12-15 are stamped 14:30 to 20:55 and the default `RTH` window keeps 18 of them, or 30 of the 78 on 2025-09-02, which is stamped 13:30 to 19:55 (executed 2026-10-01 on a hermetic page with the handler's own answers as the mock, the bars counted by hovering the chart). `Gamma` then asks for that date's chain, and SPX's newest `alphavantage` chain (2026-09-29 23:00 UTC) has 4,871 contracts, none with `gamma`, `delta`, `implied_volatility` or `gamma_computed` and all with `open_interest` (`db-query-gv8xf`; the chains of 2018-06-15 and 2021-06-15 carry `gamma` on every contract and the one of 2026-06-15 none, `db-query-j9lld`), while `/levels` selects `gamma` and no `*_computed` column ([options.py:562-579](../../platform/api/routers/options.py#L562)), so for a chain like the newest `build_summary` answers its unavailable summary, regime `unknown`, `total_gex` 0.0, no king, gate, flip or balance and a warning that vendor gamma is missing on all contracts ([gamma.py:1052-1069](../../lib/gamma.py#L1052); executed 2026-09-30 through the real handler on a synthetic chain with no Greeks, and `test_build_summary_all_gamma_missing_is_unavailable_not_zero` in [test_gamma.py](../../tests/lib/test_gamma.py) asserts the library half), of which the page reads nothing (`warnings` is not read). Not checked: the chains of the 2025 dates that have GCS bars (the queries for 2025-10-15 and 2025-12-15 hit the statement timeout and 2025-09-02 has no snapshot).
- **A failed read of a legacy GCS file is swallowed, and the loader serves the next coarser file in its place.** `_try_gcs_download` catches every exception, logs `GCS download failed for <path>` at warning level and returns `None` ([main.py:1673-1680](../../platform/api/main.py#L1673)), so `_load_date_data` goes on to the month file and then the year file ([main.py:1682-1696](../../platform/api/main.py#L1682)): with the download of SPX's 2025-12-15 minute file made to raise, the handler answered 200 with one bar, the day's daily open, high, low and close at 00:00 from the year file and no label, where it answers 78 five minute bars when the read works, and with the year file failing too it answered 404 `No data found in Cloud SQL or GCS for spx date=20251215`, a storage failure reading as no data (executed 2026-10-01 through the real handler with the download stubbed to raise and the log captured; the fallback to these files from Cloud SQL is already recorded, unlabelled, in Live's Gaps). It is the `except Exception: return None` shape that CLAUDE.md §3.7 forbids in data-access code, and no test asserts it: nothing under `tests/` names `download_parquet` or `_try_gcs_download`, and the one test of this branch stubs `blob_exists` to `False` and asserts the 404 ([test_platform_api.py](../../tests/api/test_platform_api.py)).
- **A `daily_rates` lookup that raises drops the gamma flip with one log line and no `warnings` entry.** `build_summary` catches `RateLookupError`, logs `gamma_flip unavailable for <date>` at error level and sets the rate and yield to `None`, so `gamma_flip` is `None` ([gamma.py:1086-1095](../../lib/gamma.py#L1086)), and it adds nothing to `warnings` (read at those lines; executed 2026-09-30 through the real library on the chain of the flip sweep in [test_gamma.py](../../tests/lib/test_gamma.py): flip 593.655006741416 with the lookup answering, `None` with it raising, and `gamma_flip` the only field of the answer that differs, `warnings` included). `/levels` returns that summary as it is ([options.py:836-848](../../platform/api/routers/options.py#L836)), and the page draws the `⇅ Gamma Flip` line only when `gamma_flip` is not null and reads nothing of `warnings` ([ChartsPage.tsx:473-481](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L473)), so a missing or stale rate and a chain with no flip look the same on the chart, and no test asserts the catch (the unavailable value with nothing saying so that CLAUDE.md 3.7 forbids).
- **The page reads none of the labels that mark a degraded answer**: the dates list's `source`, `cloud_sql (stale, refresh in flight)` while a refresh runs ([main.py:717-718](../../platform/api/main.py#L717)), and the reference's `source` and `stale_days` are fetched and not read ([useMarketData.ts:52-93](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useMarketData.ts#L52)), which CLAUDE.md §3.7.1 calls no disclosure.
- **`WidgetState` sends a 401 to its sign-in state only when the message matches `/\b401\b/` or `/unauthor/i`** ([WidgetState.tsx:74-78](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/shared/WidgetState.tsx#L74)), but the market-data hook throws the server's `detail`, `sign in to continue` (and `invalid or expired sign-in` for a bad token, [auth.py:205-208](../../platform/api/auth.py#L205)), so a 401 on the chart's own query shows `Couldn't load this data` with that text and a Retry button (executed 2026-09-30 in open mode), and [WidgetState.test.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/shared/WidgetState.test.ts) asserts `isAuthError` only on strings the server never sends.
- **`DataGate`'s sign-in card has no route to render on `/charts` either**, for the reasons recorded in Live's Gaps (`AuthGate` stops a signed-out visitor in firebase mode, `iap` and `open` mode report `isSignedIn` true, and `setOnUnauthorized` has no registrant), so a 401 shows each card's own text, `similar-setups 401`, `Replay failed: sign in to continue` or the backtester's `No backtest results for` banner (executed 2026-09-30 in open mode).
- **The `SignInBanner` follows the last gated answer, not any 401**: a 401 on the reference or on the backtest list raised none because later successful answers cleared the flag, while a 401 on the market data, the dates, the indicators, the signal series or the similar request left it up ([authedFetch.ts:155-166](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/lib/authedFetch.ts#L155), executed 2026-09-30 in open mode with the other routes answering).
- **`GET /api/journal/trades/{ticker}` returns the user's whole history for the ticker on every chart load and the page keeps one date's rows** ([journal.py:882-922](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L882), [useJournalChartTrades.ts:289-303](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useJournalChartTrades.ts#L289)), so the request costs what the journal holds and not what the chart shows (CLAUDE.md §3.8), which is two rows in production today ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5919900514), statement 14).
- **`GET /api/backtest/all/{ticker}` downloads and parses every run's CSV to return one short row per run** ([backtest.py:355-458](../../platform/api/routers/backtest.py#L355); IWM: 17 files and 19.62 MiB, [V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5919900514)), so a cold request costs what the prefix holds and not what it returns (CLAUDE.md §3.8), and a concurrent cold read is declined with a 503.
- **The similar card's stats query costs what the strength bucket holds, not what it returns**: on 2026-09-30 it took 2,001 ms to count 242 rows (18,984 reached) and 1,592 ms for 14,725 (153,793 reached), against the handler's docstring of sub-100ms regardless of total row count ([signals.py:330-336](../../platform/api/routers/signals.py#L330), [V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5919900514), dispatch B).
- **`historical_signals.entry_time` holds both stored conventions, and the similar card's `When` column prints the stored string with no zone** ([SimilarSetupsCard.tsx:148](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/charts/SimilarSetupsCard.tsx#L148)): IWM `live` rows sit at UTC hours 4 to 7 (2,321 rows, which only an Eastern stamp explains) and at hours 21 to 23 (1,516 rows, which only a true UTC stamp explains) ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5919900514), dispatch C statement 2).
- **Outside review mode the similar card asks only about the last bar of the series the page holds** ([ChartsPage.tsx:799-800](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L799)), which for a finished day is the last extended-hours bar (executed 2026-09-30: the signal-series request ends at the day's last bar, 19:55 in the probe).
- Sources that disagree with the code: the design seed feeds the conditions card from `POST /api/live/signal-series`, but the card renders the `chart_voter` slice of `POST /api/live/indicators` ([ChartsPage.tsx:780-786](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L780)). UI-SCREENS.md lists [charts-cards.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/charts/charts-cards.spec.ts) and [ticker-combobox.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/dashboard/ticker-combobox.spec.ts) as the page's specs, while [replay-trainer.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/charts/replay-trainer.spec.ts) and [gamma-levels.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/shared/gamma-levels.spec.ts) also drive it. The Phase 0 Chain rows were corrected in `d3703d5`: CHARTS-17 said a 401 on the chart's own query reaches `SignInEmptyState` (it reaches `WidgetError`), CHARTS-16 said `fmtRunDate` formats run dates (it formats the equity curve's axis), CHARTS-03's reference route said `none` where its last-resort branch reads bars through `lib/eastern_time.py`, CHARTS-02 and CHARTS-10 left out `GET /api/config/market-hours`, and UI-SCREENS.md named none of the thirteen routes the page calls through its hooks and gave its Data table no freshness, gating or production figures.
- Apart from solyra's marker at [ChartsPage.tsx:364](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L364) (above), no `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits on this page's path: none in `platform/api/main.py`, `platform/api/routers/journal.py`, `platform/api/routers/signals.py`, `platform/api/routers/backtest.py`, `platform/api/routers/live.py`, `platform/api/routers/options.py` or the `lib/` modules they call. Every route in the rows declares a `response_model`, and the backtest results' free-form `trades` rows are recorded above.
- **Te for CHARTS-01, CHARTS-03, CHARTS-09 and CHARTS-10 waits for a CI run that includes the branch's tests.** `the timeframe buttons and the date input ask for bars at that resolution and on that day`, `the overlay toggles flip their state, Gamma asks for the levels only once it is on, and it is absent for a non-ETF ticker` and `the crosshair bar shows the OHLC of the hovered bar, adds Prev H / L while Ref is on, and leaves with the mouse` were added on this branch in solyra commit cd2c08b ([charts-cards.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/charts/charts-cards.spec.ts)). The behaviour already existed, so they passed as written (9 passed with the warmup project); RED was obtained by mutating a scratch copy, ten one-line mutations of `ChartsPage.tsx` and one of `CandlestickChart.tsx:451` (the last in this list), each failing the assertion that covers it: the `1h` button sending 30, the date input's `min` set to the newest date, a picked date ignored, a date outside the list refused, the Gamma request no longer gated by its toggle, the Gamma button shown for every ticker, the Vol button no longer flipping, Sig starting on, `Prev` shown with `Ref` off, `H` reading the low, and the crosshair data not cleared when the pointer leaves.
- **Only CHARTS-04 and CHARTS-05 earn Te.** CHARTS-05 is ticked on `Strategy Conditions card renders both CALL and PUT columns` (the heading, four condition labels, the `CALL · 3/5` badge and the firing suffix from a mocked answer) and on the voter and route tests of [test_chart_voter.py](../../tests/lib/test_chart_voter.py) and [test_live_chart_voter.py](../../tests/api/test_live_chart_voter.py); nothing asserts its PUT badge, `No setup` or the 14 bar threshold. CHARTS-04 is ticked on the two replay tests of [replay-trainer.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/charts/replay-trainer.spec.ts), `start -> warm-start reveal -> step x2 -> Sig disabled -> future trade hidden -> Mark Entry POSTs source:replay pinned to the last revealed bar` (Start, the `15/30` warm start, two Steps to `17/30`, the disabled Sig and the hidden-trade count) and `Stop resets to the idle "Start replay" button and re-fits the full day` (Stop, Start back, the controls gone, Sig enabled; it asserts no re-fit), both on solyra main at eca7078, where the e2e job of the cited run passed 242 tests, on the 22 reducer cases of [replaySession.test.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/replaySession.test.ts) (passed in that run's Vitest job) and on the loader tests for the bars a session reveals ([test_intraday_loader_conventions.py](../../tests/api/test_intraday_loader_conventions.py), `TestMarketDataAPI` in [test_platform_api.py](../../tests/api/test_platform_api.py)); no test asserts the speed buttons, the playback timer, the Play and Pause buttons themselves, the disabled Start or what a timeframe or date change does to a session. The chart's one main test measures the canvas and no test asserts a drawn candle, marker or price line (CHARTS-02); the Similar Setups test passes on the stats grid and on the placeholder (CHARTS-06); Vitest asserts two axis formatters and no test mounts the backtester (CHARTS-07); the scorecard's page tests prove the trigger and not the scorecard: the two of [replay-trainer.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/charts/replay-trainer.spec.ts) assert that a closed session trade posts `{ticker, session_id}` with no `trade_ids`, that the modal opens with an element carrying the trade's row test id, which the unavailable card and the scored card share, and that with no closed trade the note shows and nothing is posted, no test asserts what a row or the footer shows (badge, return, system exit, edge, fill warning, scored count, win rate, agreement) or the pending and error texts, and no pytest test passes `session_id`, the only selector the page sends, so the `WHERE` on the owner and the session ([backtest.py:526-550](../../platform/api/routers/backtest.py#L526)) is asserted by none, the endpoint tests sending `trade_ids` through a stubbed journal query, while [test_replay_labeled_trades.py](../../tests/lib/test_replay_labeled_trades.py) does assert the scoring math (CHARTS-08); CHARTS-11 has its client half asserted and its server half not: the first replay test of [replay-trainer.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/charts/replay-trainer.spec.ts) asserts the prompts and a post body with `source: 'replay'`, a session UUID and the entry date and time of the last revealed bar, with the request mocked, [replaySession.test.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/replaySession.test.ts) asserts the reveal, and [test_journal_phase2.py](../../tests/api/test_journal_phase2.py) asserts that a post with no exit is created `active` with a null return, but no pytest posts `source` `replay` or a `session_id` to `POST /api/journal/trades`: `test_post_call_trade_round_trip_cloud_sql` captures the INSERT's parameters and reads neither ([test_platform_api.py](../../tests/api/test_platform_api.py)), and the schema tests check only that [gcp/schema.sql](../../gcp/schema.sql) adds the `source` column with its `manual` default and a nullable `session_id` ([test_schema_journal_migration.py](../../tests/gcp/test_schema_journal_migration.py)). The tag is what the scorecard selects by, and the route stores `manual` and no session id for a post without it ([journal.py:221-222](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L221)), so a route that dropped it would answer the same 200 and fail no test (executed 2026-10-01: through the real handler the page's post reaches the INSERT with `source` `replay` and its session id, and the same post without them reaches it as `manual` with none; with both persistence seams of the route, [journal.py:648](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L648) and [journal.py:735](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L735), made to drop a replay tag, all 514 tests of the eleven hermetic files that name the journal router, its path or `journal_entries` still passed, none of them sending a tagged write to either seam, while a test that asserts the tag passed on the real route and failed under the same mutation). The replay cases of [index.test.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/mocks/index.test.ts) run against the mock-mode engine and not the route, [contract.test.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/mocks/contract.test.ts) checks only that the vendored schema allows a body carrying `source` and `session_id`, and no test asserts a failed post, so CHARTS-11 earns no Te. CHARTS-12 is asserted as an absence and a seeded state: `no Mark Entry button and no Trades (N) side panel outside an active replay session` asserts that the button, `Mark Entry`, the panel and the tab are absent, `stop() with zero closed session trades does not POST and shows an end-of-session note instead of the modal` asserts the note for a trade that the test seeds as `active`, and the scorecard tests mock the journal read to return their closed trade, as their comment says, to avoid driving the Mark Entry flow, so no test runs a session from Mark Entry to its end, which is the path the row describes, and no pytest passes `session_id` to its route, the only selector the page sends, so CHARTS-12 earns no Te either (as CHARTS-08); and no test holds a request to observe a loading state or asserts the empty and error texts, `Snapped to` or the banner (CHARTS-13 to CHARTS-17).
- **Rows left unticked at V and why.** CHARTS-03 (the reference route is a vendor-live AlphaVantage call for dates under 30 days old on a gated route, and the bar needs a browser pointer), CHARTS-06 (every IWM row is labelled `momentum` and strengths 3 and 4 end on 2026-05-01), CHARTS-07 (one upload of 2026-02-23 that nothing writes), CHARTS-08 (no `journal_entries` row carries a replay session id), CHARTS-09 to CHARTS-12 (actions that need a browser session and a token) and CHARTS-13 to CHARTS-16 (states that production does not show without a session). CHARTS-01, CHARTS-02, CHARTS-04 and CHARTS-05 are ticked on the freshness of the dates list and the bars ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5919900514), statements 1 to 4 and 14) and CHARTS-17 on the 401 of all fourteen routes; the in-page rendering of a 401 is not part of that tick (see the `WidgetState` and `DataGate` entries above).

### Backend notes
- Pipeline order feeding this page, earliest first (America/New_York): `fetch-fred-rates` 06:30 daily (`daily_rates`) → `fetch-av-options-realtime` every 5 min from 09:00 to 15:55 Mon-Fri (REALTIME rows, SPY IWM QQQ) → `fetch-av-options-backfill` 21:00 Mon-Fri (EOD rows for SPY IWM QQQ SPX, from the job's own args, not the trigger's) → `etf-options-retention` 02:00 daily (deletes REALTIME rows older than 30 days) · `fetch-av-options-backfill` again at 05:00 on the 1st. SPX has no realtime producer, so Trinity's SPX panel and any SPX view read EOD rows only.
- Off-list tickers take a different path. `/api/options/dates/{ticker}`, the chain, the live proxy and `/levels` accept SPY, IWM, QQQ and SPX only (`VALID_TICKERS`, `platform/api/routers/options.py:86`, 400 otherwise). The live grid instead fetches REALTIME_OPTIONS on demand for a ticker outside `GRID_SCHEDULED_TICKERS` when Cloud SQL has nothing, writes the rows back (`platform/api/routers/grid.py:594`) and caps each client IP at 10 distinct tickers per 60 s (lines 375-376, 408-436).
- Freshness the page assumes: the live grid refetches every 60 s (`staleTime` 50 s), the historical grid and `/levels` hold one hour, the dates lists five minutes (`STALE_TIME_MS`). Server-side, the live grid is cached 60 s, the historical grid and the chain twelve hours, the dates lists twelve hours behind a newest-date probe, and the live chain proxy five minutes (`platform/api/routers/grid.py:100-101`, `platform/api/routers/options.py:90-116`).
- `daily_rates` is reached two calls deep: `build_summary` and `build_grid_summary` call `get_rate_and_yield` (`lib/gamma.py:1086-1088`, `:1298-1300`), which reads the table strictly (`lib/options_greeks.py:195-205`). An unavailable rate leaves the gamma flip `None` rather than a computed value.
- Writers left out: `compute-spx-greeks-backfill` updates only the `*_computed` columns (`scripts/maintenance/compute_spx_greeks.py:149-155`), which neither the chain query (`platform/api/routers/options.py:562-579`) nor the grid loaders (`platform/api/routers/grid.py:236-254`) select. Production read 2026-10-01: `gamma_computed` is null on every SPX row of the twelve snapshot dates read, so selecting it would not help today ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5923176312)).
- The page itself holds no loading, empty or error state: each view carries its own (OPTIONS-09 to 11). Producer tests: [test_fetch_av_realtime_options.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_av_realtime_options.py), [test_fetch_av_historical_options.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_av_historical_options.py) and [test_options_retention_job.py](../../tests/gcp/test_options_retention_job.py). `fetch-fred-rates`: no test found, and the picker's watchlist write (`add_to_watchlist`, [_watchlist.py:492-557](../../gcp/fetchers/_watchlist.py#L492)) has none either (Gaps).

### Gaps
- [#826](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/826) (open) is this page's blocking issue: `or 0` on gamma and open interest with no coverage gate. Gamma now has a gate in `build_summary` (`lib/gamma.py:1057`), but `open_interest or 0.0` remains in the aggregators (`lib/gamma.py:276`, `:348`, `:452-454`, `:702`) and `greeks_coverage` counts only gamma (`lib/gamma.py:203-229`). `POST /api/options/greeks` aggregates with no gate ([options.py:784](../../platform/api/routers/options.py#L784)), and the grid's `AUDIT-2026-09-07` marker ([grid.py:550](../../platform/api/routers/grid.py#L550)) records that `build_grid_summary` has none either.
- **Swing Mode shows mock nodes beside real levels.** The comment says no mock value is shown next to a real grid ([SwingMode.tsx:811-813](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L811)), but with real `/levels` data the legend's Hedge chip reads `HS.nodes.hedge[0]` ([SwingMode.tsx:415](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L415)) and the node list adds the mock midpoints and hedges ([SwingMode.tsx:526-532](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L526)). A hermetic render on 2026-09-28 with a real IWM `/levels` payload showed `Hedge 580`, `591 Midpoint` and `580 Hedge` from [gammaMapMock.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/data/gammaMapMock.ts) next to the real King 222.
- **Failures render as empty data or as zeros.** Hermetic renders on 2026-09-28: a failed grid request shows `Data unavailable: No options grid available for this symbol.`, the text of a symbol with no grid ([SwingMode.tsx:616-622](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L616)) · a failed dates request shows `No gamma levels available for` on all three Trinity panels ([TrinityTab.tsx:57](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/TrinityTab.tsx#L57)) · a failed Greeks POST with a resolved spot shows `Total GEX +0 Positive` and `Put/Call OI 0.00 Bullish skew` from `EMPTY_GREEKS` ([ProfilesTab.tsx:326](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/ProfilesTab.tsx#L326), [useOptionsGreeks.ts:145-158](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useOptionsGreeks.ts#L145)), a fabricated flat reading (Rule 4) (filed as [solyra#74](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/issues/74)). Executed again on 2026-10-01: a failed levels or dates request shows no notice on Swing or Profiles (the Profiles levels row and regime chip vanish and `Gamma Flip` falls back to the Greeks' zero-gamma or `--`; Swing draws from the grid alone), and while the Greeks request is still pending the same zero cards show.
- **Server failures read as no data.** Hermetic probes with the connection forced to fail: the chain answered 404 `No earlier data ingested for this ticker` ([options.py:580-604](../../platform/api/routers/options.py#L580)), on which Profiles switches to the live AlphaVantage proxy, and the live grid answered 200 `unavailable` with the no-chain reason ([grid.py:229-285](../../platform/api/routers/grid.py#L229)). Both come from the swallowing `query_to_dataframe`. Profiles does show which source answered (`AlphaVantage Live` in the footer), not why.
- **The live grid calls any REALTIME snapshot from the last 20 days `realtime`.** `_load_chain_for_live` returns the newest REALTIME snapshot dated on or after `today - 20 days` with `days_behind` 0, and consults the EOD tiers only when there is none ([grid.py:232-263](../../platform/api/routers/grid.py#L232)). A hermetic probe for 2026-09-28 with only a 2026-09-10 REALTIME snapshot got `realtime`, `days_behind` 0 and a single query. Swing then shows the `LIVE` pill ([SwingMode.tsx:214](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L214)) and `live {sym} dealer exposure` in its banner, including on evenings and weekends, when the day's 21:00 EOD chain is newer than the last REALTIME snapshot (filed as [stocks#1211](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1211)). Executed again on 2026-10-01: with the real REALTIME snapshot of 2026-09-30 19:55:25Z on file and the clock on 2026-10-01, the handler answered `realtime` and the pill read `LIVE 03:55 PM ET`.
- **Today's chain is pinned to its first snapshot.** The dates list includes days with only REALTIME rows, the chain takes that day's `MAX(snapshot_ts)`, and the result is cached per `(ticker, date)` for twelve hours (`platform/api/routers/options.py:89-90`, `:562-579`). A hermetic probe served a later 19:55Z snapshot's request with the cached 14:00Z chain (`cached: true`). So Profiles and Trinity, which default to the newest date, can show an intraday snapshot as the day's chain for up to twelve hours (filed as [stocks#1212](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1212)).
- The expiry chips (All, 0DTE, Weekly, Monthly, Quarterly) change only their own highlight: `expiryFilter` never reaches the grid query or `RealHeatmapGrid` ([SwingMode.tsx:794](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L794), [SwingMode.tsx:936-960](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L936)).
- `daily_rates.sp500_div_yld` is a configured constant, not a measurement: `fetch-fred-rates` writes `SP500_DIV_YIELD_DEFAULT = 0.013` into every row (`gcp/fetchers/fetch_fred_rates.py:62`, `:119`), and the gamma flip on `/levels` and the grid uses it.
- `search_tickers` returns `[]` when the key is missing or AlphaVantage fails (`lib/ticker_info.py:414-433`). A hermetic probe got HTTP 200 with `results: []`, so the picker shows no matches although [ticker-combobox.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/dashboard/ticker-combobox.spec.ts) requires `search failure renders an inline error, never an empty "no matches" lie`.
- Sources that disagree with the code: the design seed names the views Heatseeker and Flowseeker (the Checklist Elements and the UI-SCREENS headings of OPTIONS-01 to OPTIONS-04 now carry the code's names), the code names them Gamma Map and Flow ([OptionsFlowPage.tsx:24-28](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/OptionsFlowPage.tsx#L24)), and [options-flow.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/options/options-flow.spec.ts) asserts `the borrowed internal module names never appear in the app UI`. The seed's `Pick an expiration date` re-fetches the grid, levels and chain, but no view picks an expiration: Profiles steps through snapshot dates and Swing's chips are inert (above). The seed says Swing falls back to a mock grid, the code renders `Data unavailable` instead ([SwingMode.tsx:616-622](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L616)). The seed says the mock views await `/api/options/flow` and `/contract/{id}/tape`, and neither is in the snapshot. Stale comments still call Swing mock ([OptionsFlowPage.tsx:13](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/OptionsFlowPage.tsx#L13), [GammaMapSection.tsx:9-10](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/GammaMapSection.tsx#L9)).
- 04 and 05 against the code: 04 gives `via lib/` for both grid routes, which read `etf_options_snapshots` themselves ([grid.py:255](../../platform/api/routers/grid.py#L255), [grid.py:285](../../platform/api/routers/grid.py#L285), [grid.py:332](../../platform/api/routers/grid.py#L332)), reach `daily_rates` through `lib/` and write on demand, and for `/api/options/live/{ticker}/{date_str}`, which calls AlphaVantage itself ([options.py:665](../../platform/api/routers/options.py#L665)). 04 lists only `etf_options_snapshots` for `/levels`, which also reads `daily_rates`. 05's job table lists only `DB_PASS` for `fetch-av-options-backfill`, `fetch-av-options-realtime` and `intraday-bulk-backfill`, whose inline `--set-secrets` also carry `av-api-key`, and for `intraday-bulk-backfill` `gh-stocks-repo-pat` (`gcp/deploy.sh:2270-2272`, `:2318-2320`, `:3744-3750`).
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits on this page's server path. The one in `lib/agents/summarizers.py` (line 285) is inside `summarize_market_context`, and the grid imports only `classify_gamma_freshness` from that module (`platform/api/routers/grid.py:78-81`). Every route in the rows declares a `response_model`.
- The page itself has no states: they live in the child views (OPTIONS-09 to 13). No test on main asserts OPTIONS-04, OPTIONS-09, OPTIONS-10 or OPTIONS-11, and no test exercises the expiry chips.
- **The production chains are degenerate for SPX and, on the last two EOD dates, for SPY.** Read on 2026-10-01 ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5923176312)): each of the twelve SPX snapshot dates from 2026-09-15 to 2026-09-30 has no vendor gamma and no `gamma_computed`, only calls but for 2026-09-21 (4759 puts), and open interest on three dates; the newest chain is 4871 calls with open interest 0 on every contract. The SPY EOD chains of 2026-09-29 and 2026-09-30 hold calls only (6641 and 6637 contracts), against 6647 calls and 6647 puts on 2026-09-28 and 6637 of each in the REALTIME snapshot of 2026-09-30 19:55:25Z; the job log of the 2026-09-30 run reports `6637 contracts received` with no warning and the execution succeeded. IWM and QQQ hold both sides on every date read. No layer flags a missing side: the gate in `build_summary` ([gamma.py:1057](../../lib/gamma.py#L1057)) trips only when no contract has gamma, and `build_grid_summary` has none ([grid.py:550](../../platform/api/routers/grid.py#L550)).
- **Views built on such a chain draw confident readings or zeros with no warning.** Executed 2026-10-01: the real handlers on the production chains, their JSON rendered in the page. SPX: the grid answers 200 `eod_fallback` with 1091 cells that are all zero and no warning about the missing gamma, and a spot of 7240.0 that is the median strike (`method` `median_strike`, [gamma.py:523](../../lib/gamma.py#L523)); Swing reads `KING 6810 +$0K`, `TOTAL GEX +$0K` and `TOTAL VEX +$0K`, Trinity's SPX header prints `7,240` over `Chain too thin to build a ladder for SPX.` (the cause is no gamma, and the panel reads no `warnings`), and Profiles lists the API's two warnings above cards that read `TOTAL GEX +0 Positive`, `MAX PAIN $200` and `PUT/CALL OI 0.00 Bullish skew`. SPY: `/levels` and the Greeks read the date's newest snapshot, the calls-only EOD chain, and answer `positive_gamma`, `total_gex` +221.1M, spot 775.0 (`delta`), no negative strike among 125 levels, `max_pain` 50 and `put_call_ratio` 0.0, against the REALTIME snapshot's `negative_gamma`, -46.8M on `/levels` (-38.2M on the live grid) and spot 764.46; Trinity and Profiles show the first with no warning. These are the zeros and flat readings Rule 4 forbids, reached by missing input and not by a failed request.
- **Swing's legend and heatmap can come from different snapshots, and its two totals from different windows.** The legend reads `/levels` (the date's newest `snapshot_ts`, the EOD chain once the 21:00 ET job has run) and the heatmap the live grid (the newest REALTIME snapshot): on the SPY chains of 2026-09-30 the legend read `REGIME POSITIVE TOTAL GEX +$221.1M` beside a `LIVE` heatmap whose own regime is `negative_gamma`. Its Total GEX is the levels answer's (the whole chain) and its Total VEX the grid's, summed over the strikes inside the six percent window (40 on the IWM chain) ([SwingMode.tsx:846-847](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L846)): for the IWM chain of 2026-09-30 it read `TOTAL VEX −$420.2M` while Profiles reads -970.0M for the same chain, computed at the spot it posts to the Greeks handler (the handler answers -929.9M at the parity spot the levels use; the Greeks-spot finding below) (executed 2026-10-01).
- **The Flip chip, the `Flip zero-gamma` node and Profiles' `Gamma Flip` card show the gamma balance, not the flip.** Swing reads `levels.gamma_balance`, and the grid's `gamma_flip` only when it is absent ([SwingMode.tsx:843](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L843)); Profiles reads `gamma_balance` ([ProfilesTab.tsx:308](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/ProfilesTab.tsx#L308)) and then the Greeks' `zero_gamma` ([ProfilesTab.tsx:514](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/ProfilesTab.tsx#L514)). The API renamed the OI-weighted gamma-median price on 2026-06-09 and says it is not a true regime flip ([gamma.py:108-115](../../lib/gamma.py#L108), [gamma.py:588-592](../../lib/gamma.py#L588)). On the production IWM chain of 2026-09-30 the page read `FLIP 275.74` and `GAMMA FLIP $275.74` while `gamma_flip` was 290.44 and the Greeks' `zero_gamma` 216.82 (executed 2026-10-01); `/help` defines the two apart.
- **The King chip on Swing and Trinity is the lowest-strike King, not the largest.** Swing's legend `KING` chip, its node list's `KING` row and the row tagged `king` in its heatmap read `levels.kings[0]` ([SwingMode.tsx:817](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L817), [SwingMode.tsx:839](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L839)), and `build_summary` lists every strike whose |GEX| is at least half the window's largest (`NODE_KING_PCT`, [gamma.py:68](../../lib/gamma.py#L68), [gamma.py:975-979](../../lib/gamma.py#L975)), in strike order ([gamma.py:1115](../../lib/gamma.py#L1115)), so `kings[0]` is the lowest of them. Trinity's panels take the same entry ([TrinityTab.tsx:63](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/TrinityTab.tsx#L63)): the SPY panel on the calls-only EOD chain of 2026-09-30 (ten kings, 760 to 800) read `King 760` (+6.8M) where 764 has the largest (+13.4M). On Swing the gold-outlined cell is the grid's largest |net GEX| cell, the pivot rail ranks strikes by |net GEX| and `/help` defines the King as the strike with the largest absolute net GEX ([HelpPage.tsx:123](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/HelpPage.tsx#L123)). Executed 2026-10-01 on the real IWM answers (kings 275 at -7.1M, 278 at -9.9M and 280 at -5.0M): the legend read `KING 275` and the node list `275 KING −$7.1M`, the `king` tag sat on the 275 row, the gold cell was in the 278 row (`IWM 278 · 2026-09-30 (0d)`, -6.7M) and the pivot rail opened on `278 -9.9M`; Profiles lists all three kings as `★ King` chips.
- **Swing's pill and banner say the grid is unavailable while it is still loading.** The pill reads the grid's `data_source` with a default of `unavailable` ([SwingMode.tsx:943](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L943)) and the banner takes its `Live <SYM> grid unavailable` branch for any grid that is not there yet ([SwingMode.tsx:916](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L916)), so with the grid request in flight they read `UNAVAILABLE` (hint `No snapshot available`) and `Live IWM grid unavailable, tactical read is illustrative.` beside the card's own `Loading live grid…` (executed 2026-10-01, hermetic render with the request held). A slow answer reads as an outage for as long as it takes, and Historical mode with the dates request pending shows the same banner over `Data unavailable: No options grid available for this symbol.`, a failure text for a request that was never sent ([SwingMode.tsx:807](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L807)).
- **The `unavailable` grid envelope carries zero totals, and Swing prints one of them beside a legend that stays drawn.** `_unavailable_envelope` says it carries no synthetic numbers and sets `total_gex` and `total_vex` to 0.0 ([grid.py:192-214](../../platform/api/routers/grid.py#L192)), and the legend reads the grid's `total_vex` ([SwingMode.tsx:847](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L847)). Executed 2026-10-01 (hermetic renders): with the envelope the legend read `... TOTAL GEX +$1.3M TOTAL VEX +$0K` beside `Data unavailable: no realtime or EOD chain found within the lookup window`, and with a failed grid request (a 503), which leaves no grid, `... TOTAL VEX —` beside `Data unavailable: No options grid available for this symbol.`: the same missing data reads as a measured zero in one case and as nothing in the other. In both the legend and the node list stay drawn from `/levels`, so a King, gates and a total sit next to a heatmap card that says there is no data.
- **The Contract Drilldown names the clicked contract above another contract's numbers.** Reached from a Live Feed row, the header and the contract id take that row's symbol, strike, side, expiry and DTE, and every other figure is the mock SPY 605 call's ([ContractDrilldown.tsx:77-84](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/ContractDrilldown.tsx#L77); the source comment says the metrics `stay demo`). Executed 2026-10-01 (hermetic render): after the first feed row, `DVN 38 C 01/23`, the header read `DVN $38 CALL 01/23 4 DTE` over `VOLUME 48,213 OPEN INT 31,905 AVG FILL $1.42 TOTAL PREMIUM $6.85M OTM % +0.3% MULTI % 34%`, the SPY call's. The banner calls the view a placeholder, but nothing beside the header says the figures are not DVN's.
- **Profiles' `VEX` button changes the first card and the header, not the bars.** `GEXHeatmap` is given no metric ([ProfilesTab.tsx:612-618](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/ProfilesTab.tsx#L612)) and the Greeks answer's `gex_by_strike` rows hold `gex`, `call_gex` and `put_gex` only, so `VEX by Strike` labels GEX bars (executed 2026-10-01 on the fixtures and on the real IWM answer: the bars and their labels were identical in the two modes).
- **Profiles labels every Cloud SQL chain `AlphaVantage EOD`, with a date and no time.** The footer chooses its label from `metadata.source` ([ProfilesTab.tsx:633-641](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/ProfilesTab.tsx#L633)), and the chain handler answers `cloud_sql` for REALTIME rows too. Executed 2026-10-01: the real handler's answer for the SPY REALTIME snapshot of 15:55 ET (13,274 contracts) rendered `Source: AlphaVantage EOD · Cloud SQL · 13274 contracts · snapshot 2026-09-30`.
- **Profiles posts a delta-proxy spot to the Greeks handler, so its figures differ from the levels answer's for the same chain.** `ProfilesTab` gives `POST /api/options/greeks` the spot of `estimateSpotStrikeFromDeltas` (the strike of the contract whose delta is nearest plus or minus 0.5 over the whole chain, [swingGridUtils.ts:93](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/swingGridUtils.ts#L93)) before the server's parity spot ([ProfilesTab.tsx:317-321](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/ProfilesTab.tsx#L317)), while the toolbar's `Spot` input, the spot line and the display range read the server's ([ProfilesTab.tsx:333-335](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/ProfilesTab.tsx#L333)). The proxy ignores expiry and open interest, so it can anchor on a far-dated, thin contract. Executed 2026-10-01 on the stored production chains, through the real `POST /api/options/greeks` handler and the page ([the IWM figures at both spots are in a follow-up to the V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5924526763); the first V comment lists `total_vex` -929.87M as the handler's answer at the parity spot): on IWM (5600 contracts) the proxy picks IWM270630C00290000 (the 2027-06-30 call, delta 0.49984, open interest 12), the page posted `spot_price` 290.0 against the parity spot 277.995, and the handler answered `total_gex` -59,627,104, `total_vex` -970,023,890 and `implied_move` 14.89 where the parity spot gives -54,792,573, -929,868,246 and 14.98 (`max_pain` 285, `put_call_ratio` 2.28, `zero_gamma` 216.82 and the King at 278 do not move). Profiles read `TOTAL GEX -59.6M` and, under `VEX`, `TOTAL VEX -970.0M`, beside Swing's `TOTAL GEX −$54.8M` for the same chain and a toolbar that still read `Spot: parity` over 277.99. A strike's GEX scales with the square of the spot and its VEX with the spot, so the bars are scaled by (290.0 / 277.995) squared, 1.0882, against the levels' per-strike figures (the 278 bar read -10.8M where `/levels` has -9.9M), and Total VEX by 1.0432. On the SPY REALTIME snapshot of 2026-09-30 19:55:25Z (13,274 contracts) the proxy picks a 2027-09-17 put (strike 805, delta -0.50006, open interest 4937), the page posted 805.0 against 764.46 and Total GEX read -51.9M against -46.8M on `/levels`; replaying the proxy on the other stored chains (not rendered), the SPY EOD chain of 2026-09-28 gives 805.0 against 765.3 and the handler -98.5M against -89.1M, the calls-only EOD chain of 2026-09-30 is not affected (the proxy and the server both give 775.0), and SPX has no deltas, so the page falls back to the server's 7240.0.
- **A date step can leave the previous date's Greeks on the cards.** `useOptionsGreeks` keys its query on the chain's contract count and the spot, not on the ticker or the date ([useOptionsGreeks.ts:111](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useOptionsGreeks.ts#L111)), and holds an answer for 60 seconds ([useOptionsGreeks.ts:141](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useOptionsGreeks.ts#L141)). Executed 2026-10-01 (hermetic render, each Greeks POST answered with a different Total GEX): on fixture chains of ten contracts at a spot of 220, a step to the previous date asked for that date's chain and levels and for no Greeks, and the card kept `TOTAL GEX +1.0M`, the answer for the date it had left, after reading `+0` while that date's chain loaded. After 64 seconds two steps to cached chains posted nothing and left `+1.0M`, and a step to a chain not yet fetched read `+0` while it loaded and then posted at once, the card reading `+1.0M` until the second answer, `+2.0M`, arrived 2.5 seconds after. With `@tanstack/query-core` 5.102.8 a step re-requests a stale answer only when the hook's query changes or `enabled` was false (`queryObserver.js:330-332`): a chain not yet fetched is `[]` while it loads, so the key's count is 0 and `enabled` false, and a cached chain keeps both. A step between chains that differ in contract count or in the delta-proxy spot above asks again; between chains that agree on both it asks for nothing inside the 60 seconds (the committed stepper test is such a case and asserts only the chain and levels requests), and after them only for a chain not fetched yet.
- **The picker's coverage lookup reads the table for four answers (CLAUDE.md 3.8, shape A), and the picker offers symbols the page cannot show.** On 2026-10-01 the `market_data_intraday` statement of [main.py:1255](../../platform/api/main.py#L1255) took 4,344 ms and its `EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)` 4,671 ms, reading about 7.35 million rows for four tickers, and the daily statement 4,438 ms ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5923176312)); the hook holds an answer for 60 seconds and the handler has no cache, so each distinct set of search results pays it. Any symbol the search offers can be picked, and the options routes accept SPY, IWM, QQQ and SPX only: AAPL read `No options dates available for AAPL` (the route's 400 text) on Profiles and, with the grid answering 503 as in the render, `Data unavailable: No options grid available for this symbol.` on Swing (executed 2026-10-01). The picker's tables are empty in production: `ticker_info` holds no row, and `watchlists` holds 18 rows with no `ui` source, the value [insights.py:629](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L629) writes.
- **The dates-limit test ends before the requests of the view it opened, and a request escapes its mocks.** `the Swing view asks for one date; the Profiles picker asks for all` ([options-flow.spec.ts:177](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/options/options-flow.spec.ts#L177)) waits on `waitForLoadState('networkidle')` after the click on Profiles and then asserts on the requests seen so far. Executed 2026-10-01 (scratch probe with timestamps): that call returned 1 ms after the click, because the page had reached `networkidle` on first load and a tab switch is not a navigation, and the Profiles chain request followed 12 ms later and the Greeks POST 42 ms later, as the test was ending. Run alone, the dev server logged `[vite] http proxy error` with `connect ECONNREFUSED 127.0.0.1:8000` in three of five runs (`/api/options/IWM/2026-04-24` twice, `/api/options/greeks` once): requests of a page whose options routes are all mocked reached the Vite proxy. The proxy is pinned to `http://127.0.0.1:8000` ([playwright.config.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/playwright.config.ts), `VITE_API_PROXY_TARGET`), so nothing reached staging and the requests fail at once, but the test passes with unmocked requests in flight, the escape its sibling Trinity test checks for with `escaped`. Its two URL assertions are unaffected, since they read requests already seen. Tried in a scratch copy: with one more wait before the test ends, on the heatmap header `GEX by Strike`, which draws only from the Greeks answer (`gex_by_strike`), eight runs logged no escape, against four in eight for the unmodified test in the same copy. Not tried: a wait on the date label, which renders from the dates answer that also enables the chain request, so it cannot wait for the chain or the Greeks.
- **Te for OPTIONS-07, OPTIONS-08 and OPTIONS-11 waits for a CI run that includes the branch's tests.** `the view switcher mounts one view at a time and each inner toggle swaps its own view`, `the Profiles date stepper walks the snapshot dates and loads the chain and levels of the date it lands on` and `a failed Greeks request is reported as unavailable, not shown as a measured zero (solyra#74)` were added on this branch in solyra commit d82c20b, and a fourth, a plain test, `a failed Greeks request leaves the EOD footer and the King chip on screen and is attempted twice`, in solyra commit 9543894. The first two were RED by mutating the product line under test in a scratch copy (a no-op view button, all views mounted, a drill-through that stays on the feed, an ignored Trinity toggle; a no-op older chevron, an older chevron never disabled, a chain query pinned to the newest date) and GREEN as written. The third fails as written, because the product reads `+0` and `0.00` after a failed Greeks request, and passes once the cards are hidden and an `unavailable` notice is added (scratch copy); it is declared `test.fail`, so it records solyra#74 and reports the fix by passing, and OPTIONS-11 also needs that fix before it can be ticked. The fourth holds what used to open the third's body (the EOD footer, the King chip and two Greeks attempts): inside a `test.fail` the failure of any of them would have read as the expected failure. It was GREEN as written, and RED in a scratch copy when the footer label changed, when the King chips were not drawn and when the app's retry was set to 0; the third, now the defect assertion only and retried until its three checks hold together, kept failing on the `+0` card, passed against the fix and reported `Expected to fail, but passed` with its annotation kept. The cited e2e run ran on commit eca7078, so it predates all four. OPTIONS-03 and OPTIONS-04 stay unticked for a different reason: on main only the banner test asserts OPTIONS-03 and no test mounts OPTIONS-04, and the branch test asserts the inner toggle and the drill-through, not the values.
- **Only OPTIONS-05 and OPTIONS-06 earn Te.** OPTIONS-05 is ticked on the node and chip tests of the Profiles view (`renders King/Gatekeeper/Midpoint badges when the taxonomy is populated (Profiles tab)`, the four `Gamma Levels: OptionsFlowPage UI` tests named in its Tests cell and `falls back to live endpoint and shows AlphaVantage Live badge`, all on solyra main at eca7078, where the e2e job of the cited run passed) and on the handler tests of the Greeks route (`TestGreeksContractShape`, `TestGreeksMath`), the live proxy ([test_options_live.py](../../tests/api/test_options_live.py)) and the aggregation ([test_gamma.py](../../tests/lib/test_gamma.py)); nothing on main asserts a metric value, the Greeks or levels request parameters, the spot input, the metric and side toggles, the EOD footer, the `Couldn't estimate spot` notice or the chain handler's body (the plain test added on this branch, above, asserts the footer's text on the fixture chain). OPTIONS-06 is ticked on the twenty tests of the shared picker run on the Dashboard ([ticker-combobox.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/dashboard/ticker-combobox.spec.ts)), the Vitest files for the component and the store, and the handler tests for the coverage lookup, the search and the watchlist add endpoint; no test mounts the picker on `/options` or asserts that Swing and Profiles follow a pick. The write the picker triggers, `add_to_watchlist` ([_watchlist.py:492-557](../../gcp/fetchers/_watchlist.py#L492)), is asserted by no test: [test_watchlist_helper.py](../../tests/gcp/test_watchlist_helper.py) covers the loader, its helpers, the fallback alert and the membership lookup and never calls it, the handler tests patch it out ([test_platform_api.py](../../tests/api/test_platform_api.py), [test_ticker_info.py](../../tests/api/test_ticker_info.py)), and [test_backfill_ticker.py](../../tests/gcp/test_backfill_ticker.py) tests the CLI's own function of that name (`gcp/backfill_ticker.py:308`). OPTIONS-01 and OPTIONS-02 stay unticked: no test asserts a rendered cell, a legend chip, the pill, a Trinity panel or a ladder row.
- **Rows left unticked at V and why, and where a tick rests on IWM and QQQ.** OPTIONS-02 (the SPX chain has no gamma and the SPY chain no puts on the newest date, so the panels' rows are not in the table), OPTIONS-03 and OPTIONS-04 (bundled mock fixtures, no endpoint or table), OPTIONS-06 (an action; its three routes are gated and the search is a vendor-live call), OPTIONS-07 and OPTIONS-08 (actions that need a browser session) and OPTIONS-09 to OPTIONS-12 (client-side states). OPTIONS-01 and OPTIONS-05 are ticked on the freshness of `etf_options_snapshots` and `daily_rates` for IWM and QQQ ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5923176312), section b) and OPTIONS-13 on the 401 of all eleven gated routes; the in-page rendering of a 401 is not part of that tick, and the tick for 01 and 05 does not extend to SPY or SPX, whose chains are degenerate (above). Both ticks also carry display defects that V does not cover. The display findings above are the record of them, and the list here is not complete; among them are, for OPTIONS-01, the mock Hedge chip and nodes beside real levels, failures drawn as empty data or as zeros, the `LIVE` pill on an old snapshot, the inert expiry chips, the legend and the heatmap from different snapshots, the two totals over different windows, the Flip chip that is the gamma balance, the King chip that is not the largest King, the pill and banner that read unavailable while the grid loads and the zero totals of an `unavailable` answer, and, for OPTIONS-05, the zeros of a failed or pending Greeks request (solyra#74), the Gamma Flip card that is the gamma balance, the `VEX` button that leaves the bars on GEX, the `AlphaVantage EOD` label on a REALTIME chain, the Greeks computed at the delta-proxy spot and the previous date's Greeks left on the cards after a date step.
