### Backend notes
- Pipeline order feeding this page, earliest first (America/New_York): `signal-monitor` 09:25 Mon-Fri (polls AlphaVantage intraday bars through the session, logs each fire as a `trades` row with `run_kind = 'live'` through `gcp/trade_logger.py` and sets its exit, `gcp/signal_monitor.py:1774-1790`, `:2427-2436`) → `signal-monitor-eod-resolver` 16:30 Mon-Fri (closes trades left open, `gcp/signal_monitor_eod_resolver.py:416`) → `historical-signals-watchlist` 01:00 Tue-Sat (runs `scripts/run_historical_signals.py` with `--from-watchlist` over the active tickers of the `default` watchlist owner, `gcp/deploy.sh:660-689`, resumes each ticker from its latest `entry_time` plus a minute, with a 30-day bootstrap when it has none (`scripts/run_historical_signals.py:167-245`), inserts with `ON CONFLICT (ticker, entry_time, strategy) DO NOTHING` (`gcp/historical_signals.py:233-236`) and refuses to run, exit 1, when the list holds more than 25 tickers (`scripts/run_historical_signals.py:87,503-509`; the `default` list held 16 active rows on 2026-10-01)). The ORB triggers (`orb-15m-alert`, `orb-30m-alert`) run `signal-monitor` in `--mode=orb-snapshot`, which writes no `trades` row (`gcp/signal_monitor.py:2516`).
- Freshness the page assumes: the signals query and the performance summary both hold five minutes ([SignalsPage.tsx:62](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L62), [useTradeAnalytics.ts:45](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useTradeAnalytics.ts#L45)), and neither the handler nor the hook refreshes on a schedule. The newest `historical_signals` row is the previous session's, since the writer runs at 01:00: read-only queries found the latest IWM, SPY and QQQ rows on 2026-09-25 when run on 2026-09-28, on 2026-09-29 when run at 04:28 UTC on 2026-10-01, and on 2026-09-30 when run at 05:27 UTC the same day, after that night's run ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5925426180), section b).
- The picker in the header is the same component as OPTIONS-06, so a `new`-badged pick writes the ticker to `watchlists` under the signed-in user's email (`default` without an identity), while `historical-signals-watchlist` reads only the `default` owner: the pick reaches this table only if something else puts the ticker on the `default` list (Gaps). When Cloud SQL is not configured (local dev only), the handler reads the legacy GCS parquets instead (`platform/api/routers/signals.py:90`, `:253`, `source: gcs_parquet`), never as a fallback from a failed query.
- Writers left out: `scripts/backfill_timeframe_tags.py` updates only `timeframe_tag` and `expected_hold_min` (lines 172-174), neither of which the handler selects (`platform/api/routers/signals.py:190-212`).
- Producer tests: `historical-signals-watchlist` [test_historical_signals.py](../../tests/gcp/test_historical_signals.py), [test_run_historical_signals_args.py](../../tests/scripts/test_run_historical_signals_args.py), [test_run_historical_signals_provenance.py](../../tests/scripts/test_run_historical_signals_provenance.py), [test_run_historical_signals_strategy.py](../../tests/scripts/test_run_historical_signals_strategy.py) · `signal-monitor` [test_signal_monitor_persist.py](../../tests/gcp/test_signal_monitor_persist.py), [test_trade_logger_reads.py](../../tests/gcp/test_trade_logger_reads.py) · `signal-monitor-eod-resolver` [test_signal_monitor_eod_resolver.py](../../tests/gcp/test_signal_monitor_eod_resolver.py) · the migrator's `trades` load [test_migrate_trades_tz_guard.py](../../tests/gcp/test_migrate_trades_tz_guard.py).

### Gaps
- [#905](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/905) was closed as a duplicate of [#1206](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1206) on 2026-09-28, which is this page's blocking issue in its place. Checked on 2026-10-01: #1206 is still open, last updated 2026-09-28, with no assignee, and its status line reads `tracking only, not being worked on` (decided 2026-09-27), so the blocker holds and nothing on this page has moved on it. What it tracks is the live `trades` this page's Performance block sums, by buy and sell and by month (buy calls moved the called way 54.5% of the time and sell calls 39.9%, 2026-05-01 to 2026-09-25), so it bears on SIGNALS-02 directly. Its tables carry no breakdown by score, so the Score column and the Min score filter (SIGNALS-03, SIGNALS-04) meet it only through #905's sentence that score does not rank outcomes, which was not re-measured here. The Performance block shows exactly those live trades, the handler reads `trades` with `run_kind = 'live'` ([analytics.py:146-151](../../platform/api/routers/analytics.py#L146)), under the label `Performance · 90-day backtest` ([SignalsPage.tsx:211](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L211)). The hook's comments and the design seed also call them backtest aggregates ([useTradeAnalytics.ts:3-5](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useTradeAnalytics.ts#L3)).
- **The date filters see only the newest 5,000 rows.** The page asks for `limit=5000`, the handler returns the most recent rows ([signals.py:190-212](../../platform/api/routers/signals.py#L190)), and From and To filter in the browser ([SignalsPage.tsx:163-170](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L163)). A read-only query on 2026-09-28 (execution `db-query-8zkw8`) found 189,980 IWM rows back to 2015-01-02, with the 5,000th newest at 2026-07-17; on 2026-10-01 (execution `db-query-28vss`) it was 190,159 rows and the window ran from 2026-07-21 to 2026-09-29. Outside review mode, an earlier range shows `No signals match your filters` while the header counts all 190,159 (filed as [stocks#1213](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1213)); Min score and a Score sort look at the same window, although the handler accepts `direction` and `min_score` ([signals.py:225-226](../../platform/api/routers/signals.py#L225)) and the page sends neither.
- **The Min score filter offers scores no row has.** The options run 5+ to 8+ ([SignalsPage.tsx:251-255](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L251)), and `signal_strength` of IWM runs 3 to 7 (3: 153,793 rows, 4: 18,984, 5: 14,731, 6: 2,596, 7: 55, execution `db-query-28vss`). The page's window, the newest 5,000 rows, holds 5 (4,178), 6 (808) and 7 (14) only, so `5+` removes nothing although it draws `Clear`, `7+` leaves 14 rows and `8+` always empties the table. The schema comment says `3..5` and `4/5` ([schema.sql:2142-2143](../../gcp/schema.sql#L2142)) and the job stores `conditions_met` as `<score>/5` whatever the score ([trading_analysis.py:970](../../lib/trading_analysis.py#L970), `7/5` on the window's 14 top rows); the page reads neither.
- **Review mode's cutoff is read as UTC.** `end_time` is documented as Eastern ([signals.py:228](../../platform/api/routers/signals.py#L228)), but the cutoff is cast in the database session ([signals.py:172-173](../../platform/api/routers/signals.py#L172)), which runs in UTC: read-only queries on 2026-09-28 (execution `db-query-ktktq`) and on 2026-10-01 (execution `db-query-28vss`) returned `UTC` and cast `2026-09-25 10:30:00` to `10:30:00+00:00`. Of the newest 5,000 IWM rows 966 sit in UTC hours 4 to 7, which true UTC cannot hold (00:00 to 03:59 ET), and 427 in hours 21 to 0, which Eastern labels cannot hold, so `entry_time` carries both conventions CLAUDE.md §3.9 describes for its source table. The cutoff is therefore four hours early for the true-UTC rows (five under EST): executed through the real handler, a review of 2026-09-29 at 10:30 ET kept 12 of the 44 rows stored at or before 10:30 ET (14:30 UTC) that day, none from after 06:30 ET. The Time column prints each row's stored string ([SignalsPage.tsx:72](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L72)) (filed as [stocks#1210](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1210)).
- **Every error reads as missing pipeline data.** Any failed signals request, a 503 outage, a 500 defect, a 404 and a 401 alike, renders `Signal data not found for {ticker}. Run the signals generation pipeline first.` after one retry (the `isError` branch at [SignalsPage.tsx:294-299](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L294); executed 2026-10-01), with no status, no server reason, no retry button and no `role`. The case the text describes, a ticker the pipeline has not processed, answers 200 with no rows and reads `No signals match your filters` (SIGNALS-09). A failed performance summary with no cached answer renders nothing: the block shows only when `closedTrades > 0` ([SignalsPage.tsx:209](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L209)), and a failed refetch of a cached summary leaves its tiles on screen (the next bullet).
- **A failed refetch hides cached rows behind the pipeline card.** `data` survives a failed refetch, but the table is drawn only while `!isError` ([SignalsPage.tsx:307](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L307)), the card shows on `isError` ([SignalsPage.tsx:294-299](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L294)) and the label tests `data` ([SignalsPage.tsx:193](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L193)). Executed 2026-10-01 in the hermetic page, on the shared three-row fixture ([signals.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/mocks/signals.ts): `MOCK_SIGNALS` and `MOCK_TRADE_SUMMARY`, not production rows): the signals route answered 200 and then 503, the clock was moved six minutes past the five-minute `staleTime` and the tab was given focus back; after the retry (two 503 answers) the table was gone, the card read `Run the signals generation pipeline first` and the label still read `IWM · 3 signals`, so rows the page holds are hidden behind a card that blames the pipeline and nothing says they are old. Run the same way with the summary route answering 503, the cached Performance tiles stayed on screen with no marker (`Win rate` still `62.0%`). The criteria that put `signal explorer` in the label, or leave the Performance block out, after a failure hold only with no cached answer.
- **The permission state the page was built with cannot show, and the one that shows points at the pipeline.** `DataGate` replaces the body only when `blocked && !isLoading && !isSignedIn` ([SignalsPage.tsx:200](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L200)), `AuthGate` keeps a signed-out user off the page in `firebase` mode and `useUser` reports signed in always in `open` and `iap` modes, so the page's `Sign in to load data` block is unreachable (executed 2026-10-01 with `/signals` opened signed out: the sign-in screen showed and the only API request was `GET /api/config/firebase`). A signed-in user whose token is refused gets the shell's expired-session strip and SIGNALS-10's card, which says nothing about signing in.
- **A signed-in user's pick of a new symbol is not on the list the nightly job reads.** The picker's add writes `watchlists` under the signed-in user's email, or under `default` without an identity (`_watchlist_owner`, [insights.py:57-67](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L57)), and `historical-signals-watchlist` calls `load_watchlist()` with no argument, whose owner defaults to `default` ([run_historical_signals.py:129-140](../../scripts/run_historical_signals.py#L129), [_watchlist.py:411-412](../../gcp/fetchers/_watchlist.py#L411)), so the symbol gets signals only if something else puts it on the `default` list. Executed 2026-10-01: the job's ticker resolution asked for `('default', 'all')`, and the router passed the signed-in user's email to the add (`test_add_scopes_to_signed_in_user`, [test_platform_api.py](../../tests/api/test_platform_api.py), with the write patched out). Production holds 16 active `watchlists` rows, all owned by `default`, none from the `ui` source the add writes, and `ticker_info`, the OVERVIEW and peers cache that the add fills through `get_ticker_info` ([ticker_info.py:306-377](../../lib/ticker_info.py#L306)) and `get_peers` ([ticker_info.py:515-563](../../lib/ticker_info.py#L515)), holds no row ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5925426180)). The search ([ticker_info.py:403-446](../../lib/ticker_info.py#L403), [insights.py:544-556](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L544)) never reads or writes it (executed 2026-10-01 through `TestClient` with the vendor calls stubbed: the search made one vendor call and no `ticker_info` access, while the add read the row and wrote the OVERVIEW row and the peers).
- **The Performance block has four properties its label does not say.** It is not review aware: with review mode set the summary request stays the same (`days=90`, counted back from now) and the tiles do not change while the table is cut at the review time. Its call and put counts include open trades: `callCount` and `putCount` count every trade the query returned ([analytics.py:116-117](../../platform/api/routers/analytics.py#L116)), so three closed calls and two open puts read `3` closed over `3 call · 2 put` (executed through the real handler; production has no open trade today). It exists for three tickers: the live trades of the last 90 days are IWM (189 calls), QQQ (242 calls) and SPY (166 calls and 144 puts) while `historical_signals` holds rows for 16 tickers, so the block is absent, with no word, for the other 13. And a summary that fails with no cached answer leaves it out silently, while a failed refetch leaves the cached tiles unmarked. Its `Σ return` and `Avg return / trade` are sums and means of per-trade percentage points, not a compounded or account figure, and a trade that closes at exactly 0.00% counts as a loss, the backtest engine's convention ([test_backtest.py:213](../../tests/lib/test_backtest.py#L213)).
- **The table prints stored strings and thin volumes without saying so.** `Time` prints the first 16 characters of the stored string with no zone ([SignalsPage.tsx:70-73](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L70)), so `2026-07-21 04:02`, a 04:02 ET bar, and `2026-09-29 23:21`, a 19:21 ET bar, print side by side (the two conventions of the cutoff finding, stocks#1210). `Volume` reads `0K` for any bar under 500 shares ([SignalsPage.tsx:128](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L128)): 695 of the window's 5,000 rows, among them the newest (171 shares). On a payload with nulls (production's IWM rows have none) a null `Score` prints `0.0`, an absent one `NaN`, a null direction `null` and an absent time or direction `undefined`, while Price, RSI, EMA9 and Volume print `--`, although the page's own type says the render sites em-dash the gaps ([SignalsPage.tsx:26-27](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L26)); a zero score is a value, not a gap (executed 2026-10-01).
- **This page does not share the Dashboard's signals renderers.** `SignalsPage` defines its own columns ([SignalsPage.tsx:69-131](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L69)) and reads neither `return_pct` nor `conditions_met`, so area 04's finding that Live signals multiplies `return_pct` by 100 and prints a missing one as `+0.00%` does not apply here, while its finding that the Time column mixes two conventions does (stocks#1210).
- **The filter bar has no accessible names and the page no test ids.** The text beside From, To and Min score is a `span`, so `getByLabel` finds none of them; the date inputs have no `aria-label` or `id`, the direction buttons have no `aria-pressed` (the active direction is conveyed by colour only), and the sortable headers are `th` elements with a click handler and no role, `tabindex` or `aria-sort` ([SignalsPage.tsx:222-292,311-323](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L222)), against REQ-UX-001's keyboard and assistive-technology target (executed against the accessibility tree 2026-10-01). The click that removes a sort (the third on `Dir` or `Score`, the first on `Time` from the initial state while a row is in view) shows the response's order, oldest first, with no indicator.
- **A `To` date typed before review mode is held and comes back.** `localDateTo` keeps the typed value while the input shows the review date, and leaving review mode without pressing `Clear` re-applies it with no sign it had been waiting (executed 2026-10-01: `To` typed as 2026-09-20, review mode entered and left, then `To 2026-09-20`, `Clear` visible and ` · 4,256 shown` again).
- **The monitor's run of 2026-09-30 ended at 10:11 ET with an internal error.** `signal-monitor-nwmlx` reads `failed with exit code: 0 and message: Internal error` at 14:11:41 UTC, after `cap_diag: SKIP` lines for IWM and SPY (`daily_trades=5 cap=5`), while the previous session's run lasted to 16:00 ET; the newest live entries of that day are at 09:34 to 09:53 ET ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5925426180), section c). Whether it missed fires after 10:11 ET, and so what the Performance block undercounts, is not checked.
- **A fire whose trade-log write fails is counted only when the logger raises, and the Performance block undercounts it either way.** `signal_monitor.py` upserts the `signal_alerts` row first and logs the `trades` row second, and the second can fail in two ways that the monitor treats differently. A defect (a dropped column, a bad type, a `KeyError`) is raised by `TradeLogger.log_trade`, caught at the call site, added to `persist_trade_failure_count` and logged with `the signal_alerts row exists without its trades row` ([signal_monitor.py:1792-1797](../../gcp/signal_monitor.py#L1792)). An infrastructure error (a `ConnectionError`) is swallowed inside `log_trade`: `_outage_or_raise` logs a `Cloud SQL trade write failed, falling back to Parquet` warning and returns ([trade_logger.py:29-49](../../gcp/trade_logger.py#L29), called at [trade_logger.py:117](../../gcp/trade_logger.py#L117)), and the call falls through to a Parquet file under `data/trades` in the job's own container ([trade_logger.py:119-129](../../gcp/trade_logger.py#L119)), so `log_trade` returns, the monitor logs `Trade logged for <ticker> <direction>` and counts nothing. Executed 2026-10-01 on the real `SignalMonitor._persist_signal_alert` and `TradeLogger` with only `gcp.database.upsert_dataframe` replaced (no database, a temporary working directory): a `ConnectionError` on the `trades` upsert left `persist_trade_failure_count` at 0, wrote the Parquet file and logged `Trade logged`; a `KeyError` set it to 1, wrote no file and logged the failure. Either way the `signal_alerts` row has no `trades` row, and the block, which sums `trades` ([analytics.py:131-178](../../platform/api/routers/analytics.py#L131)), misses that fire with no trace on the page. No job path reads the counter (only [test_signal_monitor_persist.py:295](../../tests/gcp/test_signal_monitor_persist.py#L295) does) and the outage leaves only a warning in the job's log, so whether any fire in the 90-day window was lost either way is not checked; the counter is per run and is not stored.
- **`columnFilters` and the `Dir` column's `filterFn` are dead code** ([SignalsPage.tsx:81-82,140](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L81)): neither is ever set, and the direction filter is the `dirFilter` state.
- 04 gives `via lib/` for `/api/signals/{ticker}`, and the handler's inline SQL is already recorded in Dashboard's Gaps (area 04).
- The design seed's backend map lists `historical_signals` among `signal-monitor`'s outputs, but `gcp/signal_monitor.py` never names the table, and its writer is `historical-signals-watchlist` (`gcp/historical_signals.py:121`, `:124`, `:235`). The seed's `fetch-alphavantage-intraday` entry gives only the monthly run and omits `av-intraday-nightly`. UI-SCREENS.md listed only `/api/signals/` among this page's calls and now lists the summary and the picker's three routes as well (SIGNALS-01, SIGNALS-02), and its PR lineage no longer says that #803's RVOL shadow metric reached this page (it left scoring, `historical_signals` and this page's data as they were).
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits in `platform/api/routers/signals.py`, `platform/api/routers/analytics.py` or the picker's handlers, and every route in the rows declares a `response_model`.
- **No test on main asserts SIGNALS-03, SIGNALS-05, SIGNALS-06, SIGNALS-08 or SIGNALS-11, and the page-layer tests credited to the other rows assert little.** Checked on 2026-10-01 against every spec that opens `/signals` ([signals.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/signals/signals.spec.ts) and the route loop of [navigation.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/shared/navigation.spec.ts), which asserts the nav, `main` and a clean console) and a search of `tests/` and `src/` for `Loading signals`. `lists alert rows` looks for `4.5`, `3.0` or `2.0` and `shows CALL and PUT directions` for `CALL` and `PUT`: both are satisfied by the KPI block (the Win rate tile's `62.0%` contains `2.0`, and `168 call · 132 put` contains both words) and passed with an empty signals response and with a failed request (executed), so they assert no table row and no button. `renders signal explorer heading` asserts the title only and `shows the 90-day Performance P&L card` three labels and the value `1.74`. SIGNALS-07's and SIGNALS-10's tests cover the handler only: `test_signals_end_date_filter` and its two siblings return pre-filtered rows from a mock and hold whatever the SQL says, so no test asserts the cutoff predicate or its zone, the newest-N read, the ascending order or the direction filter, and the summary's tests assert the query and the failures, not a computed figure. SIGNALS-12's tests assert the wrapper's callback, which no code outside the tests registers, and a sign-in screen on another route.
- **Te for SIGNALS-03, SIGNALS-05, SIGNALS-06, SIGNALS-07 and SIGNALS-11 waits for a CI run that includes the branch's tests.** Three tests were added on this branch in solyra commit 8ffbb54 ([signals.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/signals/signals.spec.ts)): `the direction buttons, Min score, the date range and the column headers act on the fetched rows` (SIGNALS-03 and SIGNALS-05; it also asserts the count label at load and after PUT, the Time cells at load and after PUT, the Score cells, the Dir cells of the two CALL rows and the `No signals match your filters` message at `8+`, which SIGNALS-01, SIGNALS-04 and SIGNALS-09 do not get credit for), `Clear shows only while a filter is set and resets the direction, Min score and both dates` (SIGNALS-06) and `review mode asks the API for the cutoff, locks the To date under a global tag and gives it back` (SIGNALS-07, and the tag of SIGNALS-11). Each passed as written, because the behaviour was already there, and each was shown to fail by mutating the product line under test in a scratch copy (16 mutations: the direction filter removed, `>` for `>=` in Min score, From removed, To made exclusive, sorting removed; `Clear` leaving each of the four filters, resetting the sort or always drawn; `end_date` or `end_time` not sent, `To` not locked, the `global` tag removed, `To` showing the local value). The first test also passed with the default sort emptied (`useState<SortingState>([])`), because it served the rows newest first, the order the default sort produces; the commit `test(signals): serve rows oldest first to pin the default sort` serves them oldest first, as the handler returns them, and the same mutation then fails its Time assertion at load (17 mutations in all).
- **Rows left unticked at V and why, and what the ticks do not cover.** SIGNALS-03, SIGNALS-05 and SIGNALS-06 are client-side controls over rows a gated request returns, SIGNALS-07 is an action whose request is gated (its statement was run against production, the route was not called) and SIGNALS-08 to SIGNALS-11 are client-side states that production does not show without a browser and a token ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5925426180)). SIGNALS-01, SIGNALS-02 and SIGNALS-04 are ticked on the freshness of `historical_signals` and `trades` (IWM, with QQQ and SPY for the trades) and SIGNALS-12 on the 401 of the six gated routes; the picker's search (a vendor-live call) and its add (a write) were not exercised, and how the page presents a 401 was executed in the hermetic suite only. The four ticks also carry display defects that V does not cover. The display findings above are the record of them, and the list here is not complete; among them are, for SIGNALS-01, the count that is the table's size while the rows are the newest 5,000, the ` · n shown` that counts that window, the label that keeps the cached count over SIGNALS-10's card after a failed refetch and a signed-in user's pick that the nightly job never reads; for SIGNALS-02, the `90-day backtest` label over live trades, the call and put counts that include open trades, the block that exists for three tickers, ignores review mode, is absent with no word when the first summary fails and stays unmarked when a refetch fails, and a trade-log write whose failure is counted only when the logger raises; for SIGNALS-04, the Time column in two conventions with no zone, `0K` for bars under 500 shares, a null `Score` printed `0.0` and 500 rows of a 5,000-row window; and, for SIGNALS-12, the permission block that cannot show and the card that points at the pipeline.
- **Te for SIGNALS-09 rests on a loose page assertion.** `shows empty state when no alerts` matches `no.*signal` or `empty` and takes the first match, so it would also pass on the failure card's `not found ... signals` text; exactly one element matches today, the table's message (executed). It does not assert that the Performance block hides with no closed trade (the test leaves the default summary in place), the summary handler's answer for no trades (`test_summary_restricts_to_live_trades` returns an empty frame and asserts the status and the SQL) or the message for filters that exclude every row, which waits for a CI run that includes the branch's tests.

no call of its own) | none (state of INSIGHTS-01 to INSIGHTS-05, INSIGHTS-07 and INSIGHTS-09; no call of its own) | none (state of INSIGHTS-01 to INSIGHTS-05, INSIGHTS-07 and INSIGHTS-09; no call of its own) | solyra [authedFetch.test.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/lib/authedFetch.test.ts) (`a 401 from a gated path fires onUnauthorized`, `a 401 from an OPEN path does not fire onUnauthorized`) · [test_platform_auth.py](../../tests/api/test_platform_auth.py) (`test_firebase_requires_valid_token`: a gated path answers 401 without a token in `firebase` mode, on a synthetic route) · solyra [auth-gate.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/shared/auth-gate.spec.ts) (`firebase mode, signed out → login screen blocks the app`, on `/dashboard`) · [test_routers_insights_admin.py](../../tests/lib/test_routers_insights_admin.py) (`test_admin_requires_sign_in`, `test_admin_non_admin_user_403`; skipped in CI) · none asserts `DataGate`, the strip on this page or a 401 on an insights route |

### Backend notes
- Pipeline order feeding this page, earliest first (America/New_York, from the trigger crons in [gcp/deploy.sh](../../gcp/deploy.sh)): `auto-refresh-top-n` 08:10 Mon-Fri (ranks the `default` owner's watchlist through `lib/agents/ranker/` and enqueues the top three tickers onto `insight-pipeline-queue`, each run recorded with trigger `on_demand`, `gcp/auto_refresh_top_n.py:219`, `:288`, `:294`) → `premarket-brief` 08:30 Mon-Fri (`premarket_analysis`, read by the House Views card) → `insight-pipeline` 08:45 Mon-Fri (the `default` owner's `in_insight` tickers, `gcp/insight_pipeline_job.py:772-773`, one execution per ticker through the same queue, trigger `scheduled`, writing `insight_runs`, `insight_reports` and `insight_reports_history`) → `insight-discord-push` 09:15 Mon-Fri, a separate job that posts the day's reports to Discord and is not on this page's path. The fetchers behind the report's inputs, with their times, are in INSIGHTS-07's Data cell.
- Freshness the page assumes, from its hooks: the report, the history and the brief hold for 60 s, a by-id report for five minutes, the ranking for five minutes, the admin roster for 30 s, and a run is polled every 3 s while `queued` or `running` with no limit ([useInsights.ts:181-185](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useInsights.ts#L181)). Read-only queries found the newest live reports for SPY, IWM and QQQ at 2026-09-25 12:46 UTC, 08:46 ET when run on 2026-09-28 (execution `db-query-zdb25`), and at 2026-09-30 12:55 UTC, 08:55 ET when run at 04:06 ET on 2026-10-01 (execution `db-query-m5sb8`), so until that day's run lands (08:10 for the tickers `auto-refresh-top-n` picks, 08:45 for the `in_insight` ones) the Briefing tab shows the previous session's report under its timestamp alone.
- The report, by-id and history reads touch only `insight_reports`: the cards render what the pipeline assembled at generation time from the tables in INSIGHTS-07. The House Views card is the exception, because its brief reads `premarket_analysis` and `market_data_daily` (INSIGHTS-01). `GET /api/insights/report/{ticker}` also feeds DASHBOARD-08 and `GET /api/dashboard/brief/{ticker}` DASHBOARD-01. `GET /api/dashboard/brief/{ticker}` also overlays a live AlphaVantage GLOBAL_QUOTE price during the regular session, because the page sends no `date` ([dashboard.py:211-222](../../platform/api/routers/dashboard.py#L211), [useInsights.ts:28](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useInsights.ts#L28)). The rows here follow the producers further than those cells: the `auto-refresh-top-n` runs, the queue's role in the fan-out, Vertex AI and `model_routing`.
- INSIGHTS-08's cutoff path uses every resource INSIGHTS-07 lists, so its cells restate them with the bound each read applies. A hermetic capture of the bundle's SQL on 2026-09-28 found the same six tables read live and with a 10:30 UTC cutoff (`earnings_calendar`, `economic_events`, `etf_options_snapshots`, `market_data_daily`, `news_sentiment`, `sec_filings`), and the reflection read, the planner's override read and the route snapshot take no cutoff argument.
- The header's `TickerCombobox` ([InsightsPage.tsx:117](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/InsightsPage.tsx#L117)) has no Checklist row. Its calls, `GET /api/insights/ticker/search`, `GET /api/market/coverage` and, for a `new`-badged pick, `POST /api/insights/watchlist/add`, are the same as OPTIONS-06 and SIGNALS-01.
- Producer tests: `insight-pipeline` [test_insight_pipeline_job.py](../../tests/gcp/test_insight_pipeline_job.py), [test_insight_pipeline_memory.py](../../tests/gcp/test_insight_pipeline_memory.py), [test_canonical_provenance.py](../../tests/gcp/test_canonical_provenance.py) · the fan-out and enqueue [test_insight_tasks.py](../../tests/gcp/test_insight_tasks.py) · `auto-refresh-top-n` [test_auto_refresh.py](../../tests/gcp/test_auto_refresh.py) · the pipeline's `lib/agents/` modules [test_agent_orchestrator.py](../../tests/agents/test_agent_orchestrator.py), [test_agent_summarizers.py](../../tests/agents/test_agent_summarizers.py), [test_agent_embeddings.py](../../tests/agents/test_agent_embeddings.py), [test_agent_vertex_adapter.py](../../tests/agents/test_agent_vertex_adapter.py), [test_agent_model_routing.py](../../tests/agents/test_agent_model_routing.py), [test_trade_planner.py](../../tests/lib/test_trade_planner.py), [test_ranker.py](../../tests/agents/test_ranker.py) · `insight-discord-push` [test_insight_discord_push.py](../../tests/gcp/test_insight_discord_push.py).
- The request contract of the API-served rows in areas 09 to 12 is also checked by solyra [contract.test.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/mocks/contract.test.ts): verbs and paths against the vendored snapshot, query parameter names, the mock payloads, and sample request bodies that include the chat, the watchlist add and the playbook evaluation. It passed 14 of 14 on 2026-09-28 and is not repeated per row.

### Gaps
- [#916](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/916) (open) is this page's blocking issue: ablate the agent graph and keep numeric levels to supplied structured fields. The headline entry, stop and targets come from `lib/agents/trade_planner.py` when persona plans compute, and from the LLM portfolio manager otherwise ([orchestrator.py:626-640](../../lib/agents/orchestrator.py#L626)).
- A read-only query on 2026-09-28 (execution `db-query-8lfh7`) found an empty `persona_plans` on every live report through 2026-09-15 and on none from 2026-09-16, after PR #1102 (merged 2026-09-15) fixed the planner's silent failure. The History tab opens those older reports, whose headline plans are the LLM's, with no marker. A read on 2026-10-01 (execution `db-query-sv5tv`) counted 679 live reports with an empty list and none among the 60 written since 2026-09-16.
- **Refresh runs inside the API process on the deployed services.** Neither service sets `ENV` (read 2026-09-28), so `_is_local_dev()` is true ([insights.py:489-490](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L489)) and the run becomes a `BackgroundTask` recorded as `local_dev` ([insights.py:870-874](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L870)), never a queue task. A hermetic probe of the handler confirmed that branch, and a second with `ENV` set and `google.cloud.tasks_v2` unimportable took the same fallback (`gcp/insight_tasks.py:146-151`). A `pip install --dry-run --report` of the image's requirements resolved 92 packages without `google-cloud-tasks`, and the running image was not inspected. The services run 1 CPU and 2Gi with `run.googleapis.com/cpu-throttling=true` against the job's 4Gi, and whether a run finishes after the response is sent was not checked. `insight_runs` holds 10 `local_dev` rows, the newest from 2026-04-25 (execution `db-query-jq7t2`), all `done` in 17.4 s to 22.8 s and none newer on 2026-10-01 (execution `db-query-7vlmp`) (filed as [stocks#1214](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1214)).
- **Runs can stay `running` and the page polls them indefinitely.** Six `on_demand` runs (AMD three times, AVGO twice, MU once) sat in `running` from 2026-09-17 to 2026-09-25 (execution `db-query-wqqwr`). Two of them, AVGO and AMD on 2026-09-17, are the fan-out children the comment in `deploy_insight_pipeline` records as killed out of memory that day at the 4Gi limit, their retry standing down without re-running (`gcp/deploy.sh:584-588`), and the live job still runs 4Gi and 1 CPU (read 2026-09-28) against the function's 8Gi and 2 CPU. The other four were not checked. The page shows no failure either way: a failed request goes to the console ([InsightsPage.tsx:86-89](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/InsightsPage.tsx#L86)) and a `failed` run only stops the spinner ([InsightsPage.tsx:64-76](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/InsightsPage.tsx#L64)), its `error` never rendered. The same six rows stood on 2026-10-01 (execution `db-query-hw76g`). Hermetic runs on 2026-10-01 showed more: a status poll that itself fails leaves `queued…` and the disabled button in place while the page keeps polling, a refresh POST answered 503 logs to the console and draws nothing, and a switch to another ticker while a run is `running` keeps the `running…` label, so when the run ends the page refetches the new ticker's report and history, not those of the ticker that was run.
- **A replay result is never shown as one.** A cutoff run writes `run_kind = 'replay'` ([insights.py:455](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L455)), the Briefing tab reads live rows only ([insights.py:228](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L228), [insights.py:240](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L240)), History lists every kind without rendering `run_kind` ([InsightsPage.tsx:395-460](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/InsightsPage.tsx#L395)), and the report and by-id envelopes carry `run_kind: null` although the model declares it ([insights.py:819-826](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L819)): the real handler returned null for a live, a replay and a backfill row (executed 2026-10-01). Production holds 2 replay rows and 85 backfill rows beside 785 live ones (execution `db-query-m5sb8`), and History drew the SPY replay row of 2026-09-11 exactly like a live row. A ticker with only backfill rows, ARM (four, 2026-04-20 to 2026-04-23), reads `No report yet` on the Briefing tab while History lists them (executed). The cutoff input carries no zone and the server reads it as UTC ([insights.py:115-116](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L115)), so `10:30` means 06:30 EDT (probe), and the input's `max` is the UTC clock ([InsightsPage.tsx:167](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/InsightsPage.tsx#L167)). With the browser at 10:00 in America/New_York the input's `max` read `2026-09-30T14:00` and accepted a local `13:00`, and a cutoff typed past `max` is still sent, its 400 `as_of '2027-01-01T10:00' is in the future` reaching only the console (executed 2026-10-01).
- **A replay is not fully point-in-time.** Three reads ignore the cutoff. The catalysts section's news slice runs 23 hours 59 minutes past it ([summarizers.py:1631](../../lib/agents/summarizers.py#L1631)), so a probe with a 10:30 UTC cutoff captured a query ending at 10:29 UTC the next day. The reflection memory read takes no cutoff ([summarizers.py:1877](../../lib/agents/summarizers.py#L1877)), though no row qualifies for it today. The trade planner's blue-sky offset comes from the newest calibration (`lib/strategies/exit_config_overrides.py:119-127`). The page describes the run as using data available at the chosen time ([InsightsPage.tsx:160](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/InsightsPage.tsx#L160)) (filed as [stocks#1217](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1217)).
- **Two cards can never fill.** Signal alerts are left out of the bundle on purpose ([summarizers.py:1963-1976](../../lib/agents/summarizers.py#L1963)), so `supporting_signals` is always empty. Reflection memory needs rows with an `embedding` and `user_email IS NULL` ([summarizers.py:1902](../../lib/agents/summarizers.py#L1902)), while the journal route stamps every row with the caller's email or `local` (`platform/api/routers/journal.py:161`, `:705`) and writes no embedding, and the only embedding writer is the manual `scripts/backfill_journal_embeddings.py`. None of the 145 reports in the 60 days to 2026-09-28 carried either list (execution `db-query-jq7t2`), `journal_entries` held 2 rows with no embedding (execution `db-query-wqqwr`), and the cards present that design as an absence of data. A read on 2026-10-01 found none of the 46 reports of the last 10 days with a supporting signal or a similar past trade (execution `db-query-sv5tv`).
- **A trader or portfolio-manager failure publishes a fabricated plan with no banner.** The trader's fallback is flat with a zero entry and stop ([orchestrator.py:417-427](../../lib/agents/orchestrator.py#L417)), the manager's carries confidence 0.3 ([orchestrator.py:467-482](../../lib/agents/orchestrator.py#L467)), and neither adds a failed section. A hermetic render of such a report showed an entry of `0.00`, a stop of `0.00` and `Confidence 30%` with no degradation banner. No live report in the 60 days to 2026-09-28 had that shape (execution `db-query-zdb25`), but a replay row does: QQQ at 2026-05-06 12:45 UTC stores `stop` 0.0, an entry zone of 0.0 to 0.0 and `confidence_score` 0.0 with no failed section, and opened from History the page drew an entry zone of `0.00` to `0.00`, a stop of `0.00`, `Trade plan unavailable due to LLM error.` as the invalidation and `Analysis unavailable due to LLM error.` under both cases, with no banner (executed 2026-10-01, execution `db-query-sv5tv`). When the strat section fails, the Strat card shows candle `1`, FTFC `0.00` and `mixed` from `_build_strat_snapshot` ([orchestrator.py:1102-1117](../../lib/agents/orchestrator.py#L1102), which also applies an unmarked `or 0.0` to `ftfc_score`).
- **House Views compares a fabricated or unrelated brief.** The brief handler answers 200 with `source: 'cloud_sql'`, `bias: 'neutral'` and `has_premarket: false` when neither table holds a row for the ticker and when a read raises, because each failed read is logged and skipped ([dashboard.py:139-140](../../platform/api/routers/dashboard.py#L139), [dashboard.py:202-203](../../platform/api/routers/dashboard.py#L202), `bias` derived at [dashboard.py:226-244](../../platform/api/routers/dashboard.py#L226)), and the card draws that as a `FLAT` brief with a verdict, `HOUSES DIVERGE` against a `long` insight (executed 2026-10-01). It compares today's brief with whichever report is open: the IWM backfill report of 2026-05-22, opened from History, read `HOUSES DIVERGE` against today's `bearish` brief (executed 2026-10-01). While the brief request is pending the card reads `Brief unavailable for IWM, insight pipeline shipping standalone.`, the copy of a 404 or an unavailable source (executed 2026-10-01 with the request held; the card is [ReportCards.tsx:504-613](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/insights/ReportCards.tsx#L504) and the pending copy [ReportCards.tsx:529-537](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/insights/ReportCards.tsx#L529), read).
- **The banner names sections and drops their causes.** A degraded report stores `failed_section_reasons` and the type declares it ([insights.ts:83-85](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/types/insights.ts#L83)), but `DegradationBanner` draws only the names ([ReportCards.tsx:467-476](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/insights/ReportCards.tsx#L467)). In production `options` and `gamma` fail together: 28 of the 157 reports of the 60 days to 2026-10-01 list both, the first at 2026-09-15 21:23 UTC and the newest, AVGO, TXN and TSM, at 2026-09-30 12:20 UTC, with the reasons `chain hard-stale: 113 trading days behind 2026-09-30 (snapshot_date=2026-04-24)` for AVGO and `no etf_options_snapshots for TXN` and `... for TSM` (executions `db-query-hw76g` and `db-query-qwwjw`). The page showed `Partial report: the following sections were unavailable: options, gamma` and none of this (executed 2026-10-01).
- **A report of any age is served as current.** The Briefing tab serves the newest live row of the ticker whatever its age: the page hands `HeaderCard` the report, its `as_of`, the cost and the latency and nothing that compares the time with now ([InsightsPage.tsx:357-362](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/InsightsPage.tsx#L357)) and the card draws the time as `toLocaleString()` ([ReportCards.tsx:85-86](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/insights/ReportCards.tsx#L85)), with no stale badge, no relative time and no comparison with the session (read). The production IWM report of 2026-04-24, served by the live route in the hermetic page, drew `4/24/2026, 1:16:21 PM` and the rest of the stack with no marker (executed 2026-10-01). At 04:06 ET on 2026-10-01 MU's newest live report was from 2026-09-24 12:11 UTC (execution `db-query-qwwjw`), a week old, which the page would serve under its time alone (read). The brief behind House Views carries a `stale_days` the card never reads ([dashboard.py:166-183](../../platform/api/routers/dashboard.py#L166), read), and the handler publishes `stale_days` 0 when either date fails to parse ([dashboard.py:179-180](../../platform/api/routers/dashboard.py#L179), read), the value of a fresh row: on the real handler with the database read replaced, a row date of `not-a-date` gave `stale_days` 0, and so did an as-of of `20261001` over a row 110 trading days old, which gave 110 with a valid as-of (executed 2026-10-01), so a parse failure would read as fresh to any future reader of the field.
- **Failed reads render as empty or as a permission problem.** History has no error branch, so a 503 reads `No history yet.` ([InsightsPage.tsx:404-417](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/InsightsPage.tsx#L404)). The Agents tab shows `Admin access required to view per-agent routing.` for any roster failure, a 503 for an admin included, and `Loading routing…` for an empty roster ([AgentsPanel.tsx:85-90](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/insights/AgentsPanel.tsx#L85)), and a failed history there reads `No prior runs for` the ticker ([AgentsPanel.tsx:121-122](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/insights/AgentsPanel.tsx#L121)). Hermetic renders on 2026-09-28 showed all three.
- **The page's sign-in block cannot show.** `DataGate` wraps every tab body and would replace it with `SignInEmptyState` (`Sign in to load data`) for a signed-out user after a gated 401 ([InsightsPage.tsx:199-248](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/InsightsPage.tsx#L199), [SignInEmptyState.tsx:12-98](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/shared/SignInEmptyState.tsx#L12), read), but in `firebase` mode `AuthGate` draws the sign-in screen in place of the whole page for a signed-out user ([AuthGate.tsx:14-30](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/auth/AuthGate.tsx#L14), read; executed 2026-10-01 with `/insights` opened signed out: no `AI Insights` heading and `GET /api/config/firebase` the only request) and in `open` and `iap` modes `useUser` reports signed in always ([useUser.ts:25-27,81](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useUser.ts#L25), read), so a signed-in user's 401 shows the shell's expired-session strip and each tab's own error form and never that block (executed 2026-10-01 in `open` mode with every gated route answering 401).
- **The Agents tab mixes sources and counts what it was sent.** `Recent runs 8 of 8` is `shown of count` with `count` the length of the rows the handler returned ([insights.py:800](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L800)), not the ticker's total: IWM holds 150 reports, 123 of them live. The `As of` tile and the `Recent runs` times are UTC, unlike the localized times of the Briefing and History tabs. 472 of the 872 stored reports, 450 of them live and the newest from 2026-05-01, carry no `per_role_cost`, so an open report that exists reads `No per-agent breakdown yet, generate an insight report for this ticker.`, and the roster beside it is today's `model_routing`, not the models the open report ran on: the 2026-04-24 report's footer names `vertex:gemini-2.0-flash` while the roster lists `gemini-3.1-flash-lite` (executed 2026-10-01, executions `db-query-m5sb8`, `db-query-hw76g` and `db-query-sv5tv`).
- **Chat.** The model is hard-coded ([insights.py:1042](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L1042)) rather than read from `model_routing`. A Vertex failure is streamed as `Gemini error:` text with HTTP 200 ([insights.py:1058-1059](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L1058), probe), which the page shows as the assistant's answer. The system prompts promise signals, backtests, GEX and playbook data ([insights.py:930-960](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L930)), and the request sends only the message, mode, ticker and six prior turns ([insights.py:1029-1037](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L1029)). The route declares no `response_model` ([insights.py:1062](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L1062)), and the snapshot types its response as an empty schema. The transcript's error bubbles go back to the model as assistant turns: after a 503, a network failure and a `Gemini error:` answer the next request's `history` carried `Error: 503 Service Unavailable`, `Error: Failed to fetch` and the Gemini text, and a round trip to another tab clears the conversation (executed on the page with the replies stubbed, 2026-10-01).
- **The Watchlist tab is empty for every signed-in user.** The ranking is scoped to the caller's own rows ([insights.py:57-67](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L57)), and `watchlists` held rows for the `default` owner only on 2026-09-28 (one owner, 16 active rows, execution `db-query-wqqwr`). A hermetic probe of `rank_tickers` gave 0 candidates for a signed-in owner and 3 for `default`, and the empty copy blames the liquidity gate or empty catalyst tables ([WatchlistPanel.tsx:164-173](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/insights/WatchlistPanel.tsx#L164)). A database outage answers 200 with an empty ranking too (probe), because every ranker read goes through the swallowing `query_to_dataframe` (`lib/agents/ranker/candidates.py:60-63`, `lib/agents/ranker/signals.py:29-35`) and the watchlist load returns an empty list on failure (`gcp/fetchers/_watchlist.py:363-380`) (filed as [stocks#1215](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1215)).
- **The ranking writes a row on every call, and the ranker's recorded runs took far longer than its comments say.** `GET /api/insights/watchlist` inserts a `ranker_runs` row per request, one with 0 candidates for a signed-in user who owns no row (executed 2026-10-01, the real handler with the insert captured). `ranker_runs` holds 112 rows from 2026-04-25 to 2026-09-30 over 110 distinct days, 109 with 16 candidates, 3 with 5 and none with 0. The newest four were written at 12:10 to 12:17 UTC, the minutes of the 08:10 ET `auto-refresh-top-n` job, whose `rank_tickers` call persists the audit row by design ([auto_refresh_top_n.py:219-224](../../gcp/auto_refresh_top_n.py#L219), read). Its 2026-09-30 execution `auto-refresh-top-n-bk6j8` was created at 12:10:02, started at 12:16:40 and completed at 12:17:35 (`gcloud run jobs executions describe`, 2026-10-01), and the newest row reads 12:17:30. The four rows recorded `duration_ms` of 10849 to 46075 for 16 candidates (executions `db-query-m5sb8` and `db-query-7vlmp`), against comments that call the ranker cheap at about 5 s ([useWatchlist.ts:11-15](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useWatchlist.ts#L11), [auto_refresh_top_n.py:8](../../gcp/auto_refresh_top_n.py#L8)). The endpoint's own latency was not measured.
- **The ranker's strat signal cannot score direction.** It compares `ftfc_direction` with `bull` and `bear` (`lib/agents/ranker/signals.py:74`), while the daily writer stores `bullish`, `bearish` or `mixed` (`lib/strat.py:332-336`), the only values a read-only query found in the last 30 days (execution `db-query-7fz78`). A probe scored a fully aligned `bullish` row 0.67 against 1.0 for `bull`, and the signal clamps a bearish row's negative `ftfc_score` to zero (`lib/agents/ranker/signals.py:73`). The ranker test feeds `bull` (`tests/agents/test_ranker.py:101`). The signal reads the newest row, which after 08:20 ET is the placeholder `fetch-premarket-refresh` inserts for the tickers it covers, and its NULL strat columns score as measured zeros (`lib/agents/ranker/signals.py:68-70`, probe) (filed as [stocks#1216](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1216)).
- **A failed search reads as no matches.** The search route answers 200 with `results: []` when the AlphaVantage SYMBOL_SEARCH call raises and when the vendor answers a rate-limit notice (the real handler with the vendor call replaced, 2026-10-01), and a search the server answered 500 was requested twice and left the panel with its input and no text (hermetic). `ticker_info`, the cache the add path writes, holds no row in production, in step with `watchlists` holding no row with source `ui` (execution `db-query-m5sb8`).
- No control removes a watchlist ticker: `useRemoveFromWatchlist` ([useTickerSearch.ts:137-147](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useTickerSearch.ts#L137)) has no caller. The add route's read-back of the list turns any error into an empty list ([insights.py:639-643](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L639)), unmarked.
- `insight-pipeline` carries the value of secret `admin-token` as a plain environment variable, `ADMIN_TOKEN` (`gcp/deploy.sh:575-576`), which the live job showed as a plain value on 2026-09-28 (tracked as [stocks#850](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/850)).
- Sources that disagree with the code: 04-BACKEND-API.md gives `via lib/` for the report, history, by-id, run and chat routes, which run their own SQL on the connection from `lib/agents/model_routing.py` or call Vertex AI directly. 05-INFRASTRUCTURE.md gives `insight-pipeline` 2Gi, `deploy_insight_pipeline` sets 8Gi and 2 CPU, and the live job runs 4Gi. UI-SCREENS.md listed only `/api/insights/chat` among the page's calls until the record's API calls line was corrected on 2026-10-01 to the eleven routes of the Chain. The design seed says the Watchlist tab shows quotes and that chat talks with the report: the tab shows the ranker's ranking, and chat sends no report.
- `AUDIT-2026-05-13` markers on this page's paths: `lib/agents/summarizers.py:285` (a missing `price_vs_ema20` read as ranging), `lib/agents/trade_planner.py:874` (`or 0.0` on `atr`) and `:906` (`or 0.0` on `ftfc_score`). None sits in the router. Every route in the rows declares a `response_model` except the chat.
- No test was found for INSIGHTS-11 or INSIGHTS-14, on main or on this branch, and none on main exercises the page side of INSIGHTS-02, INSIGHTS-03, INSIGHTS-04, INSIGHTS-05, INSIGHTS-06, INSIGHTS-09, INSIGHTS-10 or INSIGHTS-13. [test_routers_insights_admin.py](../../tests/lib/test_routers_insights_admin.py) skips in the Backtest Pipeline's Run Tests step, which sets no `DB_HOST` (16 skipped, executed). Run on 2026-10-01 as a scratch copy with two lines changed (the `PROJECT_ROOT` path, and the `CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS vector` line removed) against a throwaway Postgres 16 with the module's own table definitions, 6 of its 16 tests failed, all on the module's own definitions: its four report and history tests with `column "run_kind" does not exist` because its table has no `run_kind`, its refresh test with `TypeError` because the fake `_sync_run` takes two arguments where the handler passes three, and `test_admin_update_route`, which PUTs provider `anthropic` and expects 200 although the API process registers only `vertex` ([anthropic_adapter.py:145](../../lib/agents/anthropic_adapter.py#L145) registers its adapter only when that module is imported with `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` set, and no Python source under `lib`, `gcp`, `platform` or `scripts` imports it). After `import api.main`, `available_providers()` read `['vertex']` with and without the key, and the PUT answered 400 `Provider 'anthropic' has no registered adapter`; the test passes only in a session that has collected `tests/agents/test_agent_anthropic_adapter.py` with `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` exported (executed; the [follow-up comment](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5929780117) corrects the first V comment, which read this failure as a sandbox limit). [test_agent_model_routing.py](../../tests/agents/test_agent_model_routing.py) has the same dependency, on two adapters, in `test_set_route_updates_single_role` (lines 93-102, 10 skipped in the module without `DB_HOST`, executed): it sets provider `anthropic` and, to restore, provider `vertex`, and registers neither. On the same throwaway Postgres 16 it failed alone with `Provider 'anthropic' has no registered adapter`, key set or not, failed with the Anthropic adapter's test module collected first with `Provider 'vertex' has no registered adapter` at the restore, and passed (23 passed) only with both adapter test modules collected first and the key exported (executed 2026-10-01). A comment in [test_production_writers.py](../../tests/meta/test_production_writers.py) still says the on-demand endpoint stamps `'live'`, while the handler stamps `replay` when a cutoff is set ([insights.py:455](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L455)).
- **Te for INSIGHTS-04, INSIGHTS-06, INSIGHTS-08, INSIGHTS-10 and INSIGHTS-13 waits for a CI run that includes the branch's tests.** Three tests were added on this branch in solyra commit 1aa05e2 ([insights.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/insights/insights.spec.ts)) and the first was renamed in solyra commit 2bb4f8a: `a degraded report shows the partial-report banner and its section name, a complete one shows none` (INSIGHTS-06: with the one-section fixture `failed_sections: ['judge']`, the banner sentence, the name `judge` and the `IWM` heading on the page and, after a reload on the complete fixture, no `Partial report` text; not the position of the strip, the join of several names or that the stored reasons are not drawn), `a watchlist row's Generate report runs live even while a cutoff is set` (INSIGHTS-04 and INSIGHTS-08: the first request to the row's ticker's refresh route has that path and an empty query string, none reached the IWM refresh route (the ticker active before the click, and the one route the helper's `onRefresh` recorder watches, [insights.ts:74-75](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/helpers/fixtures/insights.ts#L74)), text matching `/no report yet/i` shows, and the cutoff input holds its value with the `Replay` button still shown; not the request's method) and `a failed report, ranking or chat request says so on the page with its status` (INSIGHTS-13, with the chat's 503 text of INSIGHTS-10: `Failed to load report: insights 503` with the `AI Insights` heading and the cutoff input still on screen, `Failed to load watchlist: watchlist 503`, and, after a chat send answered 503, the message text and text beginning `Error: 503`, each found by text on the page with no order or place asserted; it clicks the Watchlist and Chat tab buttons to reach the other two forms and asserts nothing about the other tabs, the `Re-analyze` button or the filter bar). Each failed when the behaviour it asserts was removed from a scratch copy of the product code (the banner never drawn, drawn for an empty list or drawn without the name; the cutoff sent from a row, the row's run leaving the Watchlist tab or clearing the cutoff; the report error without its message or status, the ranking error branch removed, the chat bubble without its status or not appended) and the spec file's 11 tests passed on the page as it is on 2026-10-01 (12 passed in all, the twelfth being the `[warmup]` test of [routes.warmup.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/routes.warmup.ts) that the run adds), again after the rename. None asserts a loading, stale or permission row, and the second test checks the empty copy only as the sign that the page reached the new ticker's Briefing tab.
- **Rows left unticked at V and why, and what the ticks do not cover.** INSIGHTS-04 is the ranking, scoped to the caller's own `watchlists` rows, of which production holds none for a signed-in user, so the rows the page needs do not exist; INSIGHTS-05 and INSIGHTS-10 stream a vendor-live Gemini reply behind a gated route; INSIGHTS-07 and INSIGHTS-08 write `insight_runs` and `insight_reports` and call Vertex AI (the cutoff's parsing and the branch choice were executed on the real handler with the writes replaced); INSIGHTS-09 writes `watchlists` and calls AlphaVantage and FinViz; and INSIGHTS-11 to INSIGHTS-14 are client-side states that production does not show without a browser and a token ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5928962118)). INSIGHTS-01, INSIGHTS-02, INSIGHTS-03 and INSIGHTS-06 are ticked on the freshness of `insight_reports`, `model_routing`, `premarket_analysis` and `market_data_daily` and on the real handlers answering on those rows, and INSIGHTS-15 on the 401 of the eleven gated routes; the brief's live overlay (a vendor call), the admin roster as an admin and how the page presents a 401 were not exercised against staging, only on the real handlers and the hermetic page. The five ticks also carry display defects that V does not cover. The display findings above are the record of them, and the list here is not complete; among them are, for INSIGHTS-01, the House Views card that compares a flat brief the handler fabricates, or whichever report is open, and reads unavailable while its request is pending, the zero plan of a trader fallback drawn as `0.00` with no banner, Supporting Signals and Similar Past Trades that are empty on every report of the last 10 days and a report of any age served as current under its time alone; for INSIGHTS-02, the `8 of 8` that counts the rows returned, the UTC times beside local ones, the `No per-agent breakdown yet` of 472 stored reports and a roster that is today's routing and not the models the open report ran on; for INSIGHTS-03, replay and backfill rows drawn like live ones and a failed history read drawn as `No history yet.`; for INSIGHTS-06, the banner that draws the section names and drops their reasons and the failed bull, bear or trader call that adds no name; and, for INSIGHTS-15, the permission block that cannot show and the one admin message for every roster failure.
