### Backend notes
- Pipeline order feeding this page, earliest first (America/New_York): `fetch-news-sentiment-earnings` 06:00 Mon-Fri → `fetch-economic-events`, `fetch-insider-transactions` and the first `fetch-sec-filings` run 07:00 Mon-Fri → `fetch-news-sentiment` hourly from 08:00 and `fetch-news-sentiment-topics` hourly from 08:05, both to 17:00 or 17:05 Mon-Fri → `fetch-sec-filings` again at 10:00, 13:00 and 17:00 → `fetch-earnings-calendar` 19:00 Mon-Fri and Sun. All three news jobs run `gcp/fetchers/fetch_news_sentiment.py` and differ only in environment settings (`NEWS_TOPICS` for the topics job, `EARNINGS_WINDOW_DAYS` for the earnings job). All eight triggers the Data cells name exist, are ENABLED, run in America/New_York with the schedules above, and the latest execution of each scheduled job had succeeded when read (`gcloud scheduler jobs describe` and `gcloud run jobs executions list`, 2026-10-01, V evidence); `backfill-ticker` has no trigger and last ran 2026-05-04, and `gcp/fetchers/fetch_rss_news.py` has neither a job nor a trigger.
- Freshness the page assumes: the events query holds for five minutes and the types for an hour ([CatalystsPage.tsx:172](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L172), [CatalystsPage.tsx:184](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L184)), with no polling. A range fetched under five minutes ago is served from the cache (`Today` back to it sends no request, executed 2026-10-01) and only `Refresh` refetches it, while a new range blanks the page until its answer arrives. The handler caches none of its SQL reads, and the news window is the 48 hours before each request ([catalysts.py:87](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L87)).
- How the handler tiers and orders events decides what reaches Hot Now: every earnings row is `High` ([catalysts.py:475](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L475)), which makes every reporter due today or tomorrow a candidate for its ten slots, an insider cluster is always `Medium` ([catalysts.py:519](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L519)) and never one, and the page's stable sort by impact and then date keeps the handler's append order within a date, news first ([catalysts.py:353-570](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L353)). The real handler on the production rows of 2026-10-01 filled the ten slots with news rows (see the Gaps).
- `GET /api/catalysts/events` also feeds DASHBOARD-06 and DASHBOARD-09, whose cells name four tables and one news writer. The rows here record the fifth read, `sec_filings` ([catalysts.py:526-534](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L526)), and every writer of each table.
- The earnings calendar is a week long: `fetch-earnings-calendar` runs `--source all --days 30` and then keeps only `today minus 1` to `today plus 7` ([fetch_earnings_calendar.py:1534-1549](../../scripts/fetch_earnings_calendar.py#L1534)), so after the 2026-09-30 19:00 ET run the newest `earnings_date` was 2026-10-07 (V evidence), while this page's default range ends 14 days ahead.
- Producer tests: `fetch-economic-events` [test_fetch_economic_events.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_economic_events.py) · `fetch-earnings-calendar` [test_fetch_earnings_calendar_persist.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_earnings_calendar_persist.py), [test_fetch_earnings_calendar_yahoo.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_earnings_calendar_yahoo.py) · the news jobs [test_fetch_news_sentiment_args.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_news_sentiment_args.py), [test_fetch_news_sentiment_explode.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_news_sentiment_explode.py), [test_fetch_news_sentiment_incremental.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_news_sentiment_incremental.py) · `fetch-sec-filings` [test_sec_filings_retry.py](../../tests/gcp/test_sec_filings_retry.py) · news, SEC and insider parsing [test_phase2_fetchers.py](../../tests/gcp/test_phase2_fetchers.py).

### Gaps
- [#863](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/863) (open) is this page's blocking issue in UI-SCREENS.md. It concerns the weekly Discord post of `earnings_options_strategy_winners` and the `signal_metrics` classification. Neither table is on this page's path, which reads the five tables in CATALYSTS-04.
- **A database outage renders as a quiet range.** Each of the five reads runs through the swallowing `query_to_dataframe` (`gcp/database.py:239-241`) inside its own `except` ([catalysts.py:358-362](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L358) and its four siblings), so an outage answers 200 with no events and `source: Benzinga` (hermetic probe, 2026-09-28, and again on 2026-10-01 with the real handler and every read raising or returning an empty frame), and the page draws that as the empty page of CATALYSTS-11, with nothing to say that a read failed. [test_route_coverage.py](../../tests/api/test_route_coverage.py) expects that 200.
- **Benzinga is named without Benzinga data, and the database sources are miscounted.** Neither service has a `BENZINGA_API_KEY` variable (read 2026-09-28 and 2026-10-01), and the image copies only `lib/`, `gcp/`, `scripts/` and `platform/api/` (`platform/Dockerfile:40-45`), so the cached Benzinga file is absent and requests call `_fetch_live_events`, which returns nothing without a key ([catalysts.py:138-140](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L138)). The response still names Benzinga, as the `AUDIT-2026-09-07` marker records ([catalysts.py:309-318](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L309)), and the page prints `Benzinga` when `source` is missing too ([CatalystsPage.tsx:488](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L488)): a hermetic render of a failed request showed `0 events` followed by `Benzinga` in the header. The Refresh button refetches with `refresh` false ([CatalystsPage.tsx:406](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L406)), so the page never asks for a Benzinga refresh. That finding is not filed: [stocks#1223](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1223) covers only the label that names Benzinga without a key, in the response and in the header. The label `Benzinga + DB (news + sec, <n>)` counts every database event and names two of the five database sources (news and sec; economic, earnings and insider are not named): on 2026-10-01 it read `news + sec, 1565` over 897 earnings rows, 617 news rows, 35 economic events and 16 8-Ks, four of the five reads having contributed (executed through the real handler on the production rows).
- **News ignores the range and empties over weekends.** The range filters the Benzinga list before the database events are merged ([catalysts.py:276-289](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L276)), and the news query is bounded only by the 48 hours before the request ([catalysts.py:101-121](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L101)). A hermetic probe with an August range returned a 2026-09-25 article. The news jobs run Mon-Fri, and at 04:48 ET on Monday 2026-09-28 no row fell inside the window, the newest `published_ts` being 2026-09-25 18:26 UTC (execution `db-query-wqqwr`). News rows are dated `published_ts::date` ([catalysts.py:111](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L111)) in the database session's UTC zone, which the Signals area measured (area 08), so an article after 20:00 EDT is filed under the next day. Executed on 2026-10-01 (the real handler on the production rows): the range 2026-09-18 to 2026-09-25 returned the same 617 news events as the default range, dated 09-29 to 10-01, the page drew them after the range's last day, and its header counted them (counted in the response: `1695 events 1246H / 284M / 165L`, of which 617 events are news rows dated outside the range); and 19 of the 54 news rows dated 2026-10-01 were published between 20:20 ET and midnight on 09-30 (execution `db-query-tzdxz`), so they sit under a date that had not begun in ET, and Hot Now, which compares dates with the ET day, counts them as today's.
- **A NULL reads as a value, and the `or 0` guards do not do what they say.** `or 0` on financial fields, unmarked: news relevance and sentiment ([catalysts.py:394-395](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L394)), which also set the impact tier, and an insider cluster's count and value ([catalysts.py:508-509](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L508)). What a NULL becomes depends on the column the read returns (executed through the real handler and `pg8000` on a throwaway Postgres 16): when some row of the column holds a number, pandas makes it float64, a NULL reaches `or 0` as NaN, `NaN or 0` stays NaN and the guard never fires, so a NULL `sentiment_score` is sent as `null` and tiered `Low` (NaN fails both thresholds), an insider cluster whose values are all NULL reads `~$nanM` and one whose values are 0 reads `~$0.0M`; when every row of the column is NULL, pandas makes it object dtype and `None or 0` is 0, so the row is sent with `sentiment_score` 0.0 and tiered `Low`, an all-NULL value column reads `~$0.0M` and an all-NULL estimate adds no `est` text. The production reads are of the first kind: on the production clusters of 2026-09-18 to 2026-09-25 two read `~$0.0M` and one `~$nanM`, and 194 of the 268 insider rows of the last 30 days have a NULL (87) or zero (107) `transaction_value`, execution `db-query-tzdxz`. The reading carried from the Dashboard task, that a NULL sentiment becomes 0.0 in the handler, holds only for the all-NULL case: with any scored row in the read, as in the production read of 2026-10-01 (638 rows, none NULL), a NULL reaches the page as `null`, and the zero that the Dashboard card shows is its own default ([DashboardPage.tsx:924-925](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/DashboardPage.tsx#L924)). No NULL `sentiment_score` or `relevance_score` was found in the last 30 days (14,301 rows, execution `db-query-tzdxz`; execution `db-query-8lfh7` before it).
- **Earnings titles read `est nan`.** The title formats `eps_estimate` unless it is None or empty ([catalysts.py:467-474](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L467)), and a NULL double reaches it as NaN, so it reads `est nan`: 785 of the 897 earnings rows of the default range on 2026-10-01, 20 of today's 30 (executed on a throwaway Postgres 16 and on the production rows), because 840 of the 1,022 `earnings_calendar` rows in the range have no estimate (yahoo 726 of 816, earnings_whispers 34 of 34, unusual_whales 22 of 69, alphavantage 58 of 103; execution `db-query-tzdxz`). The dedupe keeps the first row of a ticker and day from a read with no `ORDER BY`, so a row without an estimate can hide another source's (6 of the 25 ticker-days that mix a NULL and a non-NULL estimate in the executed read), and 323 titles read `unknown` as the time.
- **Hot Now fills with news before scheduled events.** Every earnings row is `High` ([catalysts.py:475](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L475)) and the page's stable sort keeps the handler's append order, news first ([catalysts.py:353-570](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L353)): on 2026-10-01 the panel's ten slots all went to news rows dated today while 61 events dated today or tomorrow were High, among them today's 30 earnings reporters and tomorrow's four High economic events, and 897 of the answer's 1,113 High events were earnings rows. The panel does not say that it stops at ten (executed, the real handler on the production rows).
- **The insider read cannot match the page's window, and its rows read badly.** The default range, today minus 3 to today plus 14, matched no cluster on 2026-10-01: the newest `transaction_date` is 2026-09-23 although the table was ingested at 07:03 ET that day (execution `db-query-256dl`), so no insider row and no `INSIDER_*` chip shows unless the range is moved back (executed: three clusters for 2026-09-18 to 2026-09-25). The comment above the read promises a 3-day rolling window ([catalysts.py:479-481](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L479)) while its SQL groups by one transaction day and ticker and side ([catalysts.py:482-493](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L482)), so three insiders buying over three days are not a cluster (read). `TYPE_CONFIG` has no `INSIDER_BUY` or `INSIDER_SELL` ([CatalystsPage.tsx:120-145](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L120)), so those badges and chips show the raw type ([CatalystsPage.tsx:212](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L212), [CatalystsPage.tsx:599](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L599)) and a selected chip has white text on no background (executed in the light theme).
- **The earnings calendar ends a week ahead of the default range.** The default range ends 14 days ahead and `earnings_calendar` holds rows only to the fetch date plus 7 (see the Backend notes): after the 2026-09-30 run the newest `earnings_date` was 2026-10-07 and the range's second week, 2026-10-08 to 2026-10-15, held 11 events, all economic (executed).
- **The picker's label is a day early west of New York.** [DateRangePicker.tsx:10-15](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/shared/DateRangePicker.tsx#L10) turns a `CalendarDate` into midnight Eastern and formats it in the browser's own zone: for the request `date_from=2026-09-28&date_to=2026-10-15` a Los Angeles browser showed `Sep 27` and `Oct 14` in the trigger and in the dialog's header, while the calendar's selected cells stayed on 09-28 and 10-15 (executed with `timezoneId: 'America/Los_Angeles'`).
- **A type filter outlives its data, and the controls have no state for assistive technology.** A selected type chip stays selected when the next range lacks that type: no chip is highlighted, `All Types` is not, and the timeline is empty under a header that still counts 1,565 events (executed). `Min impact` and the chips carry no `aria-pressed`, the `View` button stays at opacity 0 under keyboard focus (executed), and the loading spinner has no accessible name.
- **A new range blanks the page, and the whole answer is drawn at once.** The page keeps no earlier answer while a new range loads (executed: Hot Now, the chips and the rows vanish under one spinner), then draws every returned event with no paging: 1,565 rows on 2026-10-01, 1,403 of them dated before today and so ahead of today's card, and a filter click took 0.9 s to 1.6 s to redraw in the hermetic page (sandbox figures).
- **No empty state and a fixed error text.** A range with no events shows only `0 events` and the filter bars (UI-SCREENS.md lists empty as absent). Any failed request reads `Failed to load catalysts: Failed to fetch catalysts`, because the hook throws one message whatever the status ([CatalystsPage.tsx:169](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L169)). Executed 2026-10-01 with the real handler's empty answer, a 500 and a 401: the empty answer draws the zero header, the controls and the Wall Street Horizon card and nothing else, and a filter combination that excludes every row leaves an empty timeline under a header that still counts every event, with no message.
- Expanding an event un-truncates its title and nothing else ([CatalystsPage.tsx:278-292](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L278)). The URL, `actual`, `forecast`, `previous`, the insider value and the 8-K items the handler sends are typed on the page ([CatalystsPage.tsx:41-57](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L41)) and never rendered: no row links to its article or filing (executed: the page's only anchors are the three navigation links and the Wall Street Horizon link), and an economic row shows no actual, forecast or previous. The types answer's `upgrade_note`, `benzinga_types` and each type's colour and icon are likewise typed and never drawn ([CatalystsPage.tsx:91-95](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L91)), and the Wall Street Horizon card's wording and its $49-149/mo price are written in the page ([CatalystsPage.tsx:363-365](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L363)), not read from the answer and not checked against the vendor.
- `eventTitle` falls back to `${e.ticker} ${e.catalyst_type}` ([CatalystsPage.tsx:79-81](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L79)), so an event with no title, event, ticker or type draws `undefined undefined` as its title (`null null` when they are null), although the page's own comment says every field is optional and nothing beyond the envelope is guaranteed ([CatalystsPage.tsx:22-24](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L22)); executed in the hermetic page on 2026-10-01. The five database reads set a ticker and a type on every event, so the deployed handler does not reach it today.
- **No source has an age on the page.** The response carries no write time or as-of for any source and the header's `source` string is the only provenance, so on 2026-10-01 a reader could not see that the calendar's last run was the previous evening's and ends a week ahead, that the newest insider transaction is dated 2026-09-23, or that news published after 20:00 ET carries the next day's date (V evidence).
- **The permission block cannot show.** `DataGate` replaces the body only when a gated call has answered 401 and the user is signed out, which `firebase` mode prevents (`AuthGate` shows the sign-in screen first, executed) and `open` and `iap` modes cannot produce (`useUser` reports signed in always); a signed-in user's 401 shows the shell's expired-session strip and this page's error box instead (executed, CATALYSTS-14 in UI-SCREENS.md).
- Secrets copied into plain environment variables on this page's producers: `fetch-earnings-calendar` carries secrets `ew-user` and `ew-pass` as `EW_USER` and `EW_PASS` (`gcp/deploy.sh:2767-2769`), and `fetch-sec-filings` carries `sec-user-agent` as `SEC_USER_AGENT` (`gcp/deploy.sh:2923-2924`). The live jobs showed all three as plain values, not secret references, on 2026-09-28 (tracked as [stocks#850](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/850)).
- Sources that disagree with the code: 04-BACKEND-API.md's Tables touched for `/api/catalysts/events` also omits `sec_filings` (its `news_sentiment` omission is in Dashboard's Gaps, area 04), and it gives `via lib/` for `/api/catalysts/types`, which returns two dictionaries defined in the router ([catalysts.py:50-78](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L50)). 05-INFRASTRUCTURE.md lists `fetch-news-sentiment` and `fetch-news-sentiment-topics` as manual, and `gcp/deploy.sh` schedules both through `if _schedule_verified` entries (`news-sentiment-hourly`, `news-topics-hourly`, `gcp/deploy.sh:4985-4998`). The design seed puts `fetch-insider-transactions` under SEC EDGAR, and the fetcher calls AlphaVantage INSIDER_TRANSACTIONS (`gcp/fetchers/fetch_insider_transactions.py:41`, `:57`). It gives the earnings calendar three sources where `--source all` runs four, Yahoo included (`scripts/fetch_earnings_calendar.py:1479-1511`). The seed says an expanded event shows details, and UI-SCREENS.md and the seed both list a stale state the page does not have (CATALYSTS-13). UI-SCREENS.md also counted 625 lines for a 635-line component, named the type chips as a consumer of `/api/catalysts/types` (they draw nothing from it), listed every typed event field as read, called its two stores `per session` (both persist in localStorage) and showed the stale state as present; corrected with this area.
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits in `platform/api/routers/catalysts.py`, and both routes declare a `response_model`.
- **What the tests assert.** No page test asserts CATALYSTS-03, CATALYSTS-05, CATALYSTS-08, CATALYSTS-10, CATALYSTS-11, CATALYSTS-12 or CATALYSTS-13, and none asserts the handler's mapping of the five reads: [test_catalysts_news_filter.py](../../tests/api/test_catalysts_news_filter.py) covers `_news_sql` and the topic list, [test_route_coverage.py](../../tests/api/test_route_coverage.py) pins the route's 200 against a dead backend, and the tests in [test_threadpool_races.py](../../tests/api/test_threadpool_races.py) replace `_db_catalyst_events`. [test_earnings_router.py](../../tests/api/test_earnings_router.py) tests `/api/earnings/*`, which no solyra source calls, so it covers no row of this area. The existing page tests assert less than their titles say: `shows filter chips` looks only for text matching `earnings`, `renders impact tier counters in header` matches the shape of the counts, `renders Hot Now panel for today/tomorrow high-impact events` the header and a headline that the timeline also holds, and `clicking a ticker navigates to /insights with that ticker active` only the URL. Mutations of a scratch copy of the page on 2026-10-01 (one product line each, with the nine tests of the Catalysts spec and the two Catalysts cases of popover-fit.spec.ts run) failed a test for removing Hot Now, removing the timeline, ignoring `High` in Min impact, flipping the sentiment arrows and pointing the ticker click at another route, and for letting Hot Now's date window slip (`lists upcoming events` failed by accident, on a headline that then appeared twice); nothing failed for removing the chips, the Wall Street Horizon card, the error box or the spinner, ignoring the type filter or the `Medium` setting, admitting Medium events to Hot Now, counting High events as zero, making the expand toggle, the range change or `Today` do nothing, dropping `setTicker`, drawing the cards newest first or fixing the `source` string.
- Three tests were added on this branch in solyra commit 7690974 ([catalysts.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/catalysts/catalysts.spec.ts)): `OK requests the picked range, Cancel discards it and Today restores the default` (CATALYSTS-06), `an event title toggles its own expanded state on click` (CATALYSTS-08) and `opening an insight report makes that ticker active on /insights, and a MACRO row has no link` (CATALYSTS-09). Mutations of a scratch copy of the page on 2026-10-01, one product line each, each failed only the test that asserts it and left the other two new tests and the warmup passing: the range test when the picker's change handler stopped setting the dates, when `Today` did nothing, when `Cancel` applied the draft, when the request sent `date_from` and `date_to` swapped and when `OK` passed the old range; the expand test when the toggle did nothing, when the expanded state was keyed by title so that both copies of an event opened together, when `aria-expanded` was removed and when the `title` attribute stayed constant; the report test when `setTicker` was dropped, when the click set another ticker, when it navigated to `/dashboard` and when a MACRO row got a button. The report test ends quiet because every request the Insights page makes for AVGO is mocked (the AVGO report route also matches the history, and `mockCommon` the brief); with the active ticker not AVGO, as in the mutation that drops `setTicker`, the page asks for the report and history of IWM, which match no registered route, reach the dev server's proxy and log `connect ECONNREFUSED 127.0.0.1:8000` for each of their two attempts whether the test passes or fails, and the existing ticker click test is quiet only because it ends at the URL match, before the Insights page asks for anything (kept open for two seconds it logs the same lines for the AVGO report and history), all executed in the scratch copy on 2026-10-01. Those three rows stay unticked at Te: each waits for a CI run that includes the branch's tests.
- **Rows left unticked at V and why, and what the ticks do not cover.** CATALYSTS-05 is a static answer behind a gated route (the card was rendered from the real handler's answer, the vendor link answered 200 and the price copy is not checked), CATALYSTS-06 to CATALYSTS-09 are actions on rows a gated request returns, and CATALYSTS-10 to CATALYSTS-13 are client-side states that production does not show without a browser and a token ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5932424871)). CATALYSTS-01 to CATALYSTS-04 are ticked on the freshness of `news_sentiment`, `economic_events`, `earnings_calendar`, `insider_transactions` and `sec_filings` and on the real handler answering on those rows, and CATALYSTS-14 on the 401 of both gated routes on staging; the events request was not called with a session, so its latency and the page as a signed-in user sees it were not observed, only the handler on production rows and the hermetic page, and the 401 is staging's (`solyra-api-prod` answers 302 from its IAP front door). The four data ticks also carry display defects that V does not cover. The display findings above are the record of them, and the list here is not complete; among them are, for CATALYSTS-01, the ten slots that went to news rows while 61 events qualified and the evening news dated as the next day; for CATALYSTS-02, the header that counts the news dated outside the range and the earnings that make 897 of 1,113 High events; for CATALYSTS-03, the raw `INSIDER_*` chip with a white selected state and a selection that outlives its data; for CATALYSTS-04, the `est nan` in 785 of 897 earnings titles, the `~$0.0M` and `~$nanM` insider titles, the three cards before today's, the 1,565 rows drawn at once, the news that ignores the range, the insider read that matched no cluster and the earnings that stop a week ahead of the range; and, for CATALYSTS-14, the permission block that cannot show.

### Backend notes
- Pipeline order feeding this page (America/New_York): `phase6-playbook` 04:30 Mon-Fri writes `playbook_cards` (`scripts/analysis/phase6_playbook.py --write-db`, [phase6_playbook.py:990](../../scripts/analysis/phase6_playbook.py#L990)) and also saves phase 6 markdown into the running container's own `reports/` directory (`scripts/analysis/shared_utils.py:328-334`), which nothing uploads. The trigger `phase6-playbook-daily` is ENABLED (`30 4 * * 1-5`, America/New_York) and the job's latest execution, created 2026-10-01 08:30:01 UTC and completed 08:42:21 UTC, succeeded ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5934848543)). During the session the conditions evaluate against the live chain in PLAYBOOK-05, whose producers are area 05's.
- Freshness the page assumes: the card set holds for an hour and refetches every 15 minutes ([PlaybookPage.tsx:64-67](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L64)), the server caches it for an hour ([playbook.py:98-99](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L98)) and re-applies the seven-day limit ([playbook.py:82](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L82)) on every hit. A read-only query on 2026-09-28 found the newest `analysis_date` at 2026-09-28 for IWM, QQQ and SPY, 216 rows each (execution `db-query-jq7t2`), and on 2026-10-01 at 2026-10-01, 21 sets of 12 cards and 252 rows each, with a set for each of the 11 weekdays from 2026-09-17 (execution `db-query-vvlbg`); the 2026-10-01 sets were written at 08:33:50 (IWM), 08:38:17 (SPY) and 08:42:18 UTC (QQQ), which is 04:33 to 04:42 ET. The age the page prints is the server's `age_days`, counted against the UTC date (see the Gaps).
- `GET /api/playbook/{ticker}` also feeds DASHBOARD-02, described the same way here.
- Which side computes what: the evaluator is inline in the router ([playbook.py:582](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L582), thresholds at [playbook.py:510-513](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L510)) and the indicators come from `lib/indicators.py` through `POST /api/live/indicators`, so the page posts a snapshot and counts the answers, as CLAUDE.md Rule 5 asks; the exception is the opening range, which the page derives itself (see the Gaps).
- The reads behind the live fill: `GET /api/live/avg-volume/{ticker}` averages the 20 newest `market_data_daily` rows that carry a volume ([live.py:404-424](../../platform/api/routers/live.py#L404)) and answers from AlphaVantage TIME_SERIES_DAILY when the table gives under 5 rows or the read fails ([live.py:425-477](../../platform/api/routers/live.py#L425)). On 2026-10-01 the newest row of each ticker was the premarket placeholder (NULL open, high, low, close and volume, written 12:21 UTC), which the query skips, so IWM's average, 23,080,481.55, is over 2026-09-02 to 2026-09-30, the newest session written at 03:00:44 UTC by the 23:00 ET `fetch-market-data` run ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5934848543)). `playbook_cards` holds IWM, QQQ and SPY only.
- Producer tests: `phase6-playbook` [test_phase6_playbook.py](../../tests/scripts/test_phase6_playbook.py), [test_phase6_playbook_scheduler.py](../../tests/gcp/test_phase6_playbook_scheduler.py).

### Gaps
- UI-SCREENS.md still gives this page Status Broken and cites [#861](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/861) for it, but that issue was closed on 2026-09-07 by PR #1005, `playbook_cards` is current (see Backend notes), and the real handlers answered with all 12 cards of the 2026-10-01 production set of each of IWM, SPY and QQQ (executed, [V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5934848543)); the page drew IWM's set in that run (section d of the comment) and, for SPY, its heading and first card in a scratch run of the PLAYBOOK-04 case (executed, and not in the comment).
- **The empty state cannot appear, and its text names the wrong script.** A ticker without rows gets a 404 ([playbook.py:342-348](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L342)), which the page shows as the amber error: the real handler's answer for `AAPL` drew `Playbook unavailable for AAPL: No playbook_cards rows for 'AAPL'. Run the phase6-playbook Cloud Run job (--write-db). (HTTP 404)`. The empty copy ([PlaybookPage.tsx:351-356](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L351)) needs a 200 with no cards, which only the spec's mock sends ([playbook.spec.ts:66-73](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/playbook/playbook.spec.ts#L66)), and it tells the reader to run `scripts/run_pipeline.py`, the backtest pipeline, whose steps write no `playbook_cards` row ([run_pipeline.py:176-235](../../scripts/run_pipeline.py#L176)); the writer is `scripts/analysis/phase6_playbook.py --write-db`, the job the 404's own text names.
- **A failed live read looks like a closed market, and a failed evaluation looks like missing data.** The page reads only `data` from the history, quote and indicators hooks and nothing of the batch query's error ([PlaybookPage.tsx:268-284](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L268), [PlaybookPage.tsx:313-314](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L313), [PlaybookPage.tsx:363](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L363)), so with the status, the history or the indicators call answering 500 the header reads `No live data, evaluation paused`, the line a closed market shows, and with the evaluation answering 500 it reads `Cards light up as live market conditions are met` over cards that read `0/N conditions met · N subjective` and `no data` on every row, with no box, strip or message that a read failed (executed with the status, the indicators and the evaluation calls answering 500, the history case read from `buildSnapshot`, which returns null without bars; the page's error box covers the card request only).
- **The live fill reverts to its first states on every change of the quote's price or volume and on every new minute bar.** The indicators query is keyed on the bar count and the last bar's time ([useLiveIndicators.ts:30-31](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useLiveIndicators.ts#L30)) as well as on the quote's price and volume and the average volume ([useLiveIndicators.ts:28-35](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useLiveIndicators.ts#L28)) and the batch query on the snapshot's signature ([usePlaybookEvaluation.ts:57](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/usePlaybookEvaluation.ts#L57), [usePlaybookEvaluation.ts:85-114](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/usePlaybookEvaluation.ts#L85)); neither sets `placeholderData`, nothing in `src/` does, and the client sets no default ([App.tsx:30-37](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/App.tsx#L30)), so `data` is undefined under each new key: the snapshot is null again ([PlaybookPage.tsx:286-296](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L286), [PlaybookPage.tsx:303](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L303)) until the indicators answer, and then the results map is empty ([PlaybookPage.tsx:314](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L314), [PlaybookPage.tsx:363](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L363)) until the evaluation answers. Executed on the unmodified page, in a scratch copy, with a quote that returns a new price and volume on every call and the indicators and the evaluation answered after 600 ms (mocked): the first fill read, at 1.0, 1.4 and 2.0 s, `No live data, evaluation paused` with `3 conditions (no live data)`, then `Cards light up as live market conditions are met` with `0/3 conditions met · 3 subjective` and `0%`, then `2/3 conditions met` and `67%`, and the same three states came again at 15.8, 16.4 and 17.0 s and at 30.8, 31.4 and 32.0 s, when the quote refetched. The quote refetches every 15 seconds ([useLiveQuote.ts:31](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useLiveQuote.ts#L31)) and the history every 60 ([useLiveHistory.ts:22](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useLiveHistory.ts#L22)), and a refetch that returns a window with a new last bar changes the key while the quote does not: executed in the same scratch copy with a quote that answered the same price and volume on all five of its calls (at 1.1, 16.1, 31.1, 46.1 and 61.1 s) and a history whose 100-bar window slid by one bar on its refetch at 61.1 s, the header line and the first card's progress read the three states at 1.3, 1.8 and 2.4 s, did not change between 2.4 and 61.2 s, and read the three states again at 61.2, 61.8 and 62.4 s (the bar count stayed 100 and the last bar's time changed). A page watched during the session therefore falls back to the paused line and to `0/N` and fills again each time the price or volume moves or a new minute bar arrives; the real latencies of the two calls were not observed, so how long each fallback lasts in production is not known.
- **Every condition the evaluator cannot judge is labelled `subjective`.** The progress line prints the count of `unknown` results as `subjective` ([PlaybookPage.tsx:167](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L167), [PlaybookPage.tsx:214-216](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L214)) whatever reason the server gave: of the 54 condition texts of a ticker's 12 cards, 14 can never be judged (10 `unrecognized`, among them `Order block nearby (institutional interest)`, `Volume increasing on bounce` and `Bearish divergence (price higher, RSI lower)`, 3 `strat pattern` and 1 `subjective`), and a failed evaluation counts every condition the same way (executed with the real evaluator on the production cards).
- **"Fully lit" counts what could be judged and the percentage counts everything.** A card is fully lit when every condition that was not unknown is met ([PlaybookPage.tsx:168](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L168), [PlaybookPage.tsx:173](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L173)) while the percentage divides by all of them ([PlaybookPage.tsx:169](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L169)): cards 8 to 11 can judge one condition in four, and card 11 was fully lit, with the strong border, at `1/4 conditions met · 3 subjective` (executed with the real evaluator on the production cards).
- **The opening-range conditions go dark after about 11:38 ET.** The history request asks for the compact series, the last 100 one-minute bars ([live.py:317-331](../../platform/api/routers/live.py#L317)), so once the last bar is past about 11:38 ET no bar from 09:30 to 09:59 is left, `orbHigh` and `orbLow` are null and every `ORB 30m` condition reads `ORB n/a` (executed with bars ending at 12:00 ET; the vendor's compact length is read in the code and not observed).
- **The opening range is the one value the page derives itself, with its own window.** `computeORB` takes the high and low of the bars timed 09:30 to 09:59 ([playbookEvaluator.ts:40-56](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/lib/playbookEvaluator.ts#L40)), where `calculate_orb` in the library also takes the bar at the window's end, 10:00 for 30 minutes ([indicators.py:636](../../lib/indicators.py#L636)), and `ORB_WINDOW_MINUTES` in the router ([playbook.py:511](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L511)) is read by nothing; CLAUDE.md Rule 5 says the app never recomputes what the server computes, and the live indicators answer has no opening range (read).
- **A missing quote is replaced by the last bar's close, unlabelled.** The snapshot's `price` is the quote's, else the last bar's close ([playbookEvaluator.ts:90](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/lib/playbookEvaluator.ts#L90)), and the evaluation's detail text (`279.92 > VWAP 279.95`) does not say which was used, while the trade levels need the quote ([PlaybookPage.tsx:365](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L365)); read in the code, not executed.
- **Conditions that describe a past state are judged on the present, and support and resistance on one anchor.** `RSI > 55 (was overbought from the 2U move)`, `RSI < 45 (was oversold from the 2D move)` and `RSI was elevated (> 60) at breakout` are each the latest RSI against the number, and `Price at or near support level (prev day low, VWAP, order block)` and its resistance twin are judged against the previous day's low or high alone ([playbook.py:727-736](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L727)), so the detail a row shows (`RSI 54.5 > 55.0`, `0.81% from prev low`) answers a narrower question than the text above it (executed with the real evaluator).
- **RVOL is judged against a whole day's average.** `RVOL > 1.0` divides the quote's volume by the 20-session average daily volume ([playbook.py:636-645](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L636)), so it can pass only once the day's volume reaches an average whole day: with an assumed 9,000,000 shares against IWM's production average of 23,080,481.55 the evaluator printed `RVOL 0.39 > 1.0` (executed). Live Market's Gaps record the same division for its strength panel, and the silent AlphaVantage fallback of the average-volume read, whose `source` this page does not read either (area 05).
- **`Avg Return` reads `+0.0%` or `-0.0%` on every production card.** The server sends `avg_return` in percent rounded to two decimals ([playbook.py:198](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L198), [playbook.py:252](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L252)) from per-trade averages of -1.28 to +0.88 basis points, so all 36 cards of the three tickers carry -0.0, 0.0, -0.01 or 0.01, the page keeps one decimal ([PlaybookPage.tsx:249](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L249)) and `-0.0 >= 0` is true ([PlaybookPage.tsx:248](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L248)): card 1 (average -0.49 bps) read a green `+0.0%` and card 5 (-0.51 bps) a red `-0.0%` (executed on the production rows).
- **The caption says what the producer does not count.** The block says the win rate is how often the target hits before the stop ([SetupCardDetails.tsx:88-90](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/playbook/SetupCardDetails.tsx#L88)), but `_score_trades` counts a trade as a win when its realised return is above zero, and a trade that touches neither level is marked to its close at the time stop ([phase6_playbook.py:213-221](../../scripts/analysis/phase6_playbook.py#L213)): the real function scored one trade that drifted up 1 basis point in 30 minutes, touching neither a 30 bp target nor a 15 bp stop, as `win_rate` 1.0 (executed on synthetic bars).
- **A card without a PUT direction is drawn as a CALL.** Anything other than PUT, NEUTRAL or a missing direction included, puts the target above the entry and the stop below ([SetupCardDetails.tsx:52](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/playbook/SetupCardDetails.tsx#L52)): a card edited to NEUTRAL drew CALL levels under a blue badge (executed); the producer writes only CALL and PUT, so no production card reaches it. Dashboard's Gaps record the same default and the `live price` label (area 04).
- **The star marks the best average even when it loses, and two typed fields are never drawn.** The star sits on `best_horizon_min`, the window of highest average return, and on the production rows that average is negative on 8 of the 12 cards of each of IWM, SPY and QQQ (counted in the rows of 2026-10-01); `best_horizon_win_rate` and `best_horizon_avg_bps` are typed and sent and never drawn ([SetupCardDetails.tsx:94-121](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/playbook/SetupCardDetails.tsx#L94)), and `generated_at`, `max_age_days`, `ticker` and `source` of the answer are never read.
- **The age is counted against the UTC date.** `age_days` is the UTC calendar date minus `analysis_date` ([playbook.py:277-280](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L277), [playbook.py:324](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L324)), so a set written at 04:30 ET read `age_days` 0 at 19:59 ET and 1 from 20:00 ET, and the page prints `(1d old)` for the rest of the evening (executed against the real handler with its clock pinned; stocks CLAUDE.md §3.9 gives the rule, and `snapshotAgeLabel` prints what it is given).
- **A page left open past the close keeps its live snapshot and calls it live.** A disabled query keeps its data ([PlaybookPage.tsx:268-272](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L268)): after the status turned `closed` at 20:05 ET the header still read `Cards light up as live market conditions are met` and card 1 still read `2/5 conditions met`, the history and the quote were requested once more in that minute and not again, and only the status polled afterwards (executed with the clock moved), so a snapshot taken at the close stays on screen as live until a reload or the next open.
- **The permission block cannot show.** `DataGate` replaces the body only when a gated call has answered 401 and the user is signed out ([PlaybookPage.tsx:337-369](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L337), [SignInEmptyState.tsx:93-98](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/shared/SignInEmptyState.tsx#L93)), which `firebase` mode prevents (`AuthGate` shows the sign-in screen first, executed with `/playbook` opened signed out) and `open` and `iap` modes cannot produce (`useUser` reports signed in always, [useUser.ts:81](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useUser.ts#L81)); a signed-in user's 401 shows the shell's expired-session strip and this page's error box instead (executed, PLAYBOOK-10 in UI-SCREENS.md).
- The count has no singular, and the spec asserts the wrong grammar: one card reads `1 setups` ([PlaybookPage.tsx:331](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L331), [playbook.spec.ts:83-88](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/playbook/playbook.spec.ts#L83)).
- `useReference` returns `null` for any non-OK response ([PlaybookPage.tsx:77](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L77)), a data-access `return null` Rule 4 forbids, and the snapshot then carries no prior close, high or low: with the reference answering 404 `Price at or near support level` read `no reference level` and nothing said the read had failed (executed). The answer's `source` and `stale_days` are not read either (Dashboard's Gaps, area 04).
- **The producer defaults a missing indicator column to a neutral value.** `build_all_cards` reads six indicator columns with `df.get(column, neutral)`, so an absent column becomes RSI 50, StochRSI K 50, RVOL 1.0 and 0 for the VWAP position, the EMA cross and the ORB trend on every bar ([phase6_playbook.py:411-416](../../scripts/analysis/phase6_playbook.py#L411)), the hardcoded neutral default that stocks CLAUDE.md §3.7 forbids; the ORB trend and the EMA cross select the trades of cards 6, 7 and 12 ([phase6_playbook.py:551](../../scripts/analysis/phase6_playbook.py#L551), [phase6_playbook.py:574](../../scripts/analysis/phase6_playbook.py#L574), [phase6_playbook.py:703](../../scripts/analysis/phase6_playbook.py#L703)), so a missing column would empty their masks without an error (read in the code; whether a production frame ever lacks one of these columns was not checked).
- **An unparseable or NULL `conditions` or `horizons` cell is read as an empty list.** `_jsonish` returns `[]` for a NULL and for a string that is not valid JSON ([playbook.py:218-228](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L218), the except branch at [playbook.py:226-227](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L226)), so such a cell would reach the page as a card with no conditions or no hold windows and no error, a data-access `return []` of the kind stocks CLAUDE.md §3.7 forbids (read in the code; whether any row or driver version reaches it was not checked, and every card of the 2026-10-01 sets arrived with its conditions, [V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5934848543)).
- The page cannot switch ticker itself (PLAYBOOK-04), as on Live (area 05): it follows the persisted store, whose value another page's combobox sets, and `playbook_cards` holds IWM, QQQ and SPY only, so any other symbol the combobox offers draws the 404 box (executed for `AAPL`).
- The spec's payload is an untyped literal ([playbook.spec.ts:16-35](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/playbook/playbook.spec.ts#L16)): its card has `direction` `long` and `win_rate` 0.62, while the page types direction as CALL, PUT or NEUTRAL ([PlaybookPage.tsx:26](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L26)) and the handler sends win rates as percents ([playbook.py:251-252](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L251)).
- Sources that disagree with the code: 04-BACKEND-API.md gives `via lib/` for both playbook routes, and the handler reads `playbook_cards` itself while the evaluator is inline in the router. 05-INFRASTRUCTURE.md's `manual` schedule for `phase6-playbook` is in Dashboard's Gaps (area 04). 05 also gives the job 8Gi, `deploy_phase6_playbook` sets 16Gi, 4 CPU and `--tasks 3 --parallelism 3` (`gcp/deploy.sh:1584-1607`), and the live job runs 16Gi, 4 CPU and 1 task (read 2026-09-28 and again 2026-10-01). The design seed gives it 1 GiB and `playbook tables`. UI-SCREENS.md gave the page's calls as `/api/market/reference/` and `/api/playbook/` only, its Purpose an as-of review mode the page never sends (it sends no `date`, and the Replay control is not shown on `/playbook`), its size as 355 lines for a 372-line component, the ticker store as per session (it persists in localStorage as `ticker-store`), the reference's producer as `fetch-market-data` (the handler asks AlphaVantage for a date under 30 days old), every typed field of the live answers as read (the page reads `price` and `volume`, `bars`, `is_open` and `session`, `avg_volume_20d`, `indicators`, and `close`, `high` and `low` of the reference), PLAYBOOK-04's ticker control as the header combobox, and a first journey whose progress bar reaches every judgeable condition and a third that switches ticker on this page; corrected with this area.
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits in `platform/api/routers/playbook.py`, and every route in the rows declares a `response_model`.
- **What the tests assert.** The six tests of [playbook.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/playbook/playbook.spec.ts) on main run on the default `IWM` with the market mocked closed and assert less than their titles say. Mutations of a scratch copy of the page on 2026-10-01 (one product line each, the six tests run) failed a test for removing the count or the age, changing the one-day wording, removing the empty box, changing the error prefix or dropping the server's reason, and removing the card name; nothing failed for dropping the ticker from the title, removing the loading box, every condition row, the direction badge or the win rate, bypassing `dataUnlessError`, changing either evaluation line or forcing the page to read live. No test was found for PLAYBOOK-06, no test mounts the trade levels with a price, and PLAYBOOK-07's spec test exercises a response the handler never sends. In [test_playbook_evaluate.py](../../tests/api/test_playbook_evaluate.py), `test_stochrsi_phrase_does_not_match_rsi_rule` asserts nothing about the separation it names (the evaluator answers unknown with no detail for its input, executed), and the docstring of `test_price_at_support_within_threshold` gives the proximity threshold as about 0.3% where `PRICE_PROXIMITY_PCT` is 0.5%.
- **The two tests added on this branch** (solyra commits 22b606f and c06ff84, [playbook.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/playbook/playbook.spec.ts)) cover PLAYBOOK-04 (`the page follows the active ticker in the store and asks for that ticker only`) and PLAYBOOK-05 (`during the session the cards fill from the posted snapshot, and a card is fully lit once every judgeable condition is met`). They pass on the unchanged page, which already does what they assert, and each of fifteen one-line mutations of the scratch copy failed one of them and no other test: the playbook, average-volume and reference hooks and the heading fixed on IWM failed the ticker test, and the batched body without its batches, the snapshot without the quote's price, average volume or prior close, no condition counted as met, no card fully lit, an unknown condition counted as judged, the page always reading no live data, a session gate that never opens, the `subjective` label renamed and the percent computed over the judged conditions instead of all of them failed the fill test; the last of these survived the first version of the test, which pinned the percent only on a card whose conditions can all be judged, and c06ff84 pins it on the fully lit card too. Six further mutations run for the review (the snapshot's volume, prior high, minutes since the open and last bar, a card lit on any met condition, and the detail text hidden) failed the fill test as well. PLAYBOOK-04 has on main only the ticker store's unit tests, [tickerStore.test.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/stores/tickerStore.test.ts) (`setTicker`, `pushRecent` and `persist partialize`), and PLAYBOOK-05 on main the handler's tests and the client helpers' ([usePlaybookEvaluation.test.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/usePlaybookEvaluation.test.ts), [playbookEvaluator.test.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/lib/playbookEvaluator.test.ts), [contract.test.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/mocks/contract.test.ts)); both rows wait for a CI run that includes the branch's tests.
- **Te: what the two ticks cover and what they leave.** PLAYBOOK-08 is ticked on the page test of the 503 (`a stale card set (503) is reported with the server reason, not rendered`), the handler's refusal, 404, 503 and raise-on-failure tests, the evaluate 400 and 422 and `responseErrorMessage`; no test asserts the status suffix, the 404, 500 and 401 forms, the retry, or the page when the evaluation, history, quote, indicators or status call fails. PLAYBOOK-09 is ticked on the label test, the refusal test, the handler's freshness contract (`test_playbook_fresh_set_is_served_with_its_date` to `test_playbook_age_days_helper`), `snapshotAgeLabel` and `dataUnlessError`; no test asserts a refused refetch of a page that holds data, the UTC counting or the live snapshot's age. The other eight rows stay unticked: PLAYBOOK-01's tests assert the count and the age and not the ticker in the title or either evaluation line, PLAYBOOK-02's page test asserts the card name only, no test asserts PLAYBOOK-03 or PLAYBOOK-06, PLAYBOOK-07's page test asserts the copy for a body the handler never sends, PLAYBOOK-10 has the wrapper's callback and a synthetic route's 401 but nothing on `DataGate` or a playbook route, and PLAYBOOK-04 and PLAYBOOK-05 are covered above.
- **Rows left unticked at V and why, and what the ticks do not cover.** PLAYBOOK-03 needs the quote's price, a vendor-live read behind a gate, and its hold-window half reads the card rows PLAYBOOK-02 is ticked on; PLAYBOOK-04 is an action and PLAYBOOK-05 needs five live calls (status, history, quote, reference and indicators), three of them AlphaVantage reads that no session-free request reaches, behind a session gate; PLAYBOOK-06 to PLAYBOOK-09 are client-side states that production does not show without a browser and a token, and PLAYBOOK-07 cannot be reached at all ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5934848543)). PLAYBOOK-01 and PLAYBOOK-02 are ticked on the freshness of `playbook_cards` and the real handler answering on those rows, and PLAYBOOK-10 on the 401 of all eight gated routes on staging (`solyra-api-prod` answers 302 from its IAP front door); the playbook request was not called with a session, so its latency and the page as a signed-in user sees it were not observed. For PLAYBOOK-01 the tick covers the title, the count and the age and not the evaluation line, which follows three calls: the status (the handler's own Eastern clock), the history (an AlphaVantage read, TIME_SERIES_INTRADAY, behind the session gate) and the indicators (computed in the handler from the posted bars). Only the history is a vendor read, and that half was not observed against production, the same kind of half that keeps PLAYBOOK-03 unticked. The quote, the page's other AlphaVantage read, is not needed to light the line, but its price and volume key the indicators query together with the bars' count and last time, so the line reverts to the paused text on each change of the quote's price or volume and on each new minute bar (see the Gaps). The last row of section d of the V comment, the average-volume handler's source, is a code read and not an executed result (corrected in a [follow-up comment](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5935963880)), and that comment's wording of the evaluation line's calls ("the status, history, quote and indicators calls", "vendor-live") is corrected in a [second comment](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5936680248). The ticks also carry display defects that V does not cover, and the findings above are the record of them; the list is not complete, and among them are, for PLAYBOOK-01, the count's `1 setups`, the UTC age, and an evaluation line that cannot tell a failed read from a closed market and that reverts on each change of the quote's price or volume and on each new minute bar; for PLAYBOOK-02, the `Avg Return` of `+0.0%` or `-0.0%` on every card and the 14 of 54 condition texts that are never judged; and for PLAYBOOK-10, a permission block that cannot show (see the Gaps).

### Backend notes
- No pipeline feeds this page. The prefix `raw/reports/` holds 45 objects, 24 markdown at the top level and 21 CSV files under `data/`, all created at 22:41:43 UTC on 2026-04-12 (the listing and copies read on 2026-10-01). The 23 `phase*.md` objects, the only ones the page lists, each open with a generated line of 2026-02-22 (06:20:55 to 23:54:33); the 24th, `timeframe_combo_analysis.md`, has none. The scripts that write such files, `scripts/analysis/phase1_strat_mining.py` to `scripts/analysis/phase7_feedback_loop.py` and `scripts/analysis/phase6_playbook.py`, save them in the process's own `reports/` directory (`save_report`, [shared_utils.py:328-335](../../scripts/analysis/shared_utils.py#L328)); [reports/README.md](../../reports/README.md) documents uploading them by hand with `gsutil cp`, and no code or workflow in this repository does it. The live `phase6-playbook` job (04:30 ET Mon-Fri, as `trading-runner@`) is no exception: its execution of 2026-10-01 (`phase6-playbook-854cj`, succeeded) logged `Report saved: /app/reports/phase6_playbook_iwm.md` and the same line for `spy`, `qqq` and `combined`, inside its container, while the bucket's objects of those names are still the April ones. The scripts read stored one-minute bars through [data_loader.py:234-311](../../lib/data_loader.py#L234) (Cloud SQL `market_data_intraday`, or local AlphaVantage parquet files) and call no vendor and no model. The bucket's lifecycle deletes objects under `raw/` at 730 days (read 2026-10-01), so these would go on 2028-04-11.
- Freshness the page assumes: the list holds for 60 s and a body for five minutes ([ReportsPage.tsx:21](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ReportsPage.tsx#L21), [ReportsPage.tsx:34](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ReportsPage.tsx#L34)). The server caches the list and each body for 24 hours ([playbook.py:100-103](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L100)) and the bucket listing for ten minutes (`platform/api/gcs_reader.py:72`), so an upload can take up to a day to show on an instance that has the old answer. None of these bounds the age of the text, and the page reads no age (REPORTS-09).
- Order: the list is the ticker's own reports sorted descending by name, then the combined ones, descending too. The real handlers on the production objects (executed 2026-10-01) returned, for IWM, SPY and QQQ, phase 6, 5, 4, 3, 2 and 1, then the combined 7, 6, 5d, 4 and 1 (eleven entries), and for AAPL and SPX only the five combined ones. The page lands on the first entry and Previous and Next walk that order, while the dropdown groups phases ascending ([reports.ts:38-59](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/lib/reports.ts#L38)).
- What the page can list: a name is listed only if it starts `phase` and ends `.md`, so `timeframe_combo_analysis.md` (11,147 bytes) and the 21 CSV files under `data/` are in the prefix and never listed. For each of the 43 entries the real list handler returned for IWM, SPY, QQQ, AAPL and SPX, the real body handler answered the bytes of that entry's object as `text/plain` (executed).

### Gaps
- [#813](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/813) (open) is this page's blocking issue in UI-SCREENS.md. It concerns walk-forward calibration in `lib/walk_forward.py` and `scripts/run_param_sweep.py`, and nothing on this page reads either.
- **The first report and Previous or Next follow the server's reverse order.** The handler sends a ticker's own reports newest name first and then the combined ones the same way ([playbook.py:393-427](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L393)), the dropdown groups phases ascending ([reports.ts:38-59](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/lib/reports.ts#L38)), and the page lands on the first entry of the server's list. The real handler on the production objects gave IWM `Phase 6: Playbook` first, the counter at `1 / 11` and Previous disabled, while the dropdown listed Phase 1 first; Next walked Phase 5, 4, 3, 2 and 1, crossed to the combined reports at Phase 7 and walked 6, 5:D, 4 and 1 down to `11 / 11` (executed 2026-10-01, the page on the real handlers). The spec's two-entry mock is already ascending, so its walk test, `prev/next walk the pipeline order and disable at the ends` in [reports.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/reports/reports.spec.ts), passes.
- **The empty state cannot appear, and a storage outage reads as not found.** An empty listing is a 404 ([playbook.py:429-433](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L429)), which the page shows as `Could not load the report list for` (executed). The listing helper returns an empty list on any storage error (`platform/api/gcs_reader.py:93-95`) and the body route's two listings do the same, so an outage is a 404 from both routes (executed with the storage client raising), not the 502 that [reports/README.md](../../reports/README.md) promises for an unreachable bucket; a 502 needs a successful listing and then a failed download. [test_route_coverage.py](../../tests/api/test_route_coverage.py) records the 404. The spec's test `an empty list shows the honest empty state` ([reports.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/reports/reports.spec.ts)) mocks a 200 with an empty list, and the empty copy's instruction, `Run the analysis pipeline to generate them`, names no job, because no job writes the prefix (see the Backend notes).
- **Both states leave a prompt that contradicts the picker.** Under the error banner and under the empty message the box reads `Select a report above` while the select is disabled and reads `No reports`, and while the list loads the select reads `No reports` with an em dash counter and nothing says the page is loading ([ReportsPage.tsx:130-133](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ReportsPage.tsx#L130), [ReportsPage.tsx:187-195](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ReportsPage.tsx#L187); executed).
- **Nothing shows a report's age, and the text is 221 days old.** The header shows the phase and filename only, the list answer carries no object time ([playbook.py:404-427](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L404)), and the page's and the server's cache lifetimes bound nothing about the text. Each of the 23 `phase*.md` objects opens with its own `Generated: 2026-02-22 <time>` line, which renders under the report's title and which the page does not read; the objects were created 172 days before the read of 2026-10-01 and the Phase 6 report's data window ends on 2026-02-20, while the Playbook page's cards come from `playbook_cards` dated 2026-10-01 (area 11) and nothing on this page says which of the two is current.
- A ticker with no reports of its own gets the combined reports under its name: the real handler gave AAPL and SPX five combined reports, never the not-found state, and the page with AAPL active read `Reports: AAPL` and landed on `Phase 7: Feedback Loop` at `1 / 5` (executed); those reports cover IWM, SPY and QQQ, and the header and filename of a combined report name no ticker.
- **The option labels capitalise after the colon.** `phaseLabel` turns `phase5d_cross_ticker` into `Phase 5:D Cross Ticker`, while the report's own title is `Phase 5D: Cross-Ticker Correlation & Confirmation` ([reports.ts:18-25](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/lib/reports.ts#L18); executed on the real list), and no Vitest test covers `phaseLabel`.
- **Errors show no reason, and a second cold request is read as a failure.** The list banner and `Report not available.` drop the server's `detail`, so a missing object, a storage failure and an empty text read alike ([ReportsPage.tsx:163-168](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ReportsPage.tsx#L163), [ReportsPage.tsx:54-61](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ReportsPage.tsx#L54)). A second cold request for a list or a text that another request is filling is a 503 `retry shortly` with `Retry-After: 5` ([playbook.py:386-391](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L386), [playbook.py:461-466](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L461); executed with two threads), which the client does not read: it retries once, so a 503 followed by a 200 recovered the list and two 503 in a row showed the banner (executed with the browser answering 503, two calls each). No test asserts the 503.
- **The sanitizer is DOMPurify's defaults, and its two tests cover a script tag and an inline handler.** Executed on the real `renderReportHtml`, `<script>`, inline handlers, `javascript:` hrefs, `target`, `<iframe>`, `<meta>`, `<base>` and `<link>` are removed, but a `<style>` element that is not first in the text, a `<form action=...>` with its controls, a remote `<img>` and a `data:` image survive, and in the browser a `<style>` in a report text restyled the page's own header ([reports.ts:66-68](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/lib/reports.ts#L66)). The objects are first-party and none of the 24 holds an HTML tag or a link.
- **The reports point at objects the bucket does not hold, and the page cannot list them.** The combined Phase 6 report's `Important Context` sends the reader to `reports/walk_forward_tf_combos_{ticker}.md` for out-of-sample validation and to `reports/options_pnl_{ticker}.md`; neither is in the prefix, and the handler's patterns (a name that starts `phase`) would not list them if they were. The Phase 5 reports' section `5G. Walk-Forward Validation` is rolling-window pattern stability over 2015 to 2026, the Phase 6 report says its results are in-sample, and no listed report holds an out-of-sample result, so the record's journey step that notes which setups degraded out-of-sample cannot be done here.
- `GET /api/reports/{ticker}/{phase}` is plain text with no `response_model` ([playbook.py:440](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L440)), typed in the snapshot as a bare string; the phase is matched as a filename prefix (`phase6` serves `phase6_playbook_iwm.md`, executed) and `VALID_PHASES` ([playbook.py:106-109](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L106)) is defined and never read.
- Sources that disagree with the code: UI-SCREENS.md described the page as backtest, walk-forward and replay-trainer results and listed solyra's [replay-trainer.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/charts/replay-trainer.spec.ts) among its specs; the page serves the phase analysis markdown only and that spec never opens `/reports` (the record is corrected). The design seed repeats the description, expects an empty list for a ticker without reports and has the reader step forward with Next and note out-of-sample degradation (the record's journeys are corrected). 04-BACKEND-API.md gives `via lib/` for both routes, which read GCS through `platform/api/gcs_reader.py`. [reports/README.md](../../reports/README.md) says an unreachable bucket gives a 502, and [GCP_DATA_DICTIONARY.md](../../platform/GCP_DATA_DICTIONARY.md) (its Reports row) says research and phase jobs write the phase report markdown to GCS, which none does (see the Backend notes). [02-FEATURE-CATALOG.md](02-FEATURE-CATALOG.md#feat-report-001) maps FEAT-REPORT-001 to `/api/analytics`, `/api/backtest` and the tables `backtest_*` and `walk_forward_results`, and FEAT-REPLAY-001 to this route with `/api/backtest/replay-trades`, while the page calls neither ([ReportsPage.tsx:17](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ReportsPage.tsx#L17), [ReportsPage.tsx:29](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ReportsPage.tsx#L29)).
- No `AUDIT` marker sits in `platform/api/routers/playbook.py` or `platform/api/gcs_reader.py`, whose swallowing listing CLAUDE.md §3.7.1 names.
- **The permission block cannot show.** `DataGate` replaces the body only when a gated call has answered 401 and the user is signed out ([ReportsPage.tsx:162-197](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ReportsPage.tsx#L162), [SignInEmptyState.tsx:93-98](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/shared/SignInEmptyState.tsx#L93)), which `firebase` mode prevents (`AuthGate` shows the sign-in screen first, executed with `/reports` opened signed out) and `open` and `iap` modes cannot produce (`useUser` reports signed in always, [useUser.ts:81](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useUser.ts#L81)); a signed-in user's 401 shows the shell's expired-session strip and this page's list banner instead (executed, REPORTS-10 in UI-SCREENS.md).
- **What the tests assert.** The seven tests of [reports.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/reports/reports.spec.ts) on main run on the default `IWM` with a two-entry mock that is already ascending and a body that is the same for every phase, and assert less than their names say. Mutations of a scratch copy of the page on 2026-10-01 (one product line each, the nine tests of the file run) failed a test for the filename shown as the phase, the list banner never drawn, the options showing the raw phase, the groups sorted descending, the select never disabled, the label or the empty message changed, the header showing the raw phase, the counter one too high, Next or Previous disabled a position early, a step of two entries or one that does not move, the markdown shown as text and the body requested for another ticker; nothing failed in the seven tests for the body always being the first report's, the select always showing the first entry, a suffix on the body request, a changed, absent or list-banner text for a failed body, the loading branch skipped, the select enabled while the list loads or the prompt under the list changed. The handler tests, by mutation of a scratch copy of the handler (one line each, the 19 tests with `report` in their names in [test_platform_api.py](../../tests/api/test_platform_api.py), [test_route_coverage.py](../../tests/api/test_route_coverage.py), [test_production_readiness.py](../../tests/lib/test_production_readiness.py) and [test_threadpool_races.py](../../tests/api/test_threadpool_races.py)), failed for a 200 on an empty list or on no matching report, the text served as JSON, the answer naming the ticker in lower case and an own entry's filename without its extension, and passed for deleting either listing loop (the stubbed listing returns the same blob for both patterns), reversing the order, keeping the ticker suffix or the extension in `phase`, changing `path`, listing an own file twice, listing another ticker's files, skipping the ticker-specific lookup of the body, taking the shortest name, keeping the `raw/` prefix, answering a download error as 500 or a missing object as 502, never filling the 24-hour caches and answering a concurrent cold request as an empty 200. `test_report_listing_endpoint_serves_phases` accepts 200, 404, 500, 502 or 503 and checks the shape only for a 200, so without storage credentials it passes on the 404 it gets (executed). No test was found for REPORTS-06 or REPORTS-09, and REPORTS-07's spec test exercises a response the handler never sends. In solyra, [contract.test.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/mocks/contract.test.ts) finds the page's two calls among the vendored snapshot's operations (a renamed literal failed it) and validates the shared mock list `MOCK_REPORT_LIST` against the list's response schema (a filename that is a number, an undeclared field, a renamed filename, a missing path, a ticker that is a number and a removed mock route each failed it), but the body route answers a bare `text/plain` string, so a mock-mode body route removed, or answering a JSON object, passed all fourteen tests; in stocks, [test_openapi_snapshot.py](../../tests/api/test_openapi_snapshot.py) fails when either route, a docstring or `ReportListResponse` changes without a regenerated `platform/api/openapi.json` (six one-line changes of the routes and the models failed it) and passed a change of the body handler's behaviour alone. Both pin the contract and neither asserts what an entry or a text holds (one-line mutations of scratch copies, 2026-10-01).
- **The two tests added on this branch** (solyra `e422a8a`, [reports.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/reports/reports.spec.ts)) cover REPORTS-05 (`Next and Previous show the report they step to, with both buttons enabled in between`) and REPORTS-08 (`a failed report body shows "Report not available." under the report header, not a blank page`). They pass on the unchanged page, which already does what they assert, and of eighteen one-line mutations of the scratch copy ten failed the first and four the second: the body always the first report's, the select always showing the first entry and a suffix on the body request failed the first and none of the seven tests on main, and a changed message, a failed body that draws nothing and a failed body that shows the list banner's text failed the second and none of the seven. The first adds to the walk test the report's own body at each of four positions, the select's value and the page header at the middle entry, and the buttons' states at three positions; the select's value and the header at the first and the last entry stay unasserted (a select showing the wrong entry, or a wrong header, at the first or the last entry passed all nine tests of the file, mutated in a three-entry list, while the same faults at the middle entry failed only the first). The second adds the whole body-failure presentation. REPORTS-05 and REPORTS-08 wait for a CI run that includes the branch's tests.
- **Te: why no row is ticked.** What every row shows, the text of the chosen entry in the order the server sends, is asserted by no test on main: the page tests run on a mock whose list is ascending and whose body is the same for every phase, and the handler tests stub the listing and the download without checking which object is asked for (the mutations above passed every test). REPORTS-01 and REPORTS-02 depend on the list handler, whose merge of the ticker's own and the combined reports, order and the values of `phase` and `path` no test asserts (`test_reports_list` passes with either listing loop deleted, and the contract and snapshot tests pin only the shape of the answer); REPORTS-03 on the body handler, whose choice of object no test asserts, and on the body following the choice, which only the test added for REPORTS-05 asserts; REPORTS-04 and REPORTS-05 have page tests that assert the header, the counter and the buttons but not the body that follows a choice or a step (REPORTS-05's body is the added test's alone and waits for a CI run that includes the branch's tests); REPORTS-07's page test asserts the copy for a response the handler never sends; REPORTS-08's body half is asserted by the added test alone and its list half by the 500 test; REPORTS-06 and REPORTS-09 have no test; and REPORTS-10 has the wrapper's callback and a synthetic route's 401 but nothing on `DataGate` or a report route.
- **Rows left unticked at V and why, and what the ticks do not cover.** REPORTS-04 and REPORTS-05 are actions that need a browser; REPORTS-06 is a client-side state that shows only while a request is in flight, so it needs a browser and a slow answer, not a failure; REPORTS-08 is a client-side state that needs a browser and an injected failure (the objects exist, so no refusal is visible); REPORTS-09 has nothing to show, since the page has no age and no stale marker; REPORTS-07 cannot be reached at all, since the handler answers 404 and never a 200 with no reports ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5938118234)). REPORTS-01, REPORTS-02 and REPORTS-03 are ticked on the listing of the prefix, a read of the object the page lands on and of every `phase*.md` object's generated line, and the real handlers answering on those objects with the eleven entries and the bytes of each entry's object, and REPORTS-10 on the 401 of both gated routes on staging (`solyra-api-prod` answers 302 from its IAP front door). The list and report requests were never answered with a session, so the services' own read of the bucket as `trading-platform-svc@`, their latency and the page as a signed-in user sees it were not observed; the account's `roles/storage.objectViewer` binding and the bucket the services are configured with (`GCS_BUCKET` on prod, `/api/health` on staging) were read. The page accepts any age: the ticks say the page can show these objects, not that they are current, which they are not (221 days old by their text). The ticks also carry display defects that V does not cover, and the list is not complete: for REPORTS-01, a dropdown that lists phases ascending while the page lands on Phase 6 and Next walks backward, the label `Phase 5:D Cross Ticker`, and a select that reads `No reports` while the list loads; for REPORTS-02, no age beside a label and a filename that name no ticker for the combined reports; for REPORTS-03, February text shown with no marker, a sanitizer that keeps a `<style>` or a `<form>`, and reports that point at objects the bucket does not hold; for REPORTS-10, a permission block that cannot show (see the entry above).
