### Backend notes
- Pipeline order feeding this page (America/New_York): `signal-monitor` 09:25 Mon-Fri writes each live `trades` entry and exit and every live `signal_alerts` row through the session → `signal-monitor-eod-resolver` 16:30 Mon-Fri closes the trades left open → `fetch-alphavantage-intraday` 21:00 Mon-Sat and on the 1st, and `fetch-market-data` 23:00 Mon-Fri, write the chart's `market_data_intraday`. Users write `journal_entries`, here through marks, the form and imports (an import cannot commit on the production table, Gaps) and on the Charts page through its marks and replay sessions, and style mining writes `user_style_results` and `playbook_cards_staging`. `scripts/backfill_journal_embeddings.py`, run by hand, writes only `embedding` and `updated_at` on `journal_entries` (lines 78-83), neither of which this page reads. On 2026-10-01 the schedules ran as written ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5941270196)): the day's `signal-monitor` was three executions, a long one created 13:25 UTC and completed 20:01 UTC and two short ones at 13:45 and 14:00 UTC, all succeeded; the resolver ran at 20:30 UTC, `fetch-alphavantage-intraday` at 01:00 UTC and `fetch-market-data` at 03:00 UTC, all succeeded. The day before, the long `signal-monitor` execution ended with a failed task (Gaps).
- Freshness the page assumes: the own journal holds 10 s, and Examples 30 s with no retry ([useJournalChartTrades.ts:312-323](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useJournalChartTrades.ts#L312), [useJournalChartTrades.ts:337-351](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useJournalChartTrades.ts#L337)), nothing polls, every mutation refetches the ticker's journal and an import every ticker's, and the page shows no data age. A read-only query on 2026-10-01 found the newest live `trades` entries on 2026-10-01 for all three tickers (IWM 19:46 UTC, SPY 13:38, QQQ 13:46), with 812, 1,009 and 914 live rows inside regular hours, the rows Examples serve, and one-minute bars up to the 20:00 ET bar of 2026-09-30 for IWM and SPY (execution `db-query-drz7l`, [V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5941270196)); the same query on 2026-09-28 had found the newest entries on 2026-09-25 for SPY and QQQ and on 2026-09-23 for IWM, with 989, 899 and 797 rows (execution `db-query-ld8s6`), so the feed has gone quiet for days and the page would have shown it as current.
- Units: the page reads every `return_pct` as a percent. `signal-monitor` and its resolver write `trades.return_pct` as a percent of the underlying ([signal_monitor.py:2328-2331](../../gcp/signal_monitor.py#L2328), [signal_monitor_eod_resolver.py:87-94](../../gcp/signal_monitor_eod_resolver.py#L87)), marks and the manual form store a percent of the underlying ([journal.py:341-355](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L341)), and broker imports a percent of the option premium ([journal.py:358-372](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L358)). A read-only query on 2026-09-28 found each of the 2,719 closed live `trades` rows in percent, the four that also fit a fraction being zero returns (execution `db-query-ld8s6`); on 2026-10-01 each of the 810 closed live IWM rows was in percent, from -0.873631 to 0.875567 with a mean absolute value of 0.1989 and none above 1 ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5941270196)).
- Who the examples admin is: the Examples handler binds `ADMIN_EMAIL`, which defaults in the source to a personal address ([journal.py:106](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L106)); `solyra-api-staging` sets the variable and `solyra-api-prod` does not (read from both services with `gcloud run services describe`), so production reads the default. Production holds two `journal_entries` rows in all, both that account's, one closed SPY row and one open IWM row ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5941270196)).
- `GET /api/journal/trades/{ticker}` also feeds CHARTS-02, `GET /api/market/dates/{ticker}` and `GET /api/market/data/{ticker}/{date}` carry CHARTS-01's and CHARTS-02's chains, and the picker is SIGNALS-01's and OPTIONS-06's, all described the same way here.
- Endpoints with no caller on this page: `GET /api/journal/seed/{ticker}` has a hook, `useSeedTrades` ([useJournalChartTrades.ts:475-491](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useJournalChartTrades.ts#L475)), that no component calls, and `POST /api/backtest/replay-trades` is called from the Charts page, not this one.
- Producer tests for `signal-monitor` and its resolver sit in Signals' Backend notes (area 08).

### Gaps
- UI-SCREENS.md cited [#717](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/717) for this page, but that issue was closed on 2026-09-11 as a duplicate of [#716](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/716); the record now cites #716, which is open. Item 1 of #716, the mix of return units, still holds: `computeJournalStats` sums and averages every row's `return_pct` ([journalStats.ts:103-165](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/lib/journalStats.ts#L103)), and a broker import carries a percent of the option premium where every other row carries a percent of the underlying (Backend notes).
- **Examples multiply a percent by 100.** `_pipeline_rows_to_trades` multiplies `trades.return_pct` by 100 ([journal.py:531](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L531)) on the premise that it holds a fraction, and it holds a percent (Backend notes). A hermetic probe of the handler served a stored 0.13, a 600 to 600.78 CALL, as 13.0, and a render of the page showed `+13.00%` in the Return column. The same values drive the rail, the KPI tiles and the equity curve on the Examples view, which a new user sees first; over production's rows the page read `Σ return -1441.38%` for a stored sum of -14.4, a third row of -7.88% for a trade that moved -0.079%, and an equity axis of -2234.3% to +789.1% ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5941270196)). The seed route multiplies the same way ([journal.py:1368](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L1368)), and [test_journal_examples.py](../../tests/api/test_journal_examples.py) pins the premise with 0.01 stored for a 600 to 606 CALL and 1.0 expected ([test_journal_examples.py:612-620](../../tests/api/test_journal_examples.py#L612)) (filed as [stocks#1219](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1219)).
- **The import commit cannot succeed on the production table.** `POST /api/journal/import/commit` writes `source = "import:<broker>"` ([journal.py:1521-1663](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L1521)), 13 to 16 characters for `import:webull`, `import:generic` and `import:robinhood`, and `journal_entries.source` is `character varying(10)` in production and in [schema.sql:1251](../../gcp/schema.sql#L1251) ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5941270196)). Run against a copy of the production table the commit answered a bare 500 (`value too long for type character varying(10)`) and the modal read `import commit failed: 500`; the same commit with the column widened to 20 as the only change answered 200 `{"imported": 3, "skipped_duplicates": 0}`. Production holds no row whose source starts `import:`. Two tests pin premises that never meet: the endpoint tests run in the local-file branch, where `test_commit_writes_source_import_broker` asserts `import:robinhood`, and [test_schema_journal_migration.py](../../tests/gcp/test_schema_journal_migration.py) asserts `VARCHAR(10)`.
- **A failed own-journal read shows as local storage.** Nothing renders that query's error: the label falls back to `source ?? 'local'` ([JournalPage.tsx:223](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L223)) and reads `Local storage (set CLOUD_SQL_CONNECTION_NAME for persistence)`, and with no rows the view falls to Examples ([useJournalChartTrades.ts:362-367](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useJournalChartTrades.ts#L362)). A render with that read answering 503 showed the label, two requests and no error text; with every journal and market route answering 401 the page said `Examples unavailable, the journal database didn't respond.`, which names the wrong cause, under the shell's expired-session strip.
- **The permission block cannot show, and a 401 is blamed on the database.** `DataGate` replaces the body only when a gated call has answered 401 and the user is signed out ([JournalPage.tsx:506-967](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L506), [SignInEmptyState.tsx:93-98](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/shared/SignInEmptyState.tsx#L93)), which `firebase` mode prevents (`AuthGate` shows the sign-in screen first, executed with `/journal` opened signed out: the only API request was `GET /api/config/firebase`) and `open` and `iap` modes cannot produce (`useUser` reports signed in always, [useUser.ts:81](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useUser.ts#L81)); a signed-in user's 401 shows the shell's expired-session strip over a page that says `Examples unavailable, the journal database didn't respond.` (above), a cause that is not the one, reads `Local storage ...` for the own read and shows the chart card's empty text (executed in `open` mode with a 401 on every journal and market route, JOURNAL-16 in UI-SCREENS.md).
- **Failed marks, exits and deletes are silent.** Only the manual form's error renders ([JournalPage.tsx:494-498](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L494)). A chart mark posts and flips the view to My journal without waiting for the answer ([JournalPage.tsx:585-590](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L585)), and the exit and both delete buttons ([JournalPage.tsx:591](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L591), [JournalPage.tsx:628](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L628), [JournalPage.tsx:942](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L942)) render nothing on a failure (filed as [solyra#76](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/issues/76), whose body describes exactly this); injected 500s on each request went out once and showed nothing, the mark leaving My journal reading `No trades logged for IWM yet.`. `Mark Entry` also does nothing, with no message, when the chart card has no bars, a delete takes one click with no confirmation, and nothing checks that a target or the stop lies on the right side of the entry (a CALL with its first target below the entry was posted and stored).
- **A delete that matches nothing reports success.** The handler ignores the row count and answers `deleted` ([journal.py:1257-1263](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L1257)), which a hermetic probe confirmed with a count of 0 and which answered 200 again for a second delete of the same id (filed as [stocks#1220](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1220)).
- **Export to Pipeline writes a file nothing reads.** The handler writes `{ticker}_trade_tracker.csv` into `data/signals/` on the serving instance ([journal.py:1376-1422](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L1376)), where a hermetic probe saw it land, and no code in `gcp/`, `lib/`, `scripts/` or `platform/` names that file. The page reports the count on success, and any failure, a 422 included, becomes a local download under `API unavailable: downloaded CSV locally` ([JournalPage.tsx:330-343](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L330)), in the same green as the success line. The file is one for each ticker whatever the caller, so two users exporting one ticker overwrite each other (read), the answer carries the server's absolute path, and an export of no trades is a 200 that empties the file (executed with a header-only result). Both buttons write the pipeline's six time columns and nothing else: no price, return or note.
- **Style mining reads a Cloud SQL outage as too few trades.** The trade read goes through the swallowing `query_to_dataframe` (`_replay_journal_query`, [backtest.py:486-490](../../platform/api/routers/backtest.py#L486)), so an outage answers 200 `{"status": "unavailable", "reason": "need >= 10 closed trades, have 0"}` (hermetic probe, repeated with the real handler and an unreachable database, where every other journal route answered 503), while the handler's docstring promises a 5xx ([backtest.py:699-700](../../platform/api/routers/backtest.py#L699)) and [test_route_coverage.py](../../tests/api/test_route_coverage.py) expects 200 with no storage. The six months of bars come through `lib/data_loader.py`, whose SQL wrapper answers empty on any error ([data_loader.py:78-114](../../lib/data_loader.py#L78)) and whose `load_best_available` then returns daily bars ([data_loader.py:498-519](../../lib/data_loader.py#L498)): a hermetic probe with the intraday query failing got the daily bars back, and the response does not say which kind the profile was validated on. The panel says `your closed trades`, but only `manual` and `chart` rows are read ([backtest.py:711-723](../../platform/api/routers/backtest.py#L711)), so a `replay` or imported row counts for nothing.
- **`staged` reaches nothing.** The two inserts go to `user_style_results` and `playbook_cards_staging` ([backtest.py:843-903](../../platform/api/routers/backtest.py#L843)), 05-c lists no reader of either, `PLAYBOOK_USER_CARDS` is `False` ([backtest.py:613](../../platform/api/routers/backtest.py#L613)), and both tables were empty on 2026-09-28 (execution `db-query-ld8s6`) and on 2026-10-01 (`db-query-drz7l`, [V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5941270196)), no owner holding ten closed `manual` or `chart` rows. The badge's tooltip says the profile `was staged server-side for signal evaluation` ([MyStylePanel.tsx:118-126](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/journal/MyStylePanel.tsx#L118)).
- **The manual form accepts nonsense.** It enables Save with a price of `0`, and neither it nor the handler compares the exit with the entry or a price with zero: the real handler stored an exit 30 minutes before the entry as a win, an entry price of 0 with a null return and status `closed`, and prices of -5 and -4 as a loss of -20%; `direction`, `entry_date` and `entry_time` are free strings, so an empty date, a time with seconds or a direction the four-character column cannot hold is a 500, which the page reports as `Failed to save trade, check API connection.` ([journal.py:193-231](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L193), [journal.py:1088-1143](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L1088)). The form's labels are not bound to their inputs (no `for` and no nesting, [JournalPage.tsx:722-790](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L722)), so the controls have no accessible name.
- **The generic importer needs a column that holds `CALL` or `PUT`.** The Schwab, Fidelity and IBKR chips start from a guessed preset of five columns and leave `Direction` empty ([ImportTradesModal.tsx:52-56](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/journal/ImportTradesModal.tsx#L52)); a Schwab-shaped file whose only candidate was the description `CALL ISHARES RUSSELL 2000 ETF` had every row skipped as `unrecognized direction`, and `Import 0 trades` was disabled. The preset is a guess the code says it never checked against a real export.
- **The loading and empty states misreport.** While the dates list loads, or after it fails, the chart card reads `No market data available for this date` over `Markets may be closed (weekend or holiday)` and the toolbar `No session data`, because the bars query is disabled until a date exists ([useMarketData.ts:47](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useMarketData.ts#L47)); a failed dates request draws no error, since the page reads only the `data` of that query ([JournalPage.tsx:200](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L200)) and the hook's `Failed to load dates` is never read, so an outage of the dates handler, which answers 503 on a database failure, looks like a closed market, and `Mark Entry` does nothing, with no message, while the card has no bars (executed with a 503); the rail and the equity card read as empty while the rows load; a user with rows sees the Examples view, with 813 rows, until the own read answers and then My journal; and with the form open on a view with no rows and no date the table area says `No trades on this session, clear the date for the Overview.` (executed).
- **Nothing shows how old the Examples are.** The newest rows of 2026-10-01 had bars for none of their session while the chart and rail showed 2026-09-30; the long `signal-monitor` execution of 2026-09-30 ended with a failed task after 46 minutes and that session has five IWM Examples rows, all entered 09:30 to 09:34 ET, with nothing to say whether it is complete (what ended the execution was not investigated; [V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5941270196)); and the feed was quiet from 2026-09-23 to 2026-09-28 for IWM (Backend notes).
- **Examples cost scales with the table.** Each load and each 30 s refetch reads every live regular-hours row of the ticker since 2026-05-01 (812 for IWM, 1,009 for SPY, 914 for QQQ) with no `limit`, joins each to an alert, and the page draws every row at once (813 table rows in the DOM, executed); nothing was timed against production (stocks CLAUDE.md §3.8). The own-journal read has no limit either.
- `POST /api/journal/import/preview` is declared `async def` ([journal.py:1431](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L1431)) and makes the blocking `_existing_entry_keys` read inside it ([journal.py:1504](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L1504)), the shape CLAUDE.md §3.10 describes.
- Sources that disagree with the code: UI-SCREENS.md listed only `/api/journal/export/` and `/api/journal/trades` among the page's calls, where the rows trace fourteen, and gave the component 945 lines, where it has 970; the record now lists the calls and says 970. The design seed lists the export route as a GET, where the page posts, and `/api/analytics/summary` and `trade-stats`, which the page never calls. Its chart journey clicks the entry bar, then the exit bar, then CALL or PUT, where the page takes the entry, then CALL or PUT, then optional targets and a stop, and the exit later from the rail card ([JournalPage.tsx:386-433](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L386)). 04-BACKEND-API.md gives `via lib/` for the create, export and import routes, which do their own SQL, file or `lib/broker_import.py` work. solyra's CLAUDE.md names [TradeMarkingChart.tsx:202](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/journal/TradeMarkingChart.tsx#L202) as the existing `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker, where that line sits in a comment on the drawing-step effect, and the one marker in solyra is at [ChartsPage.tsx:364](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L364) (area 06). The header comment of [journal.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/journal/journal.spec.ts) says it covers add, delete and export, and no test did until the one added on this branch pressed the export buttons.
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits in `platform/api/routers/journal.py` or `platform/api/routers/backtest.py`, and the one in `lib/data_loader.py` (line 660) is in `load_trades`, off this path. Every route in the rows declares a `response_model`.
- **What the tests assert.** JOURNAL-02, JOURNAL-03 and JOURNAL-06: the page tests assert specific visible facts on mocked rows, and the Examples rows behind them are asserted at the handler only under the fraction premise that production's rows contradict. JOURNAL-05, JOURNAL-08, JOURNAL-12 and JOURNAL-15 have no page assertion on main for what the rows do (the date helper of the form, the handler tests of the create route against patched calls, and the import spec's refetch count are all they have); JOURNAL-13 and JOURNAL-14 have page assertions for part of what they show (the own empty text and the equity placeholder; the style panel's two failure shapes and the import preview's 422); `route /journal loads without fatal errors` mounts the page and asserts the nav and main elements only. JOURNAL-07's page test asserts the prompts, the post's `ticker`, `direction` and `source` and the flip, not the entry price, time or targets, and nothing asserts the exit on the page. JOURNAL-09's tests run where the production width cannot occur (above). JOURNAL-16: no test asserts `DataGate`, the shell's strip or what the page shows on a 401, which `DataGate` cannot show.
- **Te.** JOURNAL-04 is ticked: on main, four page tests assert when the panel shows and its three answers, the chips, the sample sizes, the validation line, the `staged` badge and the request body, and six handler tests assert the endpoint over stub loaders, a stub validator and captured inserts (the floor of ten, the open-trade exclusion, no profile, the percent conversion, zero trades across the folds, the 503). Left unasserted, and so recorded here: the pending state, the idle text, the ticker remount, the six-month loader, a run on real tables and the answer for a Cloud SQL failure. Every other row stays unticked for the reasons above or has no assertion on main.
- **The tests added on this branch** wait for a CI run that includes the branch's tests, so JOURNAL-10, JOURNAL-11 and JOURNAL-14 stay unticked at Te. They are in [journal.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/journal/journal.spec.ts) (solyra `6fd6ba4`, tests only, no product code): `CSV downloads the active view, and Export to Pipeline posts only the closed trades and reports the count` (JOURNAL-10), `a picked session narrows the tiles and the table, Overview restores them, and the toggle swaps the rows` (JOURNAL-11) and `a failed Examples read says so in a banner and in the table area, and a failed manual save keeps the form open with its error` (JOURNAL-14, which also covers the failed save of JOURNAL-08). All three pass on the unchanged page, which already does this, and fifteen one-line mutations of a scratch copy of the page (five, four and six, the whole file run each time) failed the test they target: the CSV header without `Runner_Time`, a file name that is not lowercased, an export that keeps the open trade, a changed line wording and a line without the skipped note (JOURNAL-10); a table that ignores the session, tiles that ignore it, an `Overview` that does not clear it and a toggle that does nothing (JOURNAL-11); a banner never drawn, a banner on My journal, the card in the quiet copy, a form that closes on failure, a changed error text and an error never drawn (JOURNAL-14). Each failed its test and no other, except the dead toggle, which also failed the JOURNAL-14 test and the existing `shows empty state when no trades`; the eight existing tests of the file passed the other fourteen.
- **Rows left unticked at V and why, and what the ticks do not cover.** JOURNAL-01: its picker shows vendor-live search results and its add writes `watchlists`, and the storage label reads the caller's own answer, which needs a session. JOURNAL-04, JOURNAL-05, JOURNAL-07 and JOURNAL-08: actions (`POST /api/style/mine-and-validate`, `POST /api/journal/trades`, `PATCH /api/journal/trades/{trade_id}`) that need a session and a browser. JOURNAL-09 and JOURNAL-10: actions (`POST` import and export), and the commit fails on the copy of the production table. JOURNAL-11: client-side re-scoping that needs a browser. JOURNAL-12, JOURNAL-14 and JOURNAL-15: client-side states that production does not show without a browser and an injected delay or failure, and the page has no age to show. JOURNAL-13: client-side; the Examples empty card is not reachable for IWM, SPY or QQQ and the own empty state needs a session ([V evidence](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1234#issuecomment-5941270196)). JOURNAL-02, JOURNAL-03 and JOURNAL-06 are ticked on the freshness of `journal_entries`, `trades`, `signal_alerts` and `market_data_intraday` (one read-only dispatch, and the schedulers and jobs that write them) and on the real handlers and the page answering on copies of those rows, and JOURNAL-16 on the 401 of the fifteen gated routes on staging (`solyra-api-prod` answers 302 from its IAP front door). No journal or market request was answered with a session, so the services' own reads, their latency and the page as a signed-in user sees it were not observed, and the 401 is staging's; no write route was exercised against production, so the `DELETE` in JOURNAL-06's Chain (like the rail's Exit and the form's save) was proven only by its 401 on staging and by a run on the copy. The page shows no age (JOURNAL-15): the ticks say that the page can show these rows and bars and that they were current to the dates read, not that it says so. The four ticks also carry display defects that V does not cover. The display findings above are the record of them, and the list here is not complete; among them are, for JOURNAL-02 and JOURNAL-03, the Examples returns that read a hundred times what is stored (the Return column of JOURNAL-06 reads them too), a chart that trails the Examples rows by up to a session and a session whose monitor execution failed that shows five rows with no marker; for JOURNAL-06, a delete with no confirmation and no failure message; and, for JOURNAL-16, a permission block that cannot show and the wrong cause (`Examples unavailable, the journal database didn't respond.`) named on a 401.

### Backend notes
- No scheduled pipeline feeds this page's own tables: `user_roles`, `model_routing` and `admin_refresh_leases` are written from it (ADMIN-04, ADMIN-07, ADMIN-06), and `model_routing` starts from seed rows, though its seven rows read on 2026-09-28 carry `updated_by` `claude-code/ab-test-restore-2026-05-12`, not the page's `admin-ui` (execution `db-query-jq7t2`). The Data tab reads the freshness audit over tables whose producers 05-c lists. No scheduled trigger writes the GCS objects behind the models tab: `gcp/research/strat_engine/strat_pred_train.py` writes the per-cell artifacts through a manual dispatch of `strat-engine`, and `gcp/research/strat_engine/strat_ece_snapshot.py` writes the live-ECE snapshot on demand. The predict's features come from `strat-engine` at 23:35 ET Mon-Fri and its levels tables at 02:00 ET Tue-Sat.
- Freshness the page assumes: users, sources and routes hold 30 s, models five minutes, and the brief and the state 60 s ([useAdmin.ts:47](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useAdmin.ts#L47), [useAdmin.ts:65](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useAdmin.ts#L65), [useAdmin.ts:122](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useAdmin.ts#L122), [useAdmin.ts:183](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useAdmin.ts#L183), [useAdmin.ts:281](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useAdmin.ts#L281), [useAdmin.ts:349](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useAdmin.ts#L349)), nothing polls, and each mutation refetches its list. The server caches the freshness audit for five minutes ([health.py:42](../../platform/api/routers/health.py#L42)).
- Access: the page decides from `/api/me`'s `is_admin` alone ([AdminPage.tsx:36-48](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/AdminPage.tsx#L36)), and every admin route checks again through `_require_admin`. On prod, where `AUTH_MODE` is `iap`, the identity is the IAP header, and a Firebase account's status does not govern access there (ADMIN-05).
- `POST /api/admin/strat-engine/structure-continuation` ([admin.py:735](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L735)) has no caller in solyra, and `STRUCTURE_CONTINUATION_ENABLED` is set on neither service (read 2026-09-28), so it answers 404.
- `GET /api/admin/routes` also feeds INSIGHTS-02, described the same way here.
- The refresh registry maps its nine refreshable datasets to seven jobs, and each job grants `roles/run.invoker` to `trading-platform-svc@` (read 2026-09-28). `admin_refresh_leases` held no row on 2026-09-28 (execution `db-query-ld8s6`), and no code deletes its rows, so no refresh has been accepted since the table was created.

### Gaps
- UI-SCREENS.md still cites [#838](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/838) for this page, but that issue was closed on 2026-09-14 after access became role-based (PRs #956 and #959), and the page takes no token ([AdminPage.tsx:27-33](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/AdminPage.tsx#L27)). The leftover `ADMIN_TOKEN` on `insight-pipeline` is tracked in [#850](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/850) (open) and recorded in the Insights Gaps (area 09).
- **Every Structure Brief cell is unavailable.** `research/strat_engine/structure_brief_latest.json` does not exist (listing read 2026-09-28), so all nine cells carry the deploy-gate note. A storage failure gives the same note, since `_load_structure_brief_snapshot` returns None on any error ([admin.py:262-283](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L262)): a hermetic probe with the storage client failing got 200 with nine unavailable cells.
- **A storage outage reads as an empty shelf.** `_strat_engine_state_cells` returns an empty list when the client fails and marks a cell unavailable on any read error ([admin.py:486-495](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L486), [admin.py:516-522](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L516)). A hermetic probe with the client failing got 200 `{"cells": [], "ece_ceiling": 0.05}`, and a render of that answer read `0 / 0 models trained · 0 muted · ECE ceiling 0.050 · no live ECE snapshot yet`. Nothing flags the served models' age: the nine `model.pkl` objects date from 2026-05-26 for IWM and 2026-06-04 for SPY and QQQ (listing read 2026-09-28).
- **The intraday refresh reason is wrong, and the page never shows it.** The registry says `market_data_intraday` has no on-demand refresh because its `only writer is the monthly bulk backfill` ([admin.py:1263-1264](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L1263)), while `gcp/deploy.sh` schedules `fetch-market-data` each weeknight and `fetch-alphavantage-intraday` every night from Monday to Saturday besides the monthly run (CHARTS-01). The page disables the button with a generic tooltip ([DataSourcesPanel.tsx:117-120](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/admin/DataSourcesPanel.tsx#L117)), so the reason reaches only an API caller: a hermetic probe got it with a 409.
- **The report's own `stale` flag is dropped.** The handler passes on `stale` and `stale_age_seconds` ([admin.py:1418-1425](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L1418)), and the page's response type holds only `sources` ([useAdmin.ts:340-342](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useAdmin.ts#L340)), so a cached report handed out during another audit shows as current, the unread-field shape CLAUDE.md §3.7.1 describes. `coverage_start` is always null ([admin.py:1384](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L1384)).
- **Disable cannot work on prod.** Prod runs `AUTH_MODE=iap` (read 2026-09-28), where the handler answers 409 ([admin.py:1158-1166](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L1158)), and the page shows Disable and Enable for every account ([UsersPanel.tsx:149-163](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/admin/UsersPanel.tsx#L149)) (filed as [solyra#78](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/issues/78)).
- **Granting a role to an account that holds one fails.** `toggleRole` sends the account's current roles plus the clicked one ([UsersPanel.tsx:45-48](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/admin/UsersPanel.tsx#L45)), and the server keeps one role per account and answers 422 for two ([admin.py:1083-1087](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L1083)): a hermetic probe sending `["user", "admin"]` got 422 `an account holds one role — send [], ["user"], or ["admin"]`, a message that also leaves out `dev`. The spec toggles a role-less account only ([admin-tabs.spec.ts:74-89](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/admin/admin-tabs.spec.ts#L74)), while [test_admin_users_datasources.py](../../tests/api/test_admin_users_datasources.py) pins the 422 (filed as [solyra#77](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/issues/77)).
- **A role-store outage reads as a missing role.** `/api/me` reports no stored role when the `user_roles` lookup fails ([auth.py:229-264](../../platform/api/auth.py#L229)), so an admin granted through the table gets `Admin access required`: a hermetic probe with the lookup failing answered 200 with `is_admin` false. The `ADMIN_EMAIL` account keeps access, the fallback the code documents.
- Class probabilities: the server pads the classes a model lacks with 0.0 ([strat_pred_serve.py:442-443](../../gcp/research/strat_engine/strat_pred_serve.py#L442)), and both displays turn a missing class into 0 with an unmarked `?? 0` ([StructureBrief.tsx:318](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/structure_brief/StructureBrief.tsx#L318), [PredictForm.tsx:183](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/structure_brief/PredictForm.tsx#L183)).
- The model list offers `gemini-2.0-flash` with credentials, which the schema's own comment says 404s against the adapter's default `global` location ([schema.sql:1527-1531](../../gcp/schema.sql#L1527)), and `set_route` checks only the price table and the adapter ([model_routing.py:164-206](../../lib/agents/model_routing.py#L164)). Not checked against Vertex (filed as [stocks#1221](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1221)).
- Sources that disagree with the code: UI-SCREENS.md gives the component 369 lines, where it has 344, lists only `/api/admin/routes` among its calls, where the rows trace twelve, and marks the empty and stale states absent, while the page renders an empty search, an empty category and unavailable cells (ADMIN-10) but no age (ADMIN-12). 04-BACKEND-API.md's admin inventory holds seven of the router's twelve routes, without users, roles, status, data sources and refresh, and describes `/api/admin/models` as loading the structure-brief snapshot, where it lists priced models. The routing panel's comment says the roles seed on `vertex:gemini-2.0-flash` ([AdminPage.tsx:157-168](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/AdminPage.tsx#L157)), and the schema seeds `gemini-3.1-flash-lite` (filed as [stocks#1221](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1221)).
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits in `platform/api/routers/admin.py`, `platform/api/auth.py` or the admin components, though `_load_structure_brief_snapshot` and `_strat_engine_state_cells` swallow storage errors (above). Every route in the rows declares a `response_model`.
- No test was found for ADMIN-09, and ADMIN-08's tests are backend only: no spec runs a predict.

### Backend notes
- No pipeline feeds this page. `user_preferences` and `user_profile` are written only from it, besides the header's theme toggle, which writes preferences through the same stores (SHELL-10), and the Role line's `user_roles` comes from the Admin page (ADMIN-04). A read-only query on 2026-09-28 found one `user_preferences` row and no `user_profile` rows (execution `db-query-ld8s6`).
- Freshness the page assumes: preferences and the profile hold five minutes with one retry ([usePreferences.ts:147-151](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/usePreferences.ts#L147), [useProfile.ts:96-101](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useProfile.ts#L96)) and `/api/me` 30 s ([useUser.ts:75](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useUser.ts#L75)). The stored appearance applies once per session.
- Two persistence models by design ([SettingsPage.tsx:1-13](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SettingsPage.tsx#L1)): appearance applies at once in `localStorage` (`platform-theme`, `platform-shell-settings`) and writes through, and the other tabs are a draft saved with `Save changes`, sending the changed fields only.
- `PUT /api/me/preferences` also carries SHELL-10's header toggle, and `GET /api/me` the shell's auth status (SHELL-04, SHELL-16), described the same way here.

### Gaps
- **UI-SCREENS.md describes an older page** (spec §14). It gives the purpose as device-local appearance preferences, finds no API calls, and counts 131 lines and no E2E spec, where the page syncs appearance through `/api/me/preferences` and saves a profile through `/api/me/profile` (SETTINGS-01 to SETTINGS-07), has 492 lines, and has [settings.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/settings/settings.spec.ts). It also cites [#685](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/685) for the page, but that issue was closed on 2026-09-03 as not planned, and it concerns the Heatseeker and Flowseeker names (moved to solyra [#27](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/issues/27), also closed), not this page.
- **Nothing reads the profile.** No solyra module besides `useProfile` and this page reads a `user_profile` field, and 05-c lists the router as the table's only reader. The copy promises uses that do not exist: a time zone `Used for session clocks and event times.`, defaults for the charts, options and signals pages, sizing that pre-fills `in the journal and playbook`, and email alerts to the sign-in address, with no sender anywhere.
- **Any failure, a defect included, is a 503.** Both routers catch every exception on read and write ([preferences.py:137-141](../../platform/api/routers/preferences.py#L137), [preferences.py:189-193](../../platform/api/routers/preferences.py#L189), [profile.py:150-154](../../platform/api/routers/profile.py#L150), [profile.py:201-205](../../platform/api/routers/profile.py#L201)), so a programming error reads as an outage: a hermetic probe raising a `TypeError` in the row read got 503 `preferences temporarily unavailable` and `profile temporarily unavailable`, where the admin router checks `is_infrastructure_error` first ([admin.py:147-153](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L147)).
- **The page cannot show staleness.** The stores take the stored appearance once per session ([usePreferences.ts:159-189](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/usePreferences.ts#L159)) and later reads do not re-apply it, and the header says `Synced to your account.` with no time ([SettingsPage.tsx:191-192](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SettingsPage.tsx#L191)).
- The spec mocks the nothing-stored reads as a 200 with nulls and an empty object ([settings.spec.ts:22-50](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/settings/settings.spec.ts#L22)), while both routers answer 404 ([preferences.py:142-145](../../platform/api/routers/preferences.py#L142), [profile.py:155-158](../../platform/api/routers/profile.py#L155)), which the hooks read as nothing stored ([usePreferences.ts:92-98](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/usePreferences.ts#L92), [useProfile.ts:76-81](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useProfile.ts#L76)).
- The design seed gives 11 accent swatches, where the page has 12 ([settingsStore.ts:20-33](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/stores/settingsStore.ts#L20)), reports zero E2E coverage, and has a journey where saved sizing pre-fills the journal and playbook, which nothing does (above).
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits in either router or on the page's path, and every route in the rows declares a `response_model`.
- SETTINGS-12 is covered only for the `Synced to your account.` line ([settings.spec.ts:61-80](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/settings/settings.spec.ts#L61)). No test was found for SETTINGS-08, SETTINGS-09, `Discard`, or SETTINGS-12's other states: the stored appearance applied once per session with the local values leading after that ([usePreferences.ts:159-189](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/usePreferences.ts#L159)) and the profile's five-minute `staleTime` ([useProfile.ts:96-101](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useProfile.ts#L96)). SETTINGS-03, SETTINGS-04 and SETTINGS-05 have backend tests only.

### Backend notes
- No pipeline feeds this page. Its one request, `GET /api/config/indicators`, answers from literals in the handler and `load_config()`, which reads `alert_config.json` from the working directory ([config.py:704-718](../../lib/config.py#L704)). The API image copies no such file ([Dockerfile:40-45](../../platform/Dockerfile#L40)), so the service answers with the `AppConfig` defaults (the running image was not inspected).
- Freshness the page assumes: the config holds 24 hours ([useConfig.ts:55-66](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useConfig.ts#L55)), and the entries are written into the page.
- `GET /api/glossary/gamma` ([glossary.py:30](../../platform/api/routers/glossary.py#L30)) serves `lib/gamma_glossary.py`'s public dictionary and has no caller in solyra.

### Gaps
- **A failed config read shows fallback thresholds without a word.** `buildGlossary` substitutes fallbacks while the config is missing ([HelpPage.tsx:17-25](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/HelpPage.tsx#L17)), and nothing reads the query's error, so a failed read renders StochRSI as `Below 20 = oversold. Above 80 = overbought.`, where the server says 30 and 70 ([config.py:435-436](../../lib/config.py#L435)): a render with the read failing showed that text and no error. The comment above calls the fallbacks the documented defaults from `lib/config.py` ([HelpPage.tsx:12-15](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/HelpPage.tsx#L12)).
- The shared mock payload, which says it mirrors the production config, sets StochRSI 20 and 80 and `min_conditions` 7 ([help.ts:48-49](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/mocks/help.ts#L48)), where the server's are 30, 70 and 3, and its fan-out lists a `<WatchlistPanel>` the page does not mount ([help.ts:8-10](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/mocks/help.ts#L8)). [help.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/help/help.spec.ts) renders against that mock.
- `/api/config/indicators` returns literals for part of its answer: RSI 30 and 70, the five RSI zones, `atr.high_threshold` 2.0 and `rvol.signal_threshold` 1.0 ([config.py:85-114](../../platform/api/routers/config.py#L85)), not values from `load_config`, so the glossary's `requires RVOL > 1.0` comes from the handler rather than the signal engine's gate, which reads `rvol_gate_min` and `rvol_gate_mode` (`gcp/signal_monitor.py:1428-1431`). The answer's `call_range` and `put_range`, which no page renders, come from `SignalConfig` defaults, 25 to 50 and 50 to 75 ([config.py:427-428](../../lib/config.py#L427)): the API image copies no `alert_config.json`, the jobs' image does (`gcp/Dockerfile:23`), and that file sets 45 to 70 and 30 to 55 (the running images were not inspected).
- **No TermHover exists.** The Options Gamma Map's `Term` spans show a `title` tooltip from the mock `glossary` in [gammaMapMock.ts:255](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/data/gammaMapMock.ts#L255) ([SwingMode.tsx:129-138](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L129)), not this page's entries or `GET /api/glossary/gamma`, and its `Glossary` button opens `/help` without a term or an anchor ([SwingMode.tsx:307-315](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L307)). UI-SCREENS.md credits TermHover to PR #546, the design seed's journey follows a hovered term into the glossary, and the glossary router's docstring still names `<TermHover>` and HelpPage as its consumers ([glossary.py:1-12](../../platform/api/routers/glossary.py#L1)).
- `GET /api/glossary/gamma` has no caller and no `response_model` ([glossary.py:30](../../platform/api/routers/glossary.py#L30)).
- **The glossary is written by hand.** The 131 entries in 11 categories are literals in the page ([HelpPage.tsx:16-190](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/HelpPage.tsx#L16)), six of whose details take config values, and the gamma entries are kept apart from `lib/gamma_glossary.py`, the dictionary the router serves.
- Sources that disagree with the code: UI-SCREENS.md gives the page 296 lines, where it has 295. The design seed says the page makes no API calls, where `useIndicatorConfig` makes one, names a `STRAT` pill, where the pill reads `The Strat`, and expands a `King level` entry, where the entry is `King Node (★)`.
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits in `platform/api/routers/config.py`, `platform/api/routers/glossary.py` or the page.
- No test was found for HELP-04 or HELP-07. [help.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/help/help.spec.ts) checks the heading, the input's presence and a load budget. [gamma-levels.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/shared/gamma-levels.spec.ts) covers HELP-02, HELP-05 and HELP-06 with the Gamma Levels and The Strat pills and one search term, `King Node`.
